	internal class DataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger : DataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation
	{
		private int _currentHeatExchangerIndex;

		public int HeatExchangerIndex
		{
			set
			{
				_currentHeatExchangerIndex = value;
			}
		}

		public DataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger(BackDoor backDoor, string commandPrefix, string hipProjectName, string scenarioName)
			: base(backDoor, commandPrefix, hipProjectName, scenarioName)
		{
		}

		protected override void Initialize(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum messageType, out string command, out IBackDoorMessage msg)
		{
			string designViewMoniker = base.DesignViewMoniker;
			switch (messageType)
			{
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_Area:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture12 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				command = string.Format(invariantCulture12, "{1}:Area.{0}.{2}", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), designViewMoniker, _currentHeatExchangerIndex);
				msg = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_NumberOfShells:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture11 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				command = string.Format(invariantCulture11, "{1}:ExtraData.{0}.{2}", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), designViewMoniker, _currentHeatExchangerIndex);
				msg = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_NewArea:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture10 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				command = string.Format(invariantCulture10, "{1}/MyOwnerObject:ExtraData.{0}.0", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), designViewMoniker);
				msg = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_ExtraShells:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture9 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				command = string.Format(invariantCulture9, "{1}/MyOwnerObject:ExtraData.{0}.0", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), designViewMoniker);
				msg = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_HotSideHXName:
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_ColdSideHXName:
				command = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{1}:Name.{0}.{2}", (int)messageType, base.DesignViewMoniker, _currentHeatExchangerIndex);
				msg = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Text, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_CapitalCost:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture8 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				command = string.Format(invariantCulture8, "{1}:Cost.{0}.{2}", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), designViewMoniker, _currentHeatExchangerIndex);
				msg = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;

			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_TotalCapitalCost:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture7 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				command = string.Format(invariantCulture7, "{1}:Cost.{0}", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), designViewMoniker);
				msg = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_ExtraCapitalCost:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture6 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				command = string.Format(invariantCulture6, "{1}:Cost.{0}.[]", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), designViewMoniker);
				msg = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);

				int num = (int)messageType;

			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_EnergySaving:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture4 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;

				break;

			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_Name:

			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddAndRelocate_RetrofitStatus:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture2 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;

				break;
			}
			default:
				command = string.Empty;
				msg = null;
				break;
			}
		}

	internal class DataExtractorForCurves : EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor
	{
		public bool isRefresh;

		internal DataExtractorForCurves(BackDoor backDoor, string commandPrefix, string hipProjectName, string scenarioName)
			: base(backDoor, commandPrefix, hipProjectName, scenarioName)
		{
		}

		public override object ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum messageType)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			IBackDoorMessage backDoorMessage = null;
			switch (messageType)
			{
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Composite:
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ":EXPORT_UNSHIFTED_COMPOSITE_CURVE.1");
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Text, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Grand_Composite:
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ":EXPORT_GRAND_COMPOSITE_CURVE.1");
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Text, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Utility_Composite:
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ":EXPORT_UTILITY_COMPOSITE_CURVE.1");
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Text, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Pinch_Points:
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ":EXPORT_PINCH_POINTS.1");
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Text, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			if (backDoorMessage == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return backDoorMessage.GetData(base.MonikerPrefix + text);
		}
	}

				msg = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			}
		}
	}
	internal abstract class DataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation : EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor
	{
		private int _designViewerIndexer = 1;

		private string _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix = "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.1";

		protected const string BaseDesignViewMoniker = "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.0";

		internal int DesignViewerIndexer
		{
			get
			{
				return _designViewerIndexer;
			}
			set
			{
				_designViewerIndexer = value;
				_simulateDesignMonikerPrefix = "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200." + _designViewerIndexer;
			}
		}

		protected string DesignViewMoniker => _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix;

		internal string ScenarioViewMoniker => base.MonikerPrefix;

		public DataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation(BackDoor backDoor, string commandPrefix, string hipProjectName, string scenarioName)
			: base(backDoor, commandPrefix, hipProjectName, scenarioName)
		{
		}

		public override object ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum messageType)
		{
			string commandMoniker;
			IBackDoorMessage message;
			switch (messageType)
			{
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HXName:
			{
				CultureInfo currentCulture6 = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(currentCulture6, "{1}:Name.{0}.[]", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.TextFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_MaxExtraArea:
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_RequiredArea:
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_NewArea:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture9 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(invariantCulture9, "{1}:Area.{0}.[]", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_ExistingArea:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture8 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(invariantCulture8, "{1}/MyOwnerObject:Area.{0}.[]", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_ExtraShells:
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddExchanger_IsNewHX:
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_LMTDCorrection:
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HeatExchangerTypes:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture7 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(invariantCulture7, "{1}:ExtraData.{0}.[]", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Xchanger_BaseCapitalCost:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture6 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(invariantCulture6, "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.0:Cost.{0}.[]", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3));
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Xchanger_CurrentCapitalCost:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture5 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(invariantCulture5, "{1}:Cost.{0}.[]", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_ExtraCapitalCost:
			{
				CultureInfo currentCulture5 = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(currentCulture5, "{1}:Cost.{0}.[]", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.XChanger_HXDoF:
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_Locked:
				commandMoniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{1}:Boolean.{0}.[]", (int)messageType, _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_ColdStreamName:
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HotStreamName:
				commandMoniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{1}:Name.{0}.[]", (int)messageType, _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.TextFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.HeatExchangerCost_Models:
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_DeltaMinimumColdTemperatures:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture4 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(invariantCulture4, "{1}:DeltaTemp.{0}.[]", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_BaseLoad:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture3 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(invariantCulture3, "{1}/MyOwnerObject:HeatFlow.{0}.[]", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_DesignLoad:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture2 = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(invariantCulture2, "{1}:HeatFlow.{0}.[]", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_PaybackPeriod:
				commandMoniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{1}:Time.{0}", (int)messageType, _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaHot:
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaCold:
				commandMoniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{1}:HeatFlow.{0}.0", (int)messageType, _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_UpdateOper:
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(invariantCulture, "{1}:Boolean.{0}.0", num.ToString().Substring(0, 3), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HeatFlows:
			{
				CultureInfo currentCulture4 = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(currentCulture4, "{1}/MyOwnerObject:ExtraData.{0}.[]", num.ToString(), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_MinTemperature:
			{
				CultureInfo currentCulture3 = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(currentCulture3, "{1}:DeltaTemp.{0}.[]", num.ToString(), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.RetrofitDesign_LogFile:
			{
				CultureInfo currentCulture2 = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(currentCulture2, "{1}:Name.{0}", num.ToString(), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Text, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Retrofit_CurrentHXIndex:
			{
				CultureInfo currentCulture = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				commandMoniker = string.Format(currentCulture, "{1}:ExtraData.{0}", num.ToString(), _simulateDesignMonikerPrefix);
				message = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			default:
				Initialize(messageType, out commandMoniker, out message);
				break;
			}
			if (message == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(commandMoniker))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return message.GetData(base.MonikerPrefix + commandMoniker);
		}

		protected abstract void Initialize(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum messageType, out string commandMoniker, out IBackDoorMessage message);
	}
	internal class DataExtractorForModifyHeatExchanger : DataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation
	{
		public DataExtractorForModifyHeatExchanger(BackDoor backDoor, string commandPrefix, string hipProjectName, string scenarioName)
			: base(backDoor, commandPrefix, hipProjectName, scenarioName)
		{
		}

		protected override void Initialize(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum messageType, out string command, out IBackDoorMessage msg)
		{
			if (messageType == BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.RetrofitStatus)
			{
				CultureInfo invariantCulture = CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
				int num = (int)messageType;
				command = string.Format(invariantCulture, "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.0:ExtraData.{0}", num.ToString());
				msg = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
			}
			else
			{
				command = null;
				msg = null;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class DataExtractorForProjectSetup : EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor
	{
		internal DataExtractorForProjectSetup(BackDoor backDoor, string commandPrefix, string hipProjectName, string scenarioName)
			: base(backDoor, commandPrefix, hipProjectName, scenarioName)
		{
		}

		public override object ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum messageType)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			IBackDoorMessage backDoorMessage = null;
			switch (messageType)
			{
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.MatchConstraints_HeatStream:
				text = ":SVMHotStName.[]";
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.TextFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.MatchConstraints_ColdStream:
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ":ColdStream.{0}.[]", (int)messageType);
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.TextFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.MatchConstraints_Config:
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ":Boolean.{0}.[].[]", (int)messageType);
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Streams_HotProcess:
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Streams_ColdProcess:
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ":Name.{0}.[]", (int)messageType);
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.TextFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Streams_Configs:
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ":Enum.{0}.[].[]", (int)messageType);
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			if (backDoorMessage == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return backDoorMessage.GetData(base.MonikerPrefix + text);
		}
	}
	internal class DataExtractorForSavingPotentials : EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor
	{
		private int _baseScenarioIndex = int.MinValue;

		public bool isRefresh;

		private static object _baseScenarioIndexSyncObject = new object();

		private string _baseDesignMoniker
		{
			get
			{
				if ((double)ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.BaseScenarioDesignNumber) == 2.0)
				{
					return string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.1", new Regex("\\d+\\(Scenario").Replace(base.MonikerPrefix, string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}(Scenario", BaseScenarioIndex)));
				}
				return string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.0", new Regex("\\d+\\(Scenario").Replace(base.MonikerPrefix, string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}(Scenario", BaseScenarioIndex)));
			}
		}

		private int BaseScenarioIndex
		{
			get
			{
				lock (_baseScenarioIndexSyncObject)
				{
					if (_baseScenarioIndex < 0)
					{
						string message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}:ExtraData.{1}", base.MonikerPrefix.Substring(0, base.MonikerPrefix.LastIndexOf('/')), 205);
						IBackDoorMessage backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
						if (backDoorMessage == null)
						{
							return 0;
						}
						object data = backDoorMessage.GetData(message);
						if (data == null || !int.TryParse(data.ToString(), out var result))
						{
							return 0;
						}
						_baseScenarioIndex = result;
					}
				}
				return _baseScenarioIndex;
			}
		}

		internal DataExtractorForSavingPotentials(BackDoor backDoor, string commandPrefix, string hipProjectName, string scenarioName)
			: base(backDoor, commandPrefix, hipProjectName, scenarioName)

		public void SelectBaseDesign()
		{
			string moniker = "Viewable.500.(FeatureManager)/Viewable.500.0:Viewable.299";
			SendAttachObject(moniker, _baseDesignMoniker);
		}

		public void SetRefreshStatus()
		{
			double num = (double)ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.BaseScenarioDesignNumber);
			if (isRefresh && num == 2.0)
			{
				isRefresh = false;
			}
		}

	internal class DataExtractorForSummary : EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor
	{
		private string _projectLevelMoniker;

		public string ViewScenarioMoniker => base.MonikerPrefix;

		public string ProjectMoniker
		{
			get
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_projectLevelMoniker))
				{
					_projectLevelMoniker = base.MonikerPrefix.Substring(0, base.MonikerPrefix.LastIndexOf('/'));
				}
				return _projectLevelMoniker;
			}
		}

		internal DataExtractorForSummary(BackDoor backDoor, string commandPrefix, string hipProjectName, string scenarioName)
			: base(backDoor, commandPrefix, hipProjectName, scenarioName)
		{
		}

		public override object ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum messageType)
		{
			string text = null;
			IBackDoorMessage backDoorMessage = null;
			switch (messageType)
			{
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.CheckRetrofitReady:
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ":ExtraData.{0}", (int)messageType);
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Summary_ScenarioName:
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{1}:ExtraData.{0}", (int)messageType, ProjectMoniker);
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Text, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Summary_ScenarioIndex:
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{1}:ExtraData.{0}", (int)messageType, ProjectMoniker);
				backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, base.BackDoor);
				break;
			}
			if (backDoorMessage == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return backDoorMessage.GetData((text.IndexOf(ProjectMoniker) >= 0) ? text : (base.MonikerPrefix + text));
		}
	}
	internal abstract class WorkflowDataProcess
	{
		private IUnityContainer _container;

		private EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor _dataExtraction;

		private ValueUnitEnum _valueUnit = ValueUnitEnum.MW;

		private IValueUnit _convert;

		protected IUnityContainer Container => _container;

		protected EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor DataExtractor => _dataExtraction;

		protected string ColdStreamTooltip => UIResources.HeatExchangerTypeToolTip_ColdStream;

		protected string HotStreamTooltip => UIResources.HeatExchangerTypeToolTip_HotStream;

		protected string ColdUtilityTooltip => UIResources.HeatExchangerTypeToolTip_ColdUtility;

		protected string HotUtilityTooltip => UIResources.HeatExchangerTypeToolTip_HotUtility;

		protected IDataResolver DataResolver => UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IDataResolver>(Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());

		public ValueUnitEnum ValueUnit
		{
			get
			{
				return _valueUnit;
			}
			set
			{
				_valueUnit = value;
				_convert = UnitValueFactory.Create(value) ?? new MWValueUnit();
			}
		}

		public IValueUnit ValueUnitConvert
		{
			get
			{
				if (_convert == null)
				{
					_convert = UnitValueFactory.Create(_valueUnit) ?? new MWValueUnit();
				}
				return _convert;
			}
		}

		public WorkflowDataProcess(IUnityContainer container, EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor dataExtractor)
		{
			_container = container;
			_dataExtraction = dataExtractor;
		}

		public abstract void WorkflowProcess(object state);

		protected string SerializeToString(object source)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			try
			{
				using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
				new BinaryFormatter().Serialize(memoryStream, source);
				memoryStream.Seek(0L, SeekOrigin.Begin);
				result = Convert.ToBase64String(memoryStream.ToArray());
				memoryStream.Close();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected object DeserializeFromString(string source)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(source))
			{
				return null;
			}
			object obj = null;
			using MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream(Convert.FromBase64String(source));
			obj = new BinaryFormatter().Deserialize(memoryStream);
			memoryStream.Close();
			return obj;
		}

		public virtual void Save(object state)
		{
		}

		public virtual void Restore(object state)
		{
		}

		protected string[] GetAreaItemParam(double hxType, string hotItem, string coldItem)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			switch ((int)hxType)
			{
			case 1:
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hotItem))
				{
					list.Add(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}{1}", HotUtilityTooltip, hotItem));
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(coldItem))
				{
					list.Add(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}{1}", ColdStreamTooltip, coldItem));
				}
				break;
			case 2:
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hotItem))
				{
					list.Add(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}{1}", HotStreamTooltip, hotItem));
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(coldItem))
				{
					list.Add(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}{1}", ColdUtilityTooltip, coldItem));
				}
				break;
			case 3:
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hotItem))
				{
					list.Add(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}{1}", HotStreamTooltip, hotItem));
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(coldItem))
				{
					list.Add(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}{1}", ColdStreamTooltip, coldItem));
				}
				break;
			}
			return Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}
	}
	internal class DataProcessForAddHeatExchanger : DataProcessForHeatExchangerBaseOperation
	{
		private string NewHeatExchangerName;

		private static int _designIndexStartAt = -1;

		private static int _ownedDesignIndex = -1;

		private bool _isUpdateOperation;

		private static Dictionary<string, IList<int>> _currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign = new Dictionary<string, IList<int>>();

		private int CurrentDesignIndexStartAt
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 1;
				Dictionary<string, IList<int>>.ValueCollection values = _currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Values;
				for (int i = 0; i < base.RetrofitDesignIndex - 1; i++)
				{
					num += values.ElementAt(i).Count;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		protected override int CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					_ownedDesignIndex = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<int>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_DesignIndex, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey));
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					_ownedDesignIndex = -1;
				}
				return _ownedDesignIndex;
			}
		}

		private string _currentOwnedRetrofitKey => FormatOwnedRetrofitKey(ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, base.RetrofitDesignIndex.ToString());

		internal DataProcessForAddHeatExchanger(IUnityContainer container, EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor dataExtractor)
			: base(container, dataExtractor)
		{
		}

		protected override void SendSepcialRetrofit()
		{
			base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.{0}:AddNewHX", (CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex < 0) ? base.RetrofitDesignIndex : CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex));
		}

		protected override SummaryUtilityInformationView RegisterUtility(double hotUtility, double coldUtility, IValueUnit valueUnit)
		{
			if (_isUpdateOperation)
			{
				DataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger = base.DataExtractor as DataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger;
				string value = new Regex("[^/]+/[^/]+").Match(dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value;
				string text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{1}", dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker.Substring(0, dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker.LastIndexOf('/')), new Regex("\\d+\\(Scenario.+\\)+?").Match(dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value);
				base.DataExtractor.SendAttachObject(value + ":Viewable.299", text + "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200." + CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex);
			}
			IEnumerable<UtilityInformation> enumerable = null;
			try
			{
				enumerable = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<IEnumerable<UtilityInformation>>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Utility, ProjectKey));
			}
			catch (ResolutionFailedException)
			{
				return new SummaryUtilityInformationView();
			}
			IEnumerable<UtilityInformation> source = from item in enumerable
				where item.UtilityType == UtilityType.COOLING || item.UtilityType == UtilityType.HEATING
				orderby item.OriginalOrder
				select item;
			double[] heatFlows = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HeatFlows) as double[];
			double num = CalculateGHGEmission(source.Select((UtilityInformation item) => item.GHGEmissionCoefficient), heatFlows, valueUnit);
			double num2 = 3600.0 * CalculateCostSaving(source.Select((UtilityInformation item) => item.CostIndices), heatFlows, valueUnit);
			UtilityInformation utilityInformation = enumerable.FirstOrDefault((UtilityInformation item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
			UtilityInformation utilityInformation2 = enumerable.LastOrDefault((UtilityInformation item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
			double num3 = hotUtility;
			double num4 = coldUtility;
			double num5 = source.Sum((UtilityInformation item) => item.GHGCurrent);
			double num6 = 3600.0 * source.Sum((UtilityInformation item) => item.CostIndices * item.OriginalCurrentValue);
			return new SummaryUtilityInformationView
			{
				Name = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, UIResources.AddChange, base.RetrofitDesignIndex, NewHeatExchangerName),
				NameData = NewHeatExchangerName,
				Hot = num3.ToString(),
				Cold = num4.ToString(),
				Reduction = ((0.0 - num3 - num4 + utilityInformation.Current + utilityInformation2.Current) / (utilityInformation.Current + utilityInformation2.Current) * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				GHGFlow = num.ToString(),
				GHGReduction = ((num5 - num) / ((num5 == 0.0) ? 1.0 : num5) * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				Cost = num2.ToString(),
				CostPercentage = ((num6 - num2) / num6 * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				BelongTo = UtilityBelongTo.AddHeatExchanger,
				DesignIndex = (_isUpdateOperation ? CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex : base.DesignViewerIndex),
				ValueUnitConverter = UnitValueFactory.Create(base.ValueUnit),
				RetrofitIndex = base.RetrofitDesignIndex
			};
		}

		protected override dynamic SearchSolution()
		{
			return base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddAndRelocate_RetrofitStatus);
		}

		protected override bool Process(object state)
		{
			if (!(base.DataExtractor is DataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation))
			{
				return false;
			}
			dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.DesignViewerIndexer = 0;
			double baseDesignCold;
			double baseDesignHeat = (baseDesignCold = 0.0);
			int baseDesignCount = 0;
			Task.WaitAll(Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HXName)).ContinueWith(delegate(Task<object> nameTsk)
			{
				string[] array = nameTsk.Result as string[];
				baseDesignCount = ((array != null) ? array.Length : 0);
			}), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaCold)).ContinueWith((Task<object> coldTsk) => baseDesignCold = ConvertToDouble(coldTsk.Result)), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaHot)).ContinueWith((Task<object> hotTsk) => baseDesignHeat = ConvertToDouble(hotTsk.Result)));
			_isUpdateOperation = state != null;
			if (!_isUpdateOperation)
			{
				return ProceedAddHeatExchanger(baseDesignHeat, baseDesignCold, baseDesignCount);
			}
			int retrofitIndex = ((state is int) ? ((int)state) : (-1));
			string value = new Regex("[^/]+/[^/]+").Match(dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value;
			string text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{1}", dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.ScenarioViewMoniker.Substring(0, dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.ScenarioViewMoniker.LastIndexOf('/')), new Regex("\\d+\\(Scenario.+\\)+?").Match(dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value);
			base.DataExtractor.SendAttachObject(value + ":Viewable.299", text + "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200." + CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex);
			return UpdateSpecifiedDesignHeatExchangers(CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex, baseDesignHeat, baseDesignCold, retrofitIndex);
		}

		private bool ProceedAddHeatExchanger(double baseDesignHeat, double baseDesignCold, int baseDesignHXCount)
		{
			if (!(base.DataExtractor is DataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger))
			{
				return false;
			}
			IList<double> list = base.RetrofitStatus;
			if (list == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			NewHeatExchangerViewCollection newHeatExchangerViewCollection = new NewHeatExchangerViewCollection();
			_ownedDesignIndex = (_designIndexStartAt = base.DesignViewerIndex);
			Collection<int> value = new Collection<int>();
			if (!_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.ContainsKey(_currentOwnedRetrofitKey))
			{
				_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Add(_currentOwnedRetrofitKey, value);
			}
			else
			{
				_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign[_currentOwnedRetrofitKey] = value;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			Collection<KeyValuePair<string, object>> collection = new Collection<KeyValuePair<string, object>>();
			int i = base.DesignViewerIndex;
			int num = i;
			for (; i < _designIndexStartAt + list.Count; i++)
			{
				if (list.ElementAt(i - _designIndexStartAt) != 7.0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				base.DesignViewerIndex = num;
				num++;
				dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.DesignViewerIndexer = base.DesignViewerIndex;
				if (base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HXName) is string[] array && array.Length > baseDesignHXCount)
				{
					int num2 = FindNewHeatExchangerIndex(array);
					if (num2 >= 0)
					{
						string value2 = new Regex("[^/]+/[^/]+").Match(dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value;
						string text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{1}", dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker.Substring(0, dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker.LastIndexOf('/')), new Regex("\\d+\\(Scenario.+\\)+?").Match(dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value);
						base.DataExtractor.SendAttachObject(value2 + ":Viewable.299", text + "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200." + base.DesignViewerIndex);
						SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection = ProceedHeatExchangers(num2, UIResources.New) ?? new SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection();
						string text2 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_AddHeatExchanger_Item, "Project 1", base.ScenarioKey, null, base.DesignViewerIndex.ToString());
						stringBuilder.AppendFormat(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ";{0}={1}", text2, SerializeToString(surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection));
						collection.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, object>(text2, surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection));
						dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.HeatExchangerIndex = num2;
						newHeatExchangerViewCollection.Add(ProceedSignNewHeatExchanger(base.DesignViewerIndex, num2, baseDesignHeat, baseDesignCold));
						ProceedNewSummaryUtility(base.OriginalParameters);
						_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign[_currentOwnedRetrofitKey].Add(base.DesignViewerIndex);
					}
				}
			}
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeEvent>().Publish(new UITreeNodeArgs
			{
				State = new object[3]
				{
					FormCategory.AddNewE400,
					base.ScenarioKey,
					base.RetrofitDesignIndex.ToString()
				},
				NodeText = NewHeatExchangerName
			});
			string text3 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_AddHeatExchanger, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, base.RetrofitDesignIndex.ToString());
			string moniker = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_RetrofitIndex, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey);
			base.DataResolver.Write(moniker, base.RetrofitDesignIndex);
			stringBuilder.Insert(0, text3);
			base.DataResolver.Write(stringBuilder.ToString(), newHeatExchangerViewCollection);
			base.DataResolver.Write(text3, newHeatExchangerViewCollection, collection.ToArray());
			return newHeatExchangerViewCollection.Count > 0;
		}

		private int FindNewHeatExchangerIndex(IList<string> hxNames)
		{
			if (hxNames == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			int result = -1;
			string strB = (string)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_Name);
			for (int i = 0; i < hxNames.Count; i++)
			{
				if (string.Compare(hxNames[i], strB, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					result = i;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool UpdateSpecifiedDesignHeatExchangers(int designIndex, double baseDesignHeat, double baseDesignCold, int retrofitIndex)
		{
			if (!(base.DataExtractor is DataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger))
			{
				return false;
			}
			string text = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_AddHeatExchanger, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, retrofitIndex.ToString());
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = base.DataResolver.ResolveData(text);
			if (dictionary == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			string key = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_AddHeatExchanger_Item, "Project 1", base.ScenarioKey, null, designIndex.ToString());
			if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!(dictionary[text] is NewHeatExchangerViewCollection { Count: not 0 } newHeatExchangerViewCollection))
			{
				return false;
			}
			dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.DesignViewerIndexer = designIndex;
			string[] hxNames = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HXName) as string[];
			int num = (dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.HeatExchangerIndex = FindNewHeatExchangerIndex(hxNames));
			NewHeatExchangerView newHeatExchangerView = ProceedSignNewHeatExchanger(designIndex, num, baseDesignHeat, baseDesignCold);
			if (!MMViewModelContainer.bIsBackground)
			{
				newHeatExchangerView.IsSelected = true;
			}
			int index = designIndex - CurrentDesignIndexStartAt;
			newHeatExchangerViewCollection.RemoveAt(index);
			newHeatExchangerViewCollection.Insert(index, newHeatExchangerView);
			dictionary[key] = ProceedHeatExchangers(num, UIResources.New) ?? new SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(text);
			Collection<KeyValuePair<string, object>> collection = new Collection<KeyValuePair<string, object>>();
			foreach (string key2 in dictionary.Keys)
			{
				if (string.Compare(key2, "ID") != 0 && string.Compare(key2, text) != 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.AppendFormat(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ";{0}={1}", key2, (dictionary[key2] is string) ? dictionary[key2] : SerializeToString(dictionary[key2]));
					collection.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, object>(key2, dictionary[key2]));
				}
			}
			base.DataResolver.Write(stringBuilder.ToString(), newHeatExchangerViewCollection);
			base.DataResolver.Write(text, newHeatExchangerViewCollection, collection.ToArray());
			return true;
		}

		private double ConvertToDouble(object parseValue)
		{
			return Convert.ToDouble(parseValue, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
		}

		private NewHeatExchangerView ProceedSignNewHeatExchanger(int designIndex, int heatExchangerIndex, double baseDesignHeat, double baseDesignCold)
		{
			double totalCosts;
			double paybacks;
			double energySavingPerTime;
			double totalCold;
			double totalHeat;
			double num = (totalCosts = (paybacks = (energySavingPerTime = (totalCold = (totalHeat = 0.0)))));
			double[] extraCost = null;
			string coldSideName;
			string text;
			string text2;
			string hotSideName = (coldSideName = (text = (text2 = null)));
			Task.WaitAll(Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HXName)).ContinueWith(delegate(Task<object> nameTsk)
			{
				if (nameTsk.Result is string[] array && array.Length != 0)
				{
					NewHeatExchangerName = array[heatExchangerIndex];
				}
			}), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_TotalCapitalCost)).ContinueWith((Task<object> costTsk) => totalCosts = ConvertToDouble(costTsk.Result)), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_Payback)).ContinueWith((Task<object> payBackTsk) => paybacks = ConvertToDouble(payBackTsk.Result)), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_ExtraCapitalCost)).ContinueWith((Task<object> extraTsk) => extraCost = extraTsk.Result as double[]), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_EnergySaving)).ContinueWith((Task<object> savingTsk) => energySavingPerTime = ConvertToDouble(savingTsk.Result)), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaCold)).ContinueWith((Task<object> coldTsk) => totalCold = ConvertToDouble(coldTsk.Result)), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaHot)).ContinueWith((Task<object> hotTsk) => totalHeat = ConvertToDouble(hotTsk.Result)), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_HotSideHXName)).ContinueWith((Task<object> hotSideTsk) => hotSideName = hotSideTsk.Result as string).ContinueWith(delegate(Task<string> hotSideHXNameTsk)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hotSideHXNameTsk.Result) && base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HotStreamName) is string[] array && array.Length > heatExchangerIndex)
				{
					hotSideName = array[heatExchangerIndex];
				}
			}), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_ColdSideHXName)).ContinueWith((Task<object> coldSideTsk) => coldSideName = coldSideTsk.Result as string).ContinueWith(delegate(Task<string> coldSideHXNameTsk)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(coldSideHXNameTsk.Result) && base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_ColdStreamName) is string[] array && array.Length > heatExchangerIndex)
				{
					coldSideName = array[heatExchangerIndex];
				}
			}));
			double area = (double)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_NewArea);
			num = (double)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_ExtraShells);
			if (!hotSideName.Contains("_DummyClrIgnore"))
			{
				text = "Upstream to " + hotSideName;
			}
			else
			{
				string text3 = hotSideName.Replace("_DummyClrIgnore", " ");
				text = "Downstream to " + text3;
			}
			if (!coldSideName.Contains("_DummyHtrIgnore"))
			{
				text2 = "Upstream to " + coldSideName;
			}
			else
			{
				string text4 = coldSideName.Replace("_DummyHtrIgnore", " ");
				text2 = "Downstream to " + text4;
			}
			double num2 = -1.0;
			try
			{
				num2 = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).OperationHoursPerYear;
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return new NewHeatExchangerView(new HeatExchanger
			{
				Area = area,
				NumberOfShell = num,
				TotalCapitalCost = totalCosts,
				Payback = paybacks / 31536000.0,
				ValueConverter = UnitValueFactory.Create(base.ValueUnit),
				EnergySavingPerTime = energySavingPerTime * 3600.0 * num2,
				EnergySaving = baseDesignCold + baseDesignHeat - totalHeat - totalCold,
				EnergySavingPercentForSI = (baseDesignCold + baseDesignHeat - totalHeat - totalCold) / (baseDesignCold + baseDesignHeat) * 100.0,
				HotSideName = text,
				ColdSideName = text2
			})
			{
				DesignIndex = designIndex
			};
		}

		protected override dynamic ExtractCurrentRetrofitOwnedDesigns(string retrofitIndex)
		{
			string text = FormatOwnedRetrofitKey(ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, retrofitIndex);
			if (!_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Keys.Contains(text))
			{
				_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Add(text, new Collection<int>());
			}
			return _currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign[text];
		}

		private string FormatOwnedRetrofitKey(string projectKey, string scenarioKey, string retrofitIndex)
		{
			return string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}{1}{2}{3}{4}", scenarioKey, "RetrofitIndex", "DesignIndex", retrofitIndex, projectKey);
		}

		protected override void DeleteDesign(string retrofitIndex)
		{
			_ownedDesignIndex = -1;
			string text = FormatOwnedRetrofitKey(ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, retrofitIndex);
			if (!_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Keys.Contains(text))
			{
				return;
			}
			_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Remove(text);
			string text2 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_AddHeatExchanger, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, retrofitIndex);
			base.DataResolver.Teardown(text2);
			base.DataResolver.ClearProjectKeys(text2);
			string moniker = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_DesignIndex, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey);
			base.DataResolver.Write(moniker, 0);
			int num = Convert.ToInt32(retrofitIndex);
			if (num > 1)
			{
				IEnumerable<int> source = ExtractCurrentRetrofitOwnedDesigns((num - 1).ToString()) as IEnumerable<int>;
				if (source.Count() > 0)
				{
					_designIndexStartAt -= source.Count();
				}
			}
			int result = -1;
			if (int.TryParse(retrofitIndex, out result))
			{
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<WorkflowControlStateEvent>().Publish(new WorkflowControlStateArgs
				{
					ControlType = WorkflowControl.ClearCachedData,
					State = new object[3]
					{
						base.ScenarioKey,
						FormCategory.AddNewE400,
						result
					}
				});
			}
		}

		public override void Restore(object state)
		{
			if (!(state is object[] array) || array.Length < 3 || string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[0] as string) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[1] as string) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[2].ToString()))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_AddHeatExchanger, array[2].ToString(), array[0].ToString(), array[1].ToString());
			if (!(base.DataResolver.ResolveData(text) is Dictionary<string, object> dictionary))
			{
				return;
			}
			Collection<int> collection = new Collection<int>();
			Regex regex = new Regex(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "({0}(\\d+))$", UtilityBelongTo.AddHeatExchanger));
			new Collection<KeyValuePair<string, object>>();
			foreach (string key2 in dictionary.Keys)
			{
				if (string.Compare(key2, "ID", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(text, key2, ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare("ProjectID", key2) == 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (dictionary[key2] is SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection)
				{
					foreach (SurfaceAreanEntityView item in surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection)
					{
						item.RegesterEvent(base.OnMaxExtraAreaChange, base.OnMinimumApproachTemperatureChange);
					}
				}
				collection.Add(int.Parse(regex.Match(key2).Groups[2].Value));
			}
			string key = FormatOwnedRetrofitKey(array[2].ToString(), array[0].ToString(), array[1].ToString());
			if (!_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Add(key, collection);
				base.DesignViewerIndex += collection.Count;
			}
			else
			{
				_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign[key] = collection;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class DataProcessForCurves : WorkflowDataProcess
	{
		private static Dictionary<CurveDataType, object> CurveDataDict = new Dictionary<CurveDataType, object>();

		private string _uiTreeNodekey = string.Empty;

		private string _projectKey = "Project 1";

		private void AddPinchList()
		{
			PinchList pinchList = new PinchList();
			pinchList = GetPinchList();
			CurveDataDict.Add(CurveDataType.PINCHLIST, new PinchListViewModel(pinchList, UpdatePinchList));
		}

		private void UpdatePinchList()
		{
			if (CurveDataDict.TryGetValue(CurveDataType.PINCHLIST, out var value))
			{
				PinchListViewModel pinchListViewModel = value as PinchListViewModel;
				PinchList pinchList = GetPinchList();
				if (pinchList != null)
				{
					pinchListViewModel.PinchList = pinchList;
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddUtilityCompositeCurve()
		{
			CompositeChart compositeChart = new CompositeChart();
			compositeChart = GetCompositeChart(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Utility_Composite);
			CurveDataDict.Add(CurveDataType.UTILCOMPCUR, new CompositeChartViewModel(compositeChart, UpdateUtilityCompositeCurve));
		}

		private void UpdateUtilityCompositeCurve()
		{
			if (CurveDataDict.TryGetValue(CurveDataType.UTILCOMPCUR, out var value))
			{
				CompositeChartViewModel compositeChartViewModel = value as CompositeChartViewModel;
				CompositeChart compositeChart = GetCompositeChart(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Utility_Composite);
				if (compositeChart != null)
				{
					compositeChartViewModel.Chart = compositeChart;
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddGrandCompositeCurve()
		{
			CompositeChart compositeChart = new CompositeChart();
			compositeChart = GetCompositeChart(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Grand_Composite);
			CurveDataDict.Add(CurveDataType.GRANDCOMPCURV, new CompositeChartViewModel(compositeChart, UpdateGrandCompositeCurve));
		}

		private void UpdateGrandCompositeCurve()
		{
			if (CurveDataDict.TryGetValue(CurveDataType.GRANDCOMPCURV, out var value))
			{
				CompositeChartViewModel compositeChartViewModel = value as CompositeChartViewModel;
				CompositeChart compositeChart = GetCompositeChart(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Grand_Composite);
				if (compositeChart != null)
				{
					compositeChartViewModel.Chart = compositeChart;
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddCompositeCurve()
		{
			CompositeChart compositeChart = new CompositeChart();
			compositeChart = GetCompositeChart(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Composite);
			CurveDataDict.Add(CurveDataType.COMPCURV, new CompositeChartViewModel(compositeChart, UpdateCompositeCurve));
		}

		private void UpdateCompositeCurve()
		{
			if (CurveDataDict.TryGetValue(CurveDataType.COMPCURV, out var value))
			{
				CompositeChartViewModel compositeChartViewModel = value as CompositeChartViewModel;
				CompositeChart compositeChart = GetCompositeChart(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Composite);
				if (compositeChart != null)
				{
					compositeChartViewModel.Chart = compositeChart;
				}
			}
		}

		private CompositeChart GetCompositeChart(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum curveType)
		{
			CompositeChart result = null;
			if (curveType == BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Composite || curveType == BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Grand_Composite || curveType == BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Utility_Composite)
			{
				XmlSerializer xmlSerializer = new XmlSerializer(typeof(CompositeChart));
				string text = (string)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(curveType);
				if (text != null && text.Contains("<chart>"))
				{
					using StringReader textReader = new StringReader(text);
					result = (CompositeChart)xmlSerializer.Deserialize(textReader);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private PinchList GetPinchList()
		{
			PinchList result = null;
			XmlSerializer xmlSerializer = new XmlSerializer(typeof(PinchList));
			string text = (string)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Curves_Pinch_Points);
			if (text != null && text.Contains("<pinchlist>"))
			{
				using StringReader textReader = new StringReader(text);
				result = (PinchList)xmlSerializer.Deserialize(textReader);
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal DataProcessForCurves(IUnityContainer container, EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor extrator)
			: base(container, extrator)
		{
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeSelectionEvent>().Subscribe(OnNewNodeKey);
		}

		private void OnNewNodeKey(UITreeNodeSelectionArgs e)
		{
			object[] array = e.State as object[];
			if (_uiTreeNodekey == e.NodeKey && array != null && array.Length > 2 && array[0] is bool && (bool)array[0] && array[1] is OperationEnum && (OperationEnum)array[1] == OperationEnum.Delete)
			{
				_uiTreeNodekey = string.Empty;
			}
			else
			{
				_uiTreeNodekey = e.NodeKey;
			}
		}

		public override void WorkflowProcess(object state)
		{
			if (state == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			OperationEnum operationEnum = OperationEnum.UnKnow;
			if (state is object[] array)
			{
				operationEnum = ((array.Length != 0 && array[0] is OperationEnum) ? ((OperationEnum)array[0]) : OperationEnum.CreateDefault);
				string state2 = ((array.Length == 3 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[2] as string)) ? array[2].ToString().Split('.')[0] : "Project 1");
				switch (operationEnum)
				{
				case OperationEnum.CreateDefault:
					Create(state2);
					break;
				case OperationEnum.Save:
					Save(state2);
					break;
				case OperationEnum.Restore:
					Restore(state2);
					break;
				case OperationEnum.Add:
				case OperationEnum.Delete:
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void CreateCurves()
		{
			if (CurveDataDict.Count > 0)
			{
				CurveDataDict.Clear();
			}
			AddCompositeCurve();
			AddGrandCompositeCurve();
			AddUtilityCompositeCurve();
			AddPinchList();
			base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EngineData_Curves, _projectKey), CurveDataDict);
		}

		private void Create(object state)
		{
			try
			{
				_projectKey = state.ToString();
				CreateCurves();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		public override void Save(object state)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(state as string))
			{
				_projectKey = state.ToString();
				base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EngineData_Curves, _projectKey), CurveDataDict);
			}
		}

		public override void Restore(object state)
		{
			_projectKey = state.ToString();
			CurveDataDict = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<Dictionary<CurveDataType, object>>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EngineData_Curves, _projectKey));
			if (CurveDataDict.TryGetValue(CurveDataType.COMPCURV, out var value))
			{
				(value as CompositeChartViewModel)._updateContent = UpdateCompositeCurve;
			}
			if (CurveDataDict.TryGetValue(CurveDataType.GRANDCOMPCURV, out var value2))
			{
				(value2 as CompositeChartViewModel)._updateContent = UpdateGrandCompositeCurve;
			}
			if (CurveDataDict.TryGetValue(CurveDataType.UTILCOMPCUR, out var value3))
			{
				(value3 as CompositeChartViewModel)._updateContent = UpdateUtilityCompositeCurve;
			}
			if (CurveDataDict.TryGetValue(CurveDataType.PINCHLIST, out var value4))
			{
				(value4 as PinchListViewModel)._updateContent = UpdatePinchList;
			}
		}
	}

		internal DataProcessForHeatExchangerBaseOperation(IUnityContainer container, EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor extrator)
			: base(container, extrator)
		{
			DataSourceChangedEvent dataSourceChangedEvent = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>();
			DataSourceTypeEnum dataSourceType = DataSourceType;
			if (_updateDesignToken.ContainsKey(dataSourceType))
			{
				dataSourceChangedEvent.Unsubscribe(_updateDesignToken[dataSourceType]);
				_updateDesignToken.Remove(dataSourceType);
			}
			SubscriptionToken value = dataSourceChangedEvent.Subscribe(delegate(DataSourceChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				UpdateDesignViewer(e.State);
			}, ThreadOption.BackgroundThread, keepSubscriberReferenceAlive: true, (DataSourceChangedEventArgs arg) => arg.SourceType == DataSourceType && arg.State != null);
			_updateDesignToken.Add(dataSourceType, value);
		}

		public override void WorkflowProcess(object state)
		{
			OriginalParameters = state;
			if (!(state is object[] array) || array.Length < 2 || !Enum.TryParse<OperationEnum>(array[1].ToString(), out var result))
			{
				return;
			}
			ProjectKey = ((array.Length >= 6 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[5] as string)) ? array[5].ToString() : "Project 1");
			switch (result)
			{
			case OperationEnum.Delete:
				if (array.Length < 3 || array[2] == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				lock (_syncObject)
				{
					bool ignoreDeleteInHXNet = array.Length >= 4 && array[3] is bool && (bool)array[3];
					EventWaitHandle waitHandle = ((array.Length >= 5) ? (array[4] as EventWaitHandle) : null);
					DoDeleteOperation(array[2].ToString(), ignoreDeleteInHXNet, waitHandle);
					break;
				}
			case OperationEnum.Save:
				if (array.Length >= 3 && array[2] != null)
				{
					Save(new object[3]
					{
						ScenarioKey,
						array[2].ToString(),
						ProjectKey
					});
				}
				break;
			case OperationEnum.Restore:
				if (array.Length >= 3 && array[2] != null)
				{
					Restore(new object[3]
					{
						ScenarioKey,
						array[2].ToString(),
						ProjectKey
					});
					int num = Convert.ToInt32(array[2] as string);
					RetrofitDesignIndex = ((num >= 0) ? num : 0);
				}
				break;
			default:
				lock (_syncObject)
				{
					DoAddOperation(state);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void DoDeleteOperation(string retrofitIndex, bool ignoreDeleteInHXNet = false, EventWaitHandle waitHandle = null)
		{
			dynamic val = ExtractCurrentRetrofitOwnedDesigns(retrofitIndex);
			if (!((val == null) ? true : false))
			{
				SummaryUtilities summaryUtilities = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<SummaryUtilities>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Scenario, ProjectKey, retrofitIndex: retrofitIndex.ToString(), scenarioKey: ScenarioKey));
				if (val is IEnumerable<int>)
				{
					int[] array = ((IEnumerable<int>)val).ToArray();
					if (((IEnumerable<int>)val).Count() != 0)
					{
						DesignViewerIndex -= array.Length - 1;
						Func<int, SummaryUtilities, int> func = delegate(int designIndex, SummaryUtilities summaryCollection)
						{
							int num4 = 0;
							using (IEnumerator<SummaryUtilityInformationView> enumerator2 = summaryCollection.GetEnumerator())
							{
								while (enumerator2.MoveNext() && enumerator2.Current.DesignIndex != designIndex)
								{
									num4++;
								}
							}
							return num4;
						};
						int num = array.Length;
						int num2 = array[0];
						for (int num3 = array[num - 1]; num3 >= num2; num3--)
						{
							if (!ignoreDeleteInHXNet)
							{
								DeleteOldDesign(num3);
							}
							int index = func(num3, summaryUtilities);
							summaryUtilities.RemoveAt(index);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (val < 0)
					{
						goto IL_0471;
					}
					if (!ignoreDeleteInHXNet)
					{
						this.DeleteOldDesign(val);
					}
					SummaryUtilityInformationView summaryUtilityInformationView = null;
					foreach (SummaryUtilityInformationView item in summaryUtilities)
					{
						if (item.BelongTo == UtilityBelongTo.ModifyHeatExchanger && item.RetrofitIndex == CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex)
						{
							summaryUtilityInformationView = item;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (summaryUtilityInformationView != null)
					{
						summaryUtilities.Remove(summaryUtilityInformationView);
					}
				}
				if (summaryUtilities != null && summaryUtilities.Count > 0)
				{
					base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Scenario, ProjectKey, ScenarioKey), summaryUtilities);
					if (summaryUtilities.Count == 2)
					{
						string moniker = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_PaybackPeriod, ProjectKey, ScenarioKey);
						base.DataResolver.Write(moniker, 0);
						string moniker2 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_TotalCapitalCost, ProjectKey, ScenarioKey);
						base.DataResolver.Write(moniker2, 0);
						string moniker3 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_NewSurfaceArea, ProjectKey, ScenarioKey);
						base.DataResolver.Write(moniker3, 0);
					}
				}
				int designViewerIndexer = _designViewerIndexer - 1;
				_designViewerIndexer = designViewerIndexer;
				designViewerIndexer = RetrofitDesignIndex - 1;
				RetrofitDesignIndex = designViewerIndexer;
				string moniker4 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_RetrofitIndex, ProjectKey, ScenarioKey);
				base.DataResolver.Write(moniker4, RetrofitDesignIndex);
				DeleteDesign(retrofitIndex);
			}
			goto IL_0471;
			IL_0471:
			waitHandle?.Set();
		}

		protected abstract dynamic ExtractCurrentRetrofitOwnedDesigns(string retrofitIndex);

		protected virtual void DeleteDesign(string retrofitIndex)
		{
		}

		private void DoAddOperation(object state)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			bool canUpdate = false;
			StartEnergyAnalysis startEnergyAnalysis = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysis>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) as StartEnergyAnalysis;
			try
			{
				SendRetrofit();
				ProceedSearchSolution();
				bool flag2 = false;
				if (_retrofitStatus is IEnumerable<double>)
				{
					foreach (dynamic item in _retrofitStatus)
					{
						if (item == 7.0 || item == 31.0)
						{
							flag2 = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					flag2 = ((_retrofitStatus == null) ? ((object)false) : (_retrofitStatus == 28.0));
				}
				if (startEnergyAnalysis != null && startEnergyAnalysis.DashboardGenerate)
				{
					if (_designViewerIndexerTable != null && _designViewerIndexerTable.Count > 1 && _designViewerIndexerTable.ContainsKey(ProjectKey + ScenarioKey))
					{
						_designViewerIndexerTable.Remove(ProjectKey + ScenarioKey);
					}
					if (_retrofitSet != null && _retrofitSet.Count > 1 && _retrofitSet.ContainsKey(ProjectKey + ScenarioKey + "DesignIndex"))
					{
						_retrofitSet.Remove(ProjectKey + ScenarioKey + "DesignIndex");
					}
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					if (flag2 && DesignViewerIndex <= 0)
					{
						DesignViewerIndex = 1;
					}
					if (flag2 && RetrofitDesignIndex < 0)
					{
						RetrofitDesignIndex = 0;
					}
					int retrofitDesignIndex = RetrofitDesignIndex + 1;
					RetrofitDesignIndex = retrofitDesignIndex;
					flag = Process(null);
					retrofitDesignIndex = _designViewerIndexer + 1;
					_designViewerIndexer = retrofitDesignIndex;
					canUpdate = flag;
					goto IL_04c0;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				goto IL_04c0;
			}
			goto IL_053f;
			IL_04c0:
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisAgent>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			if (!flag)
			{
				DoDeleteOperation(RetrofitDesignIndex.ToString());
				_updateProgressBarArgs = new WorkflowControlStateArgs
				{
					ControlType = WorkflowControl.ModifyHeatExchanger_ProcessBar,
					State = new object[5]
					{
						"SolutionStatus",
						-1,
						0,
						string.Empty,
						startEnergyAnalysis.DashboardGenerate
					}
				};
			}
			goto IL_053f;
			IL_053f:
			if (_updateProgressBarArgs.ControlType == WorkflowControl.ModifyHeatExchanger_ProcessBar)
			{
				IEnergyAnalysisAgent energyAnalysisAgent = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisAgent>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
				StartEnergyAnalysis startEnergyAnalysis2 = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysis>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) as StartEnergyAnalysis;
				if (energyAnalysisAgent != null && startEnergyAnalysis2.DashboardGenerate && _updateProgressBarArgs.State.GetType() == typeof(object[]) && ((object[])_updateProgressBarArgs.State).Length > 2)
				{
					object obj = ((object[])_updateProgressBarArgs.State)[2];
					energyAnalysisAgent.OnGenerateProgress(0.0, double.Parse(obj.ToString()));
				}
			}
			if (_retrofitStatus is double && _retrofitStatus == 26.0)
			{
				Thread.Sleep(500);
			}
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<WorkflowControlStateEvent>().Publish(_updateProgressBarArgs);
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Publish(new DataSourceChangedEventArgs
			{
				SourceType = DataSourceType,
				CanUpdate = canUpdate
			});
		}

		private void SendRetrofit()
		{
			base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage("/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.0:Cost.980", Convert.ToDouble("1.0e+7", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real);
			SendSepcialRetrofit();
			base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.{0}:RunRetrofitOptimization", (CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex < 0) ? RetrofitDesignIndex : CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex));
		}

		protected abstract void SendSepcialRetrofit();

		protected bool ProceedNewSummaryUtility(object value, dynamic designIndexOrBelongTo = null)
		{
			double addNewAreaCold;
			double addNewAreaHot = (addNewAreaCold = 0.0);
			Task.WaitAll(Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaCold)).ContinueWith((Task<object> coldTsk) => addNewAreaCold = (double)coldTsk.Result), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaHot)).ContinueWith((Task<object> hotTsk) => addNewAreaHot = (double)hotTsk.Result));
			if (addNewAreaCold == -32767.0 || addNewAreaHot == -32767.0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			SummaryUtilities summaryUtilities = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<SummaryUtilities>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Scenario, ProjectKey, ScenarioKey));
			if (summaryUtilities == null || summaryUtilities.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!(value is object[] array) || array.Length < 2)
			{
				return false;
			}
			SummaryUtilityInformationView summaryUtilityInformationView = RegisterUtility(addNewAreaHot, addNewAreaCold, UnitValueFactory.Create(base.ValueUnit) ?? UnitValueFactory.Create(ValueUnitEnum.MW));
			if (summaryUtilities.Contains(summaryUtilityInformationView))
			{
				return true;
			}
			double num = 1.0;
			try
			{
				num = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).OperationHoursPerYear;
				summaryUtilityInformationView.Cost = ((double)(object)base.ValueUnitConvert.Convert(double.Parse(summaryUtilityInformationView.Cost) * num, PropertyEnum.Fiscal)).ToString("N0", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture).Replace(",", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture.NumberFormat.NumberGroupSeparator);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			if (designIndexOrBelongTo != null)
			{
				SummaryUtilityInformationView summaryUtilityInformationView2 = null;
				if (designIndexOrBelongTo is UtilityBelongTo)
				{
					summaryUtilityInformationView2 = summaryUtilities.FirstOrDefault((SummaryUtilityInformationView item) => item.BelongTo == designIndexOrBelongTo);
				}
				else
				{
					int designIndex = default(int);
					if ((!int.TryParse(designIndexOrBelongTo.ToString(), out designIndex)))
					{
						return false;
					}
					summaryUtilityInformationView2 = summaryUtilities.FirstOrDefault((SummaryUtilityInformationView item) => item.DesignIndex == designIndex);
				}
				if (summaryUtilityInformationView2 != null)
				{
					summaryUtilityInformationView.RetrofitIndex = summaryUtilityInformationView2.RetrofitIndex;
					Regex regex = new Regex("\\s(\\d+?)\\s");
					summaryUtilityInformationView.Name = regex.Replace(summaryUtilityInformationView.Name, " " + regex.Match(summaryUtilityInformationView2.Name).Groups[1].Value + " ", 1);
					summaryUtilities[summaryUtilities.IndexOf(summaryUtilityInformationView2)] = summaryUtilityInformationView;
					base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Scenario, ProjectKey, ScenarioKey), summaryUtilities);
					return true;
				}
			}
			int index = ((summaryUtilities.Count > 1) ? (summaryUtilities.Count - 1) : 0);
			summaryUtilities.Insert(index, summaryUtilityInformationView);
			base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Scenario, ProjectKey, ScenarioKey), summaryUtilities);
			return true;
		}

		protected abstract dynamic SearchSolution();

		private void ProceedSearchSolution(bool isUpdate = false)
		{
			if (!isUpdate)
			{
				_retrofitStatus = SearchSolution();
			}
			else if (base.DataExtractor is DataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation { DesignViewerIndexer: var designViewerIndexer } dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation)
			{
				dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.DesignViewerIndexer = _designViewerIndexer;
				_retrofitStatus = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.RetrofitStatus);
				if (_retrofitStatus == null)
				{
					_retrofitStatus = -1.0;
				}
				dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.DesignViewerIndexer = designViewerIndexer;
			}
			else
			{
				_retrofitStatus = -1.0;
			}
			double num = 28.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			int num3 = 0;
			if (_retrofitStatus is IEnumerable<double>)
			{
				IEnumerable<double> enumerable = (IEnumerable<double>)_retrofitStatus;
				foreach (double item in enumerable)
				{
					if (item == 7.0 || item == 31.0)
					{
						num2 += 1.0;
						num = item;
					}
				}
				if (num2 == 0.0)
				{
					num = -1.0;
					num3 = enumerable.Count();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num = ((_retrofitStatus is double) ? _retrofitStatus : ((object)(-1)));
				if (num == 28.0)
				{
					num2 = 1.0;
				}
				num3 = ((_retrofitStatus is double) ? 1 : 0);
			}
			if (!ConstValueDefine.RETROFIT_SOLUTION_CODE_SET.Contains(num))
			{
				num = -1.0;
			}
			num2 = ((num != -1.0) ? num2 : 0.0);
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (num == -1.0)
			{
				DataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation obj = base.DataExtractor as DataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation;
				obj.DesignViewerIndexer = DesignViewerIndex + num3;
				text = (obj.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.RetrofitDesign_LogFile) ?? string.Empty).ToString();
			}
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisAgent>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			StartEnergyAnalysis startEnergyAnalysis = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysis>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) as StartEnergyAnalysis;
			_updateProgressBarArgs = new WorkflowControlStateArgs
			{
				ControlType = WorkflowControl.ModifyHeatExchanger_ProcessBar,
				State = new object[5] { "SolutionStatus", num, num2, text, startEnergyAnalysis.DashboardGenerate }
			};
			if (isUpdate)
			{
				if (num != 31.0 && num != 28.0 && num != 7.0)
				{
					_updateProgressBarArgs = new WorkflowControlStateArgs
					{
						ControlType = WorkflowControl.ModifyHeatExchanger_ProcessBar,
						State = new object[5] { "SolutionStatus", 36, num2, text, startEnergyAnalysis.DashboardGenerate }
					};
				}
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<WorkflowControlStateEvent>().Publish(_updateProgressBarArgs);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateDesignViewer(object designIndexOrBelongTo)
		{
			_isUpdate = true;
			if (designIndexOrBelongTo is int num)
			{
				base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_DesignIndex, ProjectKey, ScenarioKey), num);
			}
			SwapOldDesign();

			{
				string moniker = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_RetrofitIndex, ProjectKey, ScenarioKey);
				int num2 = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<int>(moniker);
				Process(num2);

				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Publish(new DataSourceChangedEventArgs
				{
					SourceType = DataSourceType
				});
				DeleteOldDesign(DesignViewerIndex);
			}
			_isUpdate = false;
			if (UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysis>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) is StartEnergyAnalysis startEnergyAnalysis)
			{
				startEnergyAnalysis.SetDocumentDirty();
			}
		}

		private void SwapOldDesign()
		{
