				base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.{0}:OpenAddAreaHXView", CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex));
				base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.{0}:RunRetrofitOptimization", CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex));
				ProceedSearchSolution(isUpdate: true);
				if (_retrofitStatus == 7.0 || _retrofitStatus == 28.0 || _retrofitStatus == 31.0)
				{
					string value = new Regex("[^/]+/[^/]+").Match(dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value;
					string text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{1}", dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.ScenarioViewMoniker.Substring(0, dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.ScenarioViewMoniker.LastIndexOf('/')), new Regex("\\d+\\(Scenario.+\\)+?").Match(dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value);
					base.DataExtractor.SendAttachObject(value + ":Viewable.299", text + "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200." + CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex);
					base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(text + ":SwapDesigns");
				}
			}
		}

		protected double CalculateCostSaving(IEnumerable<double> costIndeices, double[] heatFlows, IValueUnit valueConvert)
		{
			if (costIndeices == null || heatFlows == null || costIndeices.Count() != heatFlows.Length)
			{
				return -1.0;
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			for (int i = 0; i < heatFlows.Length; i++)
			{
				num += costIndeices.ElementAt(i) * heatFlows[i];
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected double CalculateGHGEmission(IEnumerable<double> ghgEmissionCoefficients, double[] heatFlows, IValueUnit valueConvert)
		{
			if (ghgEmissionCoefficients == null || heatFlows == null || ghgEmissionCoefficients.Count() != heatFlows.Count())
			{
				return -1.0;
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			for (int i = 0; i < heatFlows.Length; i++)
			{
				num += ghgEmissionCoefficients.ElementAt(i) * heatFlows[i];
			}
			return num;
		}
	}
	internal class DataProcessForModifyHeatExchanger : DataProcessForHeatExchangerBaseOperation
	{
		private static Dictionary<string, int> _owendDesignIndexTable = new Dictionary<string, int>(10);

		private int _ownedDesignIndex
		{
			get
			{
				if (_owendDesignIndexTable.ContainsKey(ProjectKey + base.ScenarioKey))
				{
					return _owendDesignIndexTable[ProjectKey + base.ScenarioKey];
				}
				return -1;
			}
			set
			{
				if (_owendDesignIndexTable.ContainsKey(ProjectKey + base.ScenarioKey))
				{
					_owendDesignIndexTable[ProjectKey + base.ScenarioKey] = value;
				}
				else
				{
					_owendDesignIndexTable.Add(ProjectKey + base.ScenarioKey, value);
				}
			}
		}

		protected override int CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex => _ownedDesignIndex;

		public DataProcessForModifyHeatExchanger(IUnityContainer container, EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor extrator)
			: base(container, extrator)
		{
		}

		protected override void SendSepcialRetrofit()
		{
			base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.{0}:OpenAddAreaHXView", base.RetrofitDesignIndex));
		}

		protected override dynamic SearchSolution()
		{
			return base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.RetrofitStatus);
		}

		protected override bool Process(object state)
		{
			if (!(base.DataExtractor is DataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation))
			{
				return false;
			}
			dataExtractorForHeatExchangerBaseOperation.DesignViewerIndexer = ((_ownedDesignIndex <= 0) ? base.DesignViewerIndex : _ownedDesignIndex);
			SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection value = ProceedHeatExchangers() ?? new SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection();
			try
			{
				if (state == null)
				{
					_ownedDesignIndex = base.RetrofitDesignIndex;
					ProceedNewSummaryUtility(base.OriginalParameters);
				}
				double num = RegisterPaybackPeriod();
				string text = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_ModifyHeatExchanger, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, (state ?? ((object)_ownedDesignIndex)).ToString());
				string moniker = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Payback_ModifyHeatExchanger, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, (state ?? ((object)_ownedDesignIndex)).ToString());
				string moniker2 = text;
				string moniker3 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_RetrofitIndex, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey);
				base.DataResolver.Write(moniker3, base.RetrofitDesignIndex);
				base.DataResolver.Write(moniker2, value);
				base.DataResolver.Write(moniker, num);
				return true;
			}
			catch (ArgumentOutOfRangeException)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		private void SetLockedVariablesAsDefault(int heatExchangersCount)
		{
			double[] array = new double[heatExchangersCount];
			for (int i = 0; i < heatExchangersCount; i++)
			{
				array[i] = 0.0;
			}
			base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.{1}:Boolean.{0}.[]", 317, _ownedDesignIndex), array, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex);
		}

		protected override SummaryUtilityInformationView RegisterUtility(double hotUtility, double coldUtility, IValueUnit valueUnit)
		{
			IEnumerable<UtilityInformation> enumerable = null;
			try
			{
				enumerable = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<IEnumerable<UtilityInformation>>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Utility, ProjectKey));
			}
			catch (ResolutionFailedException)
			{
				return new SummaryUtilityInformationView();
			}
			IOrderedEnumerable<UtilityInformation> source = from item in enumerable
				where item.UtilityType == UtilityType.COOLING || item.UtilityType == UtilityType.HEATING
				orderby item.OriginalOrder
				select item;
			double[] heatFlows = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HeatFlows) as double[];
			double num = CalculateGHGEmission(source.Select((UtilityInformation item) => item.GHGEmissionCoefficient), heatFlows, valueUnit);
			double num2 = 3600.0 * CalculateCostSaving(source.Select((UtilityInformation item) => item.CostIndices), heatFlows, valueUnit);
			UtilityInformation utilityInformation = enumerable.FirstOrDefault((UtilityInformation item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
			UtilityInformation utilityInformation2 = enumerable.LastOrDefault((UtilityInformation item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
			double num3 = hotUtility;
			double num4 = coldUtility;
			double num5 = source.Sum((UtilityInformation item) => item.GHGCurrent);
			double num6 = 3600.0 * source.Sum((UtilityInformation item) => item.CostIndices * item.OriginalCurrentValue);
			double[] array = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_NewArea) as double[];
			double num7 = array.Sum();
			ModifyHXSummaryUtilityInformationView modifyHXSummaryUtilityInformationView = new ModifyHXSummaryUtilityInformationView
			{
				NameData = ((array != null) ? num7 : 0.0),
				Hot = num3.ToString(),
				AddedArea = num7,
				Cold = num4.ToString(),
				Reduction = ((0.0 - num3 - num4 + utilityInformation.Current + utilityInformation2.Current) / (utilityInformation.Current + utilityInformation2.Current) * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				GHGFlow = num.ToString(),
				GHGReduction = ((num5 - num) / ((num5 == 0.0) ? 1.0 : num5) * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				Cost = num2.ToString(),
				CostPercentage = ((num6 - num2) / num6 * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				BelongTo = UtilityBelongTo.ModifyHeatExchanger,
				ValueUnitConverter = UnitValueFactory.Create(base.ValueUnit),
				RetrofitIndex = base.RetrofitDesignIndex
			};
			modifyHXSummaryUtilityInformationView.Name = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, UIResources.AddAreaChange, ((double)(object)modifyHXSummaryUtilityInformationView.ValueUnitConverter.Convert((double)modifyHXSummaryUtilityInformationView.NameData, PropertyEnum.Area)).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture), base.RetrofitDesignIndex, SystemUnit_Label.Instance.AreaUserUnit);
			return modifyHXSummaryUtilityInformationView;
		}

		private double RegisterPaybackPeriod()
		{
			double num = (double)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_PaybackPeriod);
			num /= 31536000.0;
			base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_PaybackPeriod, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey), num);
			return num;
		}

		protected override dynamic ExtractCurrentRetrofitOwnedDesigns(string retrofitIndex)
		{
			if (!int.TryParse(retrofitIndex, out var result))
			{
				result = -1;
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected override void DeleteDesign(string retrofitIndex)
		{
			int ownedDesignIndex = _ownedDesignIndex - 1;
			_ownedDesignIndex = ownedDesignIndex;
			string text = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_ModifyHeatExchanger, ProjectKey, null, retrofitIndex);
			base.DataResolver.Teardown(text);
			base.DataResolver.ClearProjectKeys(text);
			int result = -1;
			if (int.TryParse(retrofitIndex, out result))
			{
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<WorkflowControlStateEvent>().Publish(new WorkflowControlStateArgs
				{
					ControlType = WorkflowControl.ClearCachedData,
					State = new object[3]
					{
						base.ScenarioKey,
						FormCategory.AddNewE400,
						result
					}
				});
			}
		}

		public override void Restore(object state)
		{
			if (state == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			object[] array = state as object[];
			if (array.Length < 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			string moniker = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_ModifyHeatExchanger, array[2].ToString(), array[0].ToString(), array[1].ToString());
			SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection>(moniker);
			if (surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection != null)
			{
				foreach (SurfaceAreanEntityView item in surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection)
				{
					item.RegesterEvent(base.OnMaxExtraAreaChange, base.OnMinimumApproachTemperatureChange);
				}
			}
			base.DesignViewerIndex = base.RetrofitDesignIndex;
		}
	}
	internal class DataProcessForProjectSetup : WorkflowDataProcess
	{
		private string _uiTreeNodekey = string.Empty;

		private static CultureInfo _engineeringLanguage;

		public static CultureInfo EngineeringLanguage
		{
			get
			{
				if (_engineeringLanguage != null)
				{
					return _engineeringLanguage;
				}
				string name = "en-US";
				try
				{
					object value = Registry.GetValue("HKEY_CURRENT_USER\\Software\\AspenTech\\Engineering\\Language", "", "eng");
					if (value != null)
					{
						switch (value.ToString().ToLower())
						{
						case "eng":
							name = "en-US";
							break;
						case "fra":
							name = "fr-FR";
							break;
						case "ita":
							name = "it-IT";
							break;
						case "deu":
							name = "de-DE";
							break;
						case "spa":
							name = "es-ES";
							break;
						case "jpn":
							name = "ja-JP";
							break;
						case "chs":
							name = "zh-CN";
							break;
						case "kor":
							name = "ko-KR";
							break;
						case "rus":
							name = "ru-RU";
							break;
						case "ptb":
							name = "pt-BR";
							break;
						case "qps":
							name = "qps-ploc";
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				_engineeringLanguage = new CultureInfo(name);
				return _engineeringLanguage;
			}
		}

		internal DataProcessForProjectSetup(IUnityContainer container, EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor extrator)
			: base(container, extrator)
		{
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeSelectionEvent>().Subscribe(OnNewNodeKey);
		}

		private void OnNewNodeKey(UITreeNodeSelectionArgs e)
		{
			object[] array = e.State as object[];
			if (_uiTreeNodekey == e.NodeKey && array != null && array.Length > 2 && array[0] is bool && (bool)array[0] && array[1] is OperationEnum && (OperationEnum)array[1] == OperationEnum.Delete)
			{
				_uiTreeNodekey = string.Empty;
			}
			else
			{
				_uiTreeNodekey = e.NodeKey;
			}
		}

		public override void WorkflowProcess(object state)
		{
			if (state is object[] array && array.Length >= 2)
			{
				OperationEnum operationEnum = ((array[0] is OperationEnum) ? ((OperationEnum)array[0]) : OperationEnum.UnKnow);
				string text = ((array.Length == 3 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[2] as string)) ? array[2].ToString().Split('.')[0] : "Project 1");
				switch (operationEnum)
				{
				case OperationEnum.CreateDefault:
				case OperationEnum.Add:
					ProceedMatchConstraints(text);
					ProceedEconomicMatches(array[1], text);
					break;
				case OperationEnum.Save:
					Save(text);
					break;
				case OperationEnum.Restore:
					Restore(text);
					break;
				case OperationEnum.Delete:
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void ProceedMatchConstraints(string projectKey)
		{
			IList<string> list = null;
			double[,] array = null;
			IList<string> list2 = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.MatchConstraints_HeatStream) as IList<string>;
			list = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.MatchConstraints_ColdStream) as IList<string>;
			array = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.MatchConstraints_Config) as double[,];
			if (list2 != null && list != null && array != null)
			{
				UIResources.Culture = EngineeringLanguage;
				string[] source = new string[2]
				{
					UIResources.ProjectSetup_Constraints_MatchConstraintsOption_Allow,
					UIResources.ProjectSetup_Constraints_MatchConstraintsOption_Forbidden
				};
				base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_MatchConstraints, projectKey), new ProcessStreamCollection
				{
					HotStreams = list2.AsEnumerable(),
					ColdStreams = list.AsEnumerable(),
					IsInitializeed = true,
					ModelNmaes = source.AsEnumerable(),
					MatchesIndexer = array,
					MatcheChange = SetMatchConstraintsStreamConfig
				});
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Publish(new DataSourceChangedEventArgs
				{
					SourceType = DataSourceTypeEnum.MatchConstraintsStream
				});
			}
		}

		private void SetMatchConstraintsStreamConfig(string demention, double value)
		{
			base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(":Boolean." + 1200 + "." + demention.TrimStart('.'), value, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real);

		}

		private void SyncUpEconomicData()
		{
			try
			{
				StartEnergyAnalysis startEnergyAnalysis = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysis>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) as StartEnergyAnalysis;
				HYSYSParameters hYSYSParameters = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
				string paras = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_uiTreeNodekey) ? "Project 1" : _uiTreeNodekey.Split('.')[0]);
				IList<string> utNames = hYSYSParameters.UtilityNames as IList<string>;
				startEnergyAnalysis.extractSavingInformation(hYSYSParameters.UtilityFactors, hYSYSParameters.UtilityDeltaMinTemprature, utNames, transferIsGood: true, paras);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		private void ProceedEconomicMatches(object modelNames, string projectKey)
		{
			if (!(modelNames is IEnumerable<string> enumerable))
			{
				return;
			}
			Collection<string> collection = new Collection<string>();
			foreach (string item in enumerable)
			{
				collection.Add(item);
			}
			IList<string> list = null;
			double[,] array = null;
			IList<string> list2 = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Streams_HotProcess) as IList<string>;
			list = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Streams_ColdProcess) as IList<string>;
			array = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Streams_Configs) as double[,];
			if (list2 != null && list != null && array != null)
			{
				base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_EconomicMatches, projectKey), new ProcessStreamCollection
				{

					ColdStreams = list.AsEnumerable(),

					MatchesIndexer = array,
					MatcheChange = SetEcomonicMatche
				});
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Publish(new DataSourceChangedEventArgs
				{
					SourceType = DataSourceTypeEnum.ProcessStream
				});
			}
		}

		private void SetEcomonicMatche(string demetion, double value)
		{
			base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, ":Enum.{0}.{1}", 510, demetion.TrimStart('.')), value, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real);
		}

		public override void Save(object state)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(state as string))
			{
				string projectKey = state.ToString();
				ProcessStreamCollection processStreamCollection = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<ProcessStreamCollection>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_MatchConstraints, projectKey));
				if (processStreamCollection != null)
				{
					base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_MatchConstraints, projectKey), processStreamCollection);
				}
				ProcessStreamCollection processStreamCollection2 = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<ProcessStreamCollection>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_EconomicMatches, projectKey));
				if (processStreamCollection2 != null)
				{
					base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_EconomicMatches, projectKey), processStreamCollection2);
				}
			}
		}

		public override void Restore(object state)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(state as string))
			{
				string projectKey = state.ToString();
				ProcessStreamCollection processStreamCollection = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<ProcessStreamCollection>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_MatchConstraints, projectKey));
				if (processStreamCollection != null)
				{
					processStreamCollection.MatcheChange = SetMatchConstraintsStreamConfig;
					base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_MatchConstraints, projectKey), processStreamCollection);
				}
				ProcessStreamCollection processStreamCollection2 = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<ProcessStreamCollection>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_EconomicMatches, projectKey));
				if (processStreamCollection2 != null)
				{
					processStreamCollection2.MatcheChange = SetEcomonicMatche;
					base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_EconomicMatches, projectKey), processStreamCollection2);
				}
			}
		}

	internal class DataProcessForRelocateHeatExchanger : DataProcessForHeatExchangerBaseOperation
	{
		private static int _designIndexStartAt = -1;

		private static int _ownedDesignIndex = -1;

		private string _heatExchangerName = string.Empty;

		private bool _isUpdateOperation;

		private static Dictionary<string, IList<int>> _currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign = new Dictionary<string, IList<int>>();

		private int CurrentDesignIndexStartAt
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 1;
				Dictionary<string, IList<int>>.ValueCollection values = _currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Values;
				for (int i = 0; i < base.RetrofitDesignIndex - 1; i++)
				{
					num += values.ElementAt(i).Count;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		protected override int CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex
		{
			get
			{
				try
				{
					_ownedDesignIndex = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<int>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_DesignIndex, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey));
				}
				catch (ResolutionFailedException)
				{
					_ownedDesignIndex = -1;
				}
				return _ownedDesignIndex;
			}
		}

		private string _currentOwnedRetrofitKey => FormatOwnedRetrofitKey(ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, base.RetrofitDesignIndex.ToString());

		internal DataProcessForRelocateHeatExchanger(IUnityContainer container, EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor extrator)
			: base(container, extrator)
		{
		}

		protected override void SendSepcialRetrofit()
		{
			base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.{0}:RepipHX", (CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex < 0) ? base.RetrofitDesignIndex : CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex));
		}

		protected override SummaryUtilityInformationView RegisterUtility(double hotUtility, double coldUtility, IValueUnit valueUnit)
		{
			if (_isUpdateOperation)
			{
				DataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger = base.DataExtractor as DataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger;
				string value = new Regex("[^/]+/[^/]+").Match(dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value;
				string text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{1}", dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker.Substring(0, dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker.LastIndexOf('/')), new Regex("\\d+\\(Scenario.+\\)+?").Match(dataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value);
				base.DataExtractor.SendAttachObject(value + ":Viewable.299", text + "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200." + CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex);
			}
			IEnumerable<UtilityInformation> enumerable = null;
			try
			{
				enumerable = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<IEnumerable<UtilityInformation>>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Utility, ProjectKey));
			}
			catch (ResolutionFailedException)
			{
				return new SummaryUtilityInformationView();
			}
			IOrderedEnumerable<UtilityInformation> source = from item in enumerable
				where item.UtilityType == UtilityType.COOLING || item.UtilityType == UtilityType.HEATING
				orderby item.OriginalOrder
				select item;
			double[] heatFlows = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HeatFlows) as double[];
			double num = CalculateGHGEmission(source.Select((UtilityInformation item) => item.GHGEmissionCoefficient), heatFlows, valueUnit);
			double num2 = 3600.0 * CalculateCostSaving(source.Select((UtilityInformation item) => item.CostIndices), heatFlows, valueUnit);
			UtilityInformation utilityInformation = enumerable.FirstOrDefault((UtilityInformation item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
			UtilityInformation utilityInformation2 = enumerable.LastOrDefault((UtilityInformation item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
			double num3 = hotUtility;
			double num4 = coldUtility;
			double num5 = source.Sum((UtilityInformation item) => item.GHGCurrent);
			double num6 = 3600.0 * source.Sum((UtilityInformation item) => item.CostIndices * item.OriginalCurrentValue);
			return new SummaryUtilityInformationView
			{
				Name = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, UIResources.RelocateChange, base.RetrofitDesignIndex, _heatExchangerName),
				NameData = _heatExchangerName,
				Hot = num3.ToString(),
				Cold = num4.ToString(),
				Reduction = ((0.0 - num3 - num4 + utilityInformation.Current + utilityInformation2.Current) / (utilityInformation.Current + utilityInformation2.Current) * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				GHGFlow = num.ToString(),
				GHGReduction = ((num5 - num) / ((num5 == 0.0) ? 1.0 : num5) * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				Cost = num2.ToString(),
				CostPercentage = ((num6 - num2) / num6 * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				BelongTo = UtilityBelongTo.RelocateHeatExchanger,
				DesignIndex = (_isUpdateOperation ? CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex : base.DesignViewerIndex),
				ValueUnitConverter = UnitValueFactory.Create(base.ValueUnit),
				RetrofitIndex = base.RetrofitDesignIndex
			};
		}

		protected override dynamic SearchSolution()
		{
			return base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddAndRelocate_RetrofitStatus);
		}

		protected override bool Process(object state)
		{
			if (!(base.DataExtractor is DataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger))
			{
				return false;
			}
			dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.DesignViewerIndexer = 0;
			double baseDesignCold;
			double baseDesignHeat = (baseDesignCold = 0.0);
			Task.WaitAll(Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaCold)).ContinueWith((Task<object> coldTsk) => baseDesignCold = Convert.ToDouble(coldTsk.Result, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaHot)).ContinueWith((Task<object> hotTsk) => baseDesignHeat = Convert.ToDouble(hotTsk.Result, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)));
			_isUpdateOperation = state != null;
			if (!_isUpdateOperation)
			{
				return ProceedRelocateHeatExchanger(baseDesignHeat, baseDesignCold);
			}
			int retrofitIndex = ((state is int) ? ((int)state) : (-1));
			string value = new Regex("[^/]+/[^/]+").Match(dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value;
			string text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{1}", dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker.Substring(0, dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker.LastIndexOf('/')), new Regex("\\d+\\(Scenario.+\\)+?").Match(dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value);
			base.DataExtractor.SendAttachObject(value + ":Viewable.299", text + "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200." + CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex);
			return UpdateSpecifiedDesignHeatExchangers(CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex, baseDesignHeat, baseDesignCold, retrofitIndex);
		}

		private bool ProceedRelocateHeatExchanger(double baseDesignHeat, double baseDesignCold)
		{
			if (!(base.DataExtractor is DataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger))
			{
				return false;
			}
			IList<double> list = base.RetrofitStatus;
			if (list == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			RelocateHeatExchangerViewCollection relocateHeatExchangerViewCollection = new RelocateHeatExchangerViewCollection();
			_ownedDesignIndex = (_designIndexStartAt = base.DesignViewerIndex);
			Collection<int> value = new Collection<int>();
			if (!_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.ContainsKey(_currentOwnedRetrofitKey))
			{
				_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Add(_currentOwnedRetrofitKey, value);
			}
			else
			{
				_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign[_currentOwnedRetrofitKey] = value;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			Collection<KeyValuePair<string, object>> collection = new Collection<KeyValuePair<string, object>>();
			int i = base.DesignViewerIndex;
			int num = i;
			try
			{
				for (; i < _designIndexStartAt + list.Count; i++)
				{
					if (list.ElementAt(i - _designIndexStartAt) == 31.0)
					{
						base.DesignViewerIndex = num;
						num++;
						dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.DesignViewerIndexer = base.DesignViewerIndex;
						int num2 = FindRelocateHeatExchangerIndex();
						if (num2 >= 0)
						{
							string value2 = new Regex("[^/]+/[^/]+").Match(dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value;
							string text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{1}", dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker.Substring(0, dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker.LastIndexOf('/')), new Regex("\\d+\\(Scenario.+\\)+?").Match(dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.ScenarioViewMoniker).Value);
							base.DataExtractor.SendAttachObject(value2 + ":Viewable.299", text + "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200." + base.DesignViewerIndex);
							dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.HeatExchangerIndex = num2;
							relocateHeatExchangerViewCollection.Add(ProceedSingleHeatExchanger(base.DesignViewerIndex, num2, baseDesignCold, baseDesignHeat));
							SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection = ProceedHeatExchangers(num2, UIResources.Relocate) ?? new SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection();
							string text2 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_RelocateHeatExchanger_Item, "Project 1", base.ScenarioKey, null, base.DesignViewerIndex.ToString());
							stringBuilder.AppendFormat(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ";{0}={1}", text2, SerializeToString(surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection));
							collection.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, object>(text2, surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection));
							ProceedNewSummaryUtility(base.OriginalParameters);
							_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign[_currentOwnedRetrofitKey].Add(base.DesignViewerIndex);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeEvent>().Publish(new UITreeNodeArgs
			{
				State = new object[3]
				{
					FormCategory.RelocateE108,
					base.ScenarioKey,
					base.RetrofitDesignIndex.ToString()
				},
				NodeText = _heatExchangerName
			});
			string text3 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_RelocateHeatExchanger, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, base.RetrofitDesignIndex.ToString());
			string moniker = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_RetrofitIndex, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey);
			base.DataResolver.Write(moniker, base.RetrofitDesignIndex);
			stringBuilder.Insert(0, text3);
			base.DataResolver.Write(stringBuilder.ToString(), relocateHeatExchangerViewCollection);
			base.DataResolver.Write(text3, relocateHeatExchangerViewCollection, collection.ToArray());
			return relocateHeatExchangerViewCollection.Count > 0;
		}

		private int FindRelocateHeatExchangerIndex()
		{
			int result = -1;
			string strA = (string)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.RelocateXchanger_Name);
			if (!(base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HXName) is string[] array))
			{
				return result;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if (string.Compare(strA, array[i]) == 0)
				{
					result = i;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private RelocateHeatExchangerView ProceedSingleHeatExchanger(int designIndex, int hxIndex, double baseDesignCold, double baseDesignHeat)
		{
			double paybacks;
			double energySavingPerTime;
			double totalCold;
			double totalHeat;
			double num;
			double totalCosts = (paybacks = (energySavingPerTime = (totalCold = (totalHeat = (num = 0.0)))));
			double[] extraCost = null;
			string coldSideName;
			string text;
			string text2;
			string hotSideName = (coldSideName = (text = (text2 = null)));
			Task.WaitAll(Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.RelocateXchanger_Name)).ContinueWith(delegate(Task<object> nameTsk)
			{
				_heatExchangerName = nameTsk.Result as string;
			}), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_TotalCapitalCost)).ContinueWith((Task<object> costTsk) => totalCosts = (double)costTsk.Result), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_Payback)).ContinueWith((Task<object> payBackTsk) => paybacks = (double)payBackTsk.Result), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_ExtraCapitalCost)).ContinueWith((Task<object> extraTsk) => extraCost = extraTsk.Result as double[]), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_EnergySaving)).ContinueWith((Task<object> savingTsk) => energySavingPerTime = (double)savingTsk.Result), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaCold)).ContinueWith((Task<object> coldTsk) => totalCold = (double)coldTsk.Result), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_AddNewAreaHot)).ContinueWith((Task<object> hotTsk) => totalHeat = (double)hotTsk.Result), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_HotSideHXName)).ContinueWith((Task<object> hotSideTsk) => hotSideName = hotSideTsk.Result as string).ContinueWith(delegate(Task<string> hotSideHXNameTsk)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hotSideHXNameTsk.Result) && base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_HotStreamName) is string[] array && array.Length > hxIndex)
				{
					hotSideName = array[hxIndex];
				}
			}), Task.Factory.StartNew(() => base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_ColdSideHXName)).ContinueWith((Task<object> coldSideTsk) => coldSideName = coldSideTsk.Result as string).ContinueWith(delegate(Task<string> coldSideHXNameTsk)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(coldSideHXNameTsk.Result) && base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.ModifyXchanger_ColdStreamName) is string[] array && array.Length > hxIndex)
				{
					coldSideName = array[hxIndex];
				}
			}));
			if (!hotSideName.Contains("_DummyClrIgnore"))
			{
				text = "Upstream to " + hotSideName;
			}
			else
			{
				string text3 = hotSideName.Replace("_DummyClrIgnore", " ");
				text = "Downstream to " + text3;
			}
			if (!coldSideName.Contains("_DummyHtrIgnore"))
			{
				text2 = "Upstream to " + coldSideName;
			}
			else
			{
				string text4 = coldSideName.Replace("_DummyHtrIgnore", " ");
				text2 = "Downstream to " + text4;
			}
			double num2 = -1.0;
			try
			{
				num2 = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).OperationHoursPerYear;
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			num = (double)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.AddXchanger_NewArea);
			return new RelocateHeatExchangerView(new HeatExchanger
			{
				Area = num,
				Name = _heatExchangerName,
				TotalCapitalCost = totalCosts,
				Payback = paybacks / 31536000.0,
				ValueConverter = UnitValueFactory.Create(base.ValueUnit),
				EnergySavingPerTime = energySavingPerTime * 3600.0 * num2,
				EnergySaving = baseDesignCold + baseDesignHeat - totalHeat - totalCold,
				EnergySavingPercentForSI = (baseDesignCold + baseDesignHeat - totalHeat - totalCold) / (baseDesignCold + baseDesignHeat) * 100.0,
				HotSideName = text,
				ColdSideName = text2
			})
			{
				DesignIndex = designIndex
			};
		}

		private bool UpdateSpecifiedDesignHeatExchangers(int desIndex, double baseDesignHeat, double baseDesignCold, int retrofitIndex)
		{
			if (!(base.DataExtractor is DataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger))
			{
				return false;
			}
			string text = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_RelocateHeatExchanger, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, retrofitIndex.ToString());
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = base.DataResolver.ResolveData(text);
			if (dictionary == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			string key = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_RelocateHeatExchanger_Item, "Project 1", base.ScenarioKey, null, desIndex.ToString());
			if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!(dictionary[text] is RelocateHeatExchangerViewCollection relocateHeatExchangerViewCollection))
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num = (dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.DesignViewerIndexer = CurrentRetrofitOwnedDesignIndex);
			int num2 = FindRelocateHeatExchangerIndex();
			if (num2 == -1)
			{
				return false;
			}
			dataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger.HeatExchangerIndex = num2;
			RelocateHeatExchangerView relocateHeatExchangerView = ProceedSingleHeatExchanger(num, num2, baseDesignCold, baseDesignHeat);
			if (!MMViewModelContainer.bIsBackground)
			{
				relocateHeatExchangerView.IsSelected = true;
			}
			int index = num - CurrentDesignIndexStartAt;
			relocateHeatExchangerViewCollection.RemoveAt(index);
			relocateHeatExchangerViewCollection.Insert(index, relocateHeatExchangerView);
			dictionary[key] = ProceedHeatExchangers(num2, UIResources.Relocate) ?? new SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(text);
			Collection<KeyValuePair<string, object>> collection = new Collection<KeyValuePair<string, object>>();
			foreach (string key2 in dictionary.Keys)
			{
				if (string.Compare(key2, "ID") != 0 && string.Compare(key2, text) != 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.AppendFormat(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, ";{0}={1}", key2, (dictionary[key2] is string) ? dictionary[key2] : SerializeToString(dictionary[key2]));
					collection.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, object>(key2, dictionary[key2]));
				}
			}
			base.DataResolver.Write(stringBuilder.ToString(), relocateHeatExchangerViewCollection);
			base.DataResolver.Write(text, relocateHeatExchangerViewCollection, collection.ToArray());
			return true;
		}

		protected override dynamic ExtractCurrentRetrofitOwnedDesigns(string retrofitIndex)
		{
			string text = FormatOwnedRetrofitKey(ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, retrofitIndex);
			if (!_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Keys.Contains(text))
			{
				_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Add(text, new Collection<int>());
			}
			return _currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign[text];
		}

		private string FormatOwnedRetrofitKey(string projectKey, string scenarioKey, string retrofitIndex)
		{
			return string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}{1}{2}{3}{4}", scenarioKey, "RetrofitIndex", "DesignIndex", retrofitIndex, projectKey);
		}

		protected override void DeleteDesign(string retrofitIndex)
		{
			_ownedDesignIndex = -1;
			string text = FormatOwnedRetrofitKey(ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, retrofitIndex);
			if (!_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Keys.Contains(text))
			{
				return;
			}
			_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Remove(text);
			string text2 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_RelocateHeatExchanger, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey, retrofitIndex.ToString());
			base.DataResolver.Teardown(text2);
			base.DataResolver.ClearProjectKeys(text2);
			string moniker = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_DesignIndex, ProjectKey, base.ScenarioKey);
			base.DataResolver.Write(moniker, 0);
			int num = Convert.ToInt32(retrofitIndex);
			if (num > 1)
			{
				IEnumerable<int> source = ExtractCurrentRetrofitOwnedDesigns((num - 1).ToString()) as IEnumerable<int>;
				if (source.Count() > 0)
				{
					_designIndexStartAt -= source.Count();
				}
			}
			int result = -1;
			if (int.TryParse(retrofitIndex, out result))
			{
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<WorkflowControlStateEvent>().Publish(new WorkflowControlStateArgs
				{
					ControlType = WorkflowControl.ClearCachedData,
					State = new object[3]
					{
						base.ScenarioKey,
						FormCategory.AddNewE400,
						result
					}
				});
			}
		}

		public override void Restore(object state)
		{
			if (!(state is object[] array) || array.Length < 3 || string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[0] as string) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[1] as string) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[2].ToString()))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_RelocateHeatExchanger, array[2].ToString(), array[0].ToString(), array[1].ToString());
			if (!(base.DataResolver.ResolveData(text) is Dictionary<string, object> dictionary))
			{
				return;
			}
			Collection<int> collection = new Collection<int>();
			Regex regex = new Regex(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "({0}(\\d+))$", UtilityBelongTo.RelocateHeatExchanger));
			foreach (string key2 in dictionary.Keys)
			{
				if (string.Compare(key2, "ID", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(text, key2, ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare("ProjectID", key2) == 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (dictionary[key2] is SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection)
				{
					foreach (SurfaceAreanEntityView item in surfaceAreanEntityViewCollection)
					{
						item.RegesterEvent(base.OnMaxExtraAreaChange, base.OnMinimumApproachTemperatureChange);
					}
				}
				collection.Add(int.Parse(regex.Match(key2).Groups[2].Value));
			}
			string key = FormatOwnedRetrofitKey(array[2].ToString(), array[0].ToString(), array[1].ToString());
			if (!_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign.Add(key, collection);
				base.DesignViewerIndex += collection.Count;
			}
			else
			{
				_currentRetrofitOwnedAllDesign[key] = collection;
			}
		}
	}

		internal DataProcessForSavingPotentials(IUnityContainer container, EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor dataExtractor)
			: base(container, dataExtractor)
		{
		}

		public override void WorkflowProcess(object state)
		{
			if (state != null)
			{
				OperationEnum operationEnum = OperationEnum.UnKnow;
				if (state is object[] array)
				{
					operationEnum = ((array.Length != 0 && array[0] is OperationEnum) ? ((OperationEnum)array[0]) : OperationEnum.CreateDefault);
					_projectKey = ((array.Length == 2 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[1] as string)) ? array[1].ToString().Split('.')[0] : null);
				}
				switch (operationEnum)
				{
				case OperationEnum.CreateDefault:
					Create(state);
					break;
				case OperationEnum.Save:
					Save(_projectKey);
					break;
				case OperationEnum.Restore:
					Restore(_projectKey);
					break;
				case OperationEnum.Add:
				case OperationEnum.Delete:
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void Create(object state)
		{
			if (!(state is object[] array) || array.Length < 5)
			{
				return;
			}
			_gHGEmissionCoefficients = array[0] as IList<double>;
			_deltaTemperatures = array[1] as IList<double>;
			_namesOfUtility = array[2] as IList<string>;
			_analysisedData.UtilityNames = array[3] as IList<string>;
			_valueUnit = ((array.Length < 6) ? ValueUnitEnum.MW : ((array[5] is ValueUnitEnum) ? ((ValueUnitEnum)array[5]) : ValueUnitEnum.MW));
			if (array.Length > 6)
			{
				object[] array2 = array[6] as object[];
				_projectKey = ((array2 != null && array2.Length != 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(array2[0] as string)) ? array2[0].ToString() : (_projectKey ?? (array[6] as string)));
			}
			if (_gHGEmissionCoefficients == null || _deltaTemperatures == null || _namesOfUtility == null || _analysisedData.UtilityNames == null)
			{
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Publish(new DataSourceChangedEventArgs
				{
					SourceType = DataSourceTypeEnum.UtilityView
				});
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				GetAndPublishRelevantData((bool)array[4]);
			}
			catch (InvalidCastException)
			{
				GetAndPublishRelevantData(isTransferSucceed: false);
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				ex2.ToString();
			}
		}

		private void GetAndPublishRelevantData(bool isTransferSucceed, string specifiedUtilityName = null, double specifiedUtilityDeltaT = -1.0, double globalDeltaMin = 10.0)
		{
			SurfaceAreanBaseViewCollection surfaceAreanBaseViewCollection = new SurfaceAreanBaseViewCollection();
			if (isTransferSucceed)
			{
				StartEnergyAnalysis startEnergyAnalysis = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysis>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) as StartEnergyAnalysis;
				if (base.DataExtractor != null && startEnergyAnalysis != null)
				{
					if (startEnergyAnalysis.HasTopologyChanged)
					{
						((DataExtractorForSavingPotentials)base.DataExtractor).SetRefreshStatus();
					}
					((DataExtractorForSavingPotentials)base.DataExtractor).SelectBaseDesign();
				}
				Collection<SurfaceAreanBaseEntity> collection = ProceedHeatExchangers();
				if (collection != null)
				{
					foreach (SurfaceAreanBaseEntity item in collection)
					{
						surfaceAreanBaseViewCollection.Add(new SurfaceAreanBaseView(item));
					}
				}
				ProceedRelevantDataFromEnergyAnalyzer(specifiedUtilityName, specifiedUtilityDeltaT);
				if (collection != null && collection.Count > 0)
				{
					foreach (UtilityInformation utility in _utilityCollection)
					{
						utility.HeatExchangers = collection.Where((SurfaceAreanBaseEntity hxItem) => string.Compare(utility.Name, hxItem.UtilityName, ignoreCase: true) == 0);
					}
					UtilityInformation utilityInformation = _utilityCollection.FirstOrDefault((UtilityInformation item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
					if (utilityInformation != null)
					{
						utilityInformation.HeatExchangers = collection.Where((SurfaceAreanBaseEntity hxItem) => string.IsNullOrEmpty(hxItem.UtilityName));
					}
				}
			}
			double operationHoursPerYear = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).OperationHoursPerYear;
			UtilityView utilityView = new UtilityView(_utilityCollection, SetMinimumDeltaT, null, operationHoursPerYear, globalDeltaMin);
			utilityView.ProcessPinchTemperature = new Temperature((double)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.SavingPotentials_HotSidePinch), (double)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.SavingPotentials_ColdSidePinch));
			base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Utility, _projectKey), _utilityCollection);
			if (surfaceAreanBaseViewCollection != null)
			{
				base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_SavingPotentailsHX, _projectKey), surfaceAreanBaseViewCollection);
			}
			base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_UtilityView, _projectKey), utilityView);
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Publish(new DataSourceChangedEventArgs
			{
				SourceType = DataSourceTypeEnum.UtilityView
			});
		}

					GHGTarget = num4,
					CarbonCostSavingsPerYear = (num3 - num4) * num19 * 31536000.0,
					CarbonCostSavingsPercent = ((num3 == 0.0 || num19 == 0.0) ? 0.0 : ((num3 - num4) / num3 * 100.0)),
					CostIndicesPercentage = (num7 - num8) / Math.Abs(num7) * 100.0
				};
				utilityInformation2.UtilityStatusToolTip = GenerateUtilStatusToolTip(utilName, utilityInformation2.UtilityStatus, UtilityType.HEATING, inletTemp2, outletTemp2);
				collection.Add(utilityInformation2);
			}
			if (collection2.Count > 0)
			{
				UtilityInformation utilityInformation3 = new UtilityInformation
				{
					Name = text + UIResources.Total_Cold_Utilities,
					UtilityStatus = utilityStatus2,
					TargetFlow = -1.0,
					Current = num10,
					Target = num11,
					UtilityType = UtilityType.NONE,
					ValueUnitConvert = valueUnitConvert,
					CostIndicesAbsoluteCost = num14,
					GHGCurrent = num12,
					GHGTarget = num13,
					CarbonCostSavingsPerYear = (num12 - num13) * num19 * 31536000.0,
					CarbonCostSavingsPercent = ((num12 == 0.0 || num19 == 0.0) ? 0.0 : ((num12 - num13) / num12 * 100.0)),
					CostIndicesPercentage = (num6 - num9) / Math.Abs(num6) * 100.0
				};
				utilityInformation3.UtilityStatusToolTip = GenerateUtilStatusToolTip(utilName2, utilityInformation3.UtilityStatus, UtilityType.COOLING, inletTemp, outletTemp);
				collection2.Add(utilityInformation3);
			}
			_utilityCollection = collection;
			foreach (UtilityInformation item in collection2)
			{
				_utilityCollection.Add(item);
			}
		}

		private string GenerateUtilStatusToolTip(string utilName, string utilStatusType, UtilityType enumType, double inletTemp, double outletTemp)
		{
			string result = null;
			double num = (double)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.SavingPotentials_HotSidePinch);
			double num2 = (double)base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.SavingPotentials_ColdSidePinch);
			IValueUnit valueUnit = UnitValueFactory.Create(_valueUnit);
			_ = ((double)(object)valueUnit.Convert(inletTemp, PropertyEnum.Temperature)).ToString("F1", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + SystemUnit_Label.Instance.TempUserUnit;
			_ = ((double)(object)valueUnit.Convert(outletTemp, PropertyEnum.Temperature)).ToString("F1", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + SystemUnit_Label.Instance.TempUserUnit;
			string arg = ((double)(object)valueUnit.Convert(num, PropertyEnum.Temperature)).ToString("F1", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + SystemUnit_Label.Instance.TempUserUnit;
			string arg2 = ((double)(object)valueUnit.Convert(num2, PropertyEnum.Temperature)).ToString("F1", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + SystemUnit_Label.Instance.TempUserUnit;
			switch (utilStatusType)
			{
			case "Sufficient":
				if (enumType == UtilityType.HEATING)
				{
					result = UIResources.HotUtilStatus_Sufficient;
				}
				if (enumType == UtilityType.COOLING)
				{
					result = UIResources.ColdUtilStatus_Sufficient;
				}
				break;
			case "Insufficient":
				if (enumType == UtilityType.HEATING)
				{
					result = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, UIResources.HotUtilStatus_Insufficient, utilName);
				}
				if (enumType == UtilityType.COOLING)
				{
					result = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, UIResources.ColdUtilStatus_Insufficient, utilName);
				}
				break;
			case "Cross pinch":
				if (enumType == UtilityType.HEATING)
				{
					result = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, UIResources.HotUtilStatus_CrossPinch, utilName, arg);
				}
				if (enumType == UtilityType.COOLING)
				{
					result = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, UIResources.ColdUtilStatus_CrossPinch, utilName, arg2);
				}
				break;
			default:
				result = null;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void SetUtilMinDelta(string utilityName, double deltaTemperature)
		{
			int i;
			for (i = 0; i < _analysisedData.UtilityNames.Count && string.Compare(utilityName, _analysisedData.UtilityNames[i]) != 0; i++)
			{
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < _namesOfUtility.Count; j++)
			{
				if (string.Compare(utilityName, _namesOfUtility[j]) == 0)
				{
					if (_deltaTemperatures.Count > j)
					{
						_deltaTemperatures[j] = deltaTemperature;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IApplicationWrapper>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).SetUtilityDeltaTMin(utilityName, deltaTemperature);
			base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, ":DeltaTemp.{0}.{1}", 442, i), deltaTemperature, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real);
			if (UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysis>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) is StartEnergyAnalysis startEnergyAnalysis)
			{
				_valueUnit = startEnergyAnalysis.ValueUnit;
			}
			GetAndPublishRelevantData(isTransferSucceed: true, utilityName, deltaTemperature);
		}

		private bool SetMinimumDeltaT(double differMinTemperature)
		{
			try
			{
				base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(":DeltaTemp.1", differMinTemperature, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real);
				UtilityView utilityView = null;
				IDataResolver dataResolver = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IDataResolver>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
				if (dataResolver != null)
				{
					utilityView = dataResolver.ResolveData<UtilityView>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_UtilityView));
				}
				StartEnergyAnalysis startEnergyAnalysis = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysis>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) as StartEnergyAnalysis;
				if (utilityView != null && startEnergyAnalysis != null)
				{
					_valueUnit = startEnergyAnalysis.ValueUnit;
					GetAndPublishRelevantData(isTransferSucceed: true, null, -1.0, utilityView.GlobalMinDeltaTemperature);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				_ = ex.Message;
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override void Save(object state)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(state as string))
			{
				string projectKey = state.ToString();
				Collection<UtilityInformation> value = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<Collection<UtilityInformation>>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Utility, projectKey));
				base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Utility, projectKey), value);
				UtilityView value2 = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<UtilityView>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_UtilityView, projectKey));
				base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_UtilityView, projectKey), value2);
				SurfaceAreanBaseViewCollection value3 = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<SurfaceAreanBaseViewCollection>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_SavingPotentailsHX, projectKey));
				base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_SavingPotentailsHX, projectKey), value3);
			}
		}

		public override void Restore(object state)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(state as string))
			{
				return;
			}
			string projectKey = state.ToString();
			_utilityCollection = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<Collection<UtilityInformation>>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Utility, projectKey));
			ReFixUtilityDTMin(_utilityCollection);
			UtilityView utilityView = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<UtilityView>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_UtilityView, projectKey));
			double operationHoursPerYear = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).OperationHoursPerYear;
			UtilityView utilityView2 = new UtilityView(_utilityCollection, SetMinimumDeltaT, null, operationHoursPerYear);
			utilityView2.ProcessPinchTemperature = utilityView.PPTempInternalUnits;
			base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_UtilityView, projectKey), utilityView2);
			base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Utility, projectKey), _utilityCollection);
			SurfaceAreanBaseViewCollection surfaceAreanBaseViewCollection = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<SurfaceAreanBaseViewCollection>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_SavingPotentailsHX, projectKey));
			if (surfaceAreanBaseViewCollection == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (SurfaceAreanBaseView item in surfaceAreanBaseViewCollection)
			{
				item.RegisterUMethods(SetOverallUBySimSpec, ResetOverallUByAEA, SetOverallUByUserSpec);
			}
		}

		private void ReFixUtilityDTMin(Collection<UtilityInformation> _utilityCollection)
		{
			string text = "    ";
			if (_utilityCollection == null || _utilityCollection.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < _utilityCollection.Count; i++)
			{
				UtilityInformation utilityInformation = _utilityCollection[i];
				if (utilityInformation.Name == text + UIResources.Total_Cold_Utilities || utilityInformation.Name == text + UIResources.Total_Hot_Utilities)
				{
					_utilityCollection[i].DeltaMinTemperature = -1000.0;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	internal class DataProcessForSummary : WorkflowDataProcess
	{
		private string _scenarioKey;

		private string _projectKey;

		private static Collection<string> _scenarioContainer = new Collection<string>();

		private static object _syncObject = new object();

		private static Dictionary<string, string> _namesofHXNetScenarios = new Dictionary<string, string>();

		private double MinLMTDCorrection
		{
			get
			{
				Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>())?.GetValue(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Constraint_MinLMTDConrrectionFactor, _projectKey));
				if (dictionary == null || dictionary.Count == 0)
				{
					return 0.8;
				}
				if (double.TryParse(dictionary.First().Value, out var result))
				{
					return result;
				}
				return 0.8;
			}
		}

		private double _exchangerMinApproachTemperature
		{
			get
			{
				Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>())?.GetValue(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Constraint_ExchangerMinApproachTemperature, _projectKey));
				if (dictionary == null || dictionary.Count == 0)
				{
					return 10.0;
				}
				if (double.TryParse(dictionary.First().Value, out var result))
				{
					return result;
				}
				return 10.0;
			}
		}

		private double _exchangerMaxAreaPerShell
		{
			get
			{
				Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>())?.GetValue(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Constraint_MAxAreaPerShell, _projectKey));
				if (dictionary == null || dictionary.Count == 0)
				{
					return 500.0;
				}
				if (double.TryParse(dictionary.First().Value, out var result))
				{
					return result;
				}
				return 500.0;
			}
		}

		public DataProcessForSummary(IUnityContainer container, EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor dataExtractor)
			: base(container, dataExtractor)
		{
		}

		public override void WorkflowProcess(object state)
		{
			if (!(state is object[] array) || array.Length < 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			_scenarioKey = ((array.Length >= 4) ? array[3] : base.DataExtractor.ScenarioIndexer)?.ToString() + " Scenario";
			_projectKey = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(array[0] as string)) ? array[0].ToString().Split('.')[0] : "Project 1");
			OperationEnum result;
			if (array.Length >= 5)
			{
				if (!Enum.TryParse<OperationEnum>(array[4].ToString(), out result))
				{
					result = OperationEnum.UnKnow;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				result = OperationEnum.UnKnow;
			}
			switch (result)
			{
			case OperationEnum.Delete:
				lock (_syncObject)
				{
					string text = array[0] as string;
					if (!_namesofHXNetScenarios.ContainsKey(text))
					{
						SystemUnit_Label.ScenarioDeleteEventWaitHandle.Set();
						break;
					}
					int currentScenarioIndex = GetCurrentScenarioIndex(_namesofHXNetScenarios[text]);
					if (currentScenarioIndex == int.MinValue)
					{
						SystemUnit_Label.ScenarioDeleteEventWaitHandle.Set();
						break;
					}
					if (_namesofHXNetScenarios.ContainsKey(text))
					{
						_namesofHXNetScenarios.Remove(text);
					}
					DeleteScenarioProcess(array[1] as string, currentScenarioIndex.ToString(), text);
					break;
				}
			case OperationEnum.Save:
				Save(_scenarioKey);
				break;
			case OperationEnum.Restore:
				Restore(_scenarioKey);
				break;
			default:
				lock (_syncObject)
				{
					AddScenarioProcess(state);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddScenarioProcess(object state)
		{
			object[] paras = state as object[];
			int retType = 0;
			int maxDes = 5;
			if (paras.Length >= 7)
			{
				retType = Convert.ToInt32(paras[5]);
				maxDes = Convert.ToInt32(paras[6]);
			}
			if (paras != null && paras.Length > 1)
			{
				string text = paras[0] as string;
				if (_scenarioContainer != null && _scenarioContainer.Count > 1 && _scenarioContainer.Contains(text))
				{
					_scenarioContainer.Remove(text);
				}
				if (_namesofHXNetScenarios != null && _namesofHXNetScenarios.Count > 1 && _namesofHXNetScenarios.ContainsKey(text))
				{
					_namesofHXNetScenarios.Remove(text);
				}
			}
			Task.WaitAll(Task.Factory.StartNew(delegate
			{
				if (!_scenarioContainer.Contains(paras[0] as string))
				{
					if (paras.Length >= 3 && !(bool)paras[2])
					{
						if (!(paras[1] is string) || !(base.DataExtractor is DataExtractorForSummary dataExtractorForSummary))
						{
							return;
						}
						dataExtractorForSummary.SendAttachObject(paras[1]?.ToString() + ":Viewable.299", dataExtractorForSummary.ViewScenarioMoniker);
					}
					base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(":RetrofitNow");
					_scenarioContainer.Add(paras[0] as string);
				}
			}).ContinueWith(delegate
			{
				if (base.DataExtractor is DataExtractorForSummary dataExtractorForSummary)
				{
					base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{1}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{0}:ExtraData.{2}", (paras.Length >= 4) ? paras[3] : base.DataExtractor.ScenarioIndexer, dataExtractorForSummary.ProjectMoniker, 765), MinLMTDCorrection, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real);
					base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{1}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{0}/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.0:DeltaTemp.{2}", (paras.Length >= 4) ? paras[3] : base.DataExtractor.ScenarioIndexer, dataExtractorForSummary.ProjectMoniker, 607), _exchangerMinApproachTemperature, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real);
					base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{1}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{0}/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.0:Area.{2}", (paras.Length >= 4) ? paras[3] : base.DataExtractor.ScenarioIndexer, dataExtractorForSummary.ProjectMoniker, 304), _exchangerMaxAreaPerShell, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real);
					if (retType == 1)
					{
						base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{1}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{0}:ExtraData.{2}", (paras.Length >= 4) ? paras[3] : base.DataExtractor.ScenarioIndexer, dataExtractorForSummary.ProjectMoniker, 767), maxDes, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real);
					}
					if (retType == 2)
					{
						base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{1}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{0}:ExtraData.{2}", (paras.Length >= 4) ? paras[3] : base.DataExtractor.ScenarioIndexer, dataExtractorForSummary.ProjectMoniker, 768), maxDes, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real);
					}
				}
			}), Task.Factory.StartNew(ProceedSummaryUtilities));
			string newScenarioName = GetNewScenarioName();
			if (!_namesofHXNetScenarios.ContainsKey(paras[0] as string))
			{
				_namesofHXNetScenarios.Add(paras[0] as string, newScenarioName);
			}
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Publish(new DataSourceChangedEventArgs
			{
				SourceType = DataSourceTypeEnum.Summary
			});
		}

		private void DeleteScenarioProcess(string moniker, string scenarioSubffixIndexer, string scenarioNodeKey)
		{
			if (_scenarioContainer.Contains(scenarioNodeKey))
			{
				_scenarioContainer.Remove(scenarioNodeKey);
			}
			DataExtractorForSummary dataExtractorForSummary = base.DataExtractor as DataExtractorForSummary;
			string objectMoniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{1}", dataExtractorForSummary.ViewScenarioMoniker.Substring(0, dataExtractorForSummary.ViewScenarioMoniker.LastIndexOf('/')), scenarioSubffixIndexer);
			dataExtractorForSummary.SendAttachObject(moniker + ":Viewable.299", objectMoniker);
			dataExtractorForSummary.SendBackDoorMessage(dataExtractorForSummary.ViewScenarioMoniker.Substring(0, dataExtractorForSummary.ViewScenarioMoniker.LastIndexOf('/')) + ":RemoveFromDesignView");
			SystemUnit_Label.ScenarioDeleteEventWaitHandle.Set();
		}

		private void ProceedSummaryUtilities()
		{
			IEnumerable<UtilityInformation> enumerable = null;
			try
			{
				enumerable = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<IEnumerable<UtilityInformation>>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Utility, _projectKey));
			}
			catch (ResolutionFailedException)
			{
				return;
			}
			UtilityInformation utilityInformation = enumerable.FirstOrDefault((UtilityInformation item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
			UtilityInformation utilityInformation2 = enumerable.LastOrDefault((UtilityInformation item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
			IEnumerable<UtilityInformation> source = enumerable.Where((UtilityInformation item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.COOLING || item.UtilityType == UtilityType.HEATING);
			double num = source.Sum((UtilityInformation item) => item.CostIndices * item.OriginalCurrentValue);
			double num2 = 1.0;
			try
			{
				num2 = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).OperationHoursPerYear;
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			SummaryUtilities summaryUtilities = new SummaryUtilities();
			summaryUtilities.Add(new SummaryUtilityInformationView
			{
				Name = UIResources.Current_Simulation_Case,
				Hot = utilityInformation.Current.ToString(),
				Cold = utilityInformation2.Current.ToString(),
				Reduction = "--",
				GHGFlow = (utilityInformation.GHGCurrent + utilityInformation2.GHGCurrent).ToString(),
				GHGReduction = "--",
				Cost = ((double)(object)base.ValueUnitConvert.Convert(num * num2 * 3600.0, PropertyEnum.Fiscal)).ToString("N0", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture).Replace(",", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture.NumberFormat.NumberGroupSeparator),
				CostPercentage = "--",
				ValueUnitConverter = UnitValueFactory.Create(base.ValueUnit),
				BelongTo = (UtilityBelongTo)15
			});
			double num3 = source.Sum((UtilityInformation item) => item.GHGTarget);
			double num4 = source.Sum((UtilityInformation item) => item.GHGCurrent);
			double num5 = source.Sum((UtilityInformation item) => item.CostIndices * item.OriginalTargetValue);
			summaryUtilities.Add(new SummaryUtilityInformationView
			{
				Name = UIResources.Target,
				Hot = utilityInformation.Target.ToString(),
				Cold = utilityInformation2.Target.ToString(),
				Reduction = ((utilityInformation.Current + utilityInformation2.Current - utilityInformation2.Target - utilityInformation.Target) / (utilityInformation.Current + utilityInformation2.Current) * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				GHGFlow = num3.ToString(),
				GHGReduction = ((num4 - num3) / ((num4 == 0.0) ? 1.0 : num4) * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				Cost = ((double)(object)base.ValueUnitConvert.Convert(num5 * num2 * 3600.0, PropertyEnum.Fiscal)).ToString("N0", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture).Replace(",", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture.NumberFormat.NumberGroupSeparator),
				CostPercentage = ((num - num5) / num * 100.0).ToString("0.0", CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
				ValueUnitConverter = UnitValueFactory.Create(base.ValueUnit),
				BelongTo = (UtilityBelongTo)15
			});
			base.DataResolver.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Scenario, _projectKey, _scenarioKey), summaryUtilities);
		}

		private string GetNewScenarioName()
		{
			return base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Summary_ScenarioName).ToString();
		}

		private int GetCurrentScenarioIndex(string currentName)
		{
			base.DataExtractor.SendBackDoorMessage(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}:ExtraData.{1}", ((DataExtractorForSummary)base.DataExtractor).ProjectMoniker, 258), currentName, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Text);
			int result = int.MinValue;
			object obj = base.DataExtractor.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Summary_ScenarioIndex);
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return result;
			}
			if (string.Compare(obj.ToString(), "-1") == 0)
			{
				return result;
			}
			return (int)(double)obj;
		}

		public void ClearScenarioCollections()
		{
			if (_namesofHXNetScenarios != null)
			{
				_namesofHXNetScenarios.Clear();
			}
			if (_scenarioContainer != null)
			{
				_scenarioContainer.Clear();
			}
		}

		public override void Save(object state)
		{
			base.DataResolver.Write("ScenarioMapping", _namesofHXNetScenarios);
		}

		public override void Restore(object state)
		{
			if (state == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string moniker = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Scenario, _projectKey, state.ToString());
			SummaryUtilities summaryUtilities = base.DataResolver.ResolveData<SummaryUtilities>(moniker);
			if (summaryUtilities != null)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				foreach (SummaryUtilityInformationView item in summaryUtilities)
				{
					if (item.ValueUnitConverter == null)
					{
						item.ValueUnitConverter = UnitValueFactory.Create(base.ValueUnit);
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					base.DataResolver.Write(moniker, summaryUtilities);
				}
			}
			_namesofHXNetScenarios = (base.DataResolver.ResolveData("ScenarioMapping") as Dictionary<string, string>) ?? new Dictionary<string, string>();
		}
	}
	internal class EnergyAnalyzerApplication : IDisposable
	{
		private object _energyAnalyzerPointer;

		private _Application _baseApp;

		private CommandProtectionOverride _command;

		private string _appName;

		private string _lastError;

		private bool _isDisposed;

		private const string Current_Version = "41.0";

		private const string Older_Version = "40.0";

		private const string Energy_Analyzer_Regestry_Key = "Software\\AspenTech\\Aspen Energy Analyzer\\";

		private const string Energy_Analyzer_InterfaceFile = "aeainterface.dll";

		private const string Energy_Analyzer_AppPath = "\\concept\\concepts.exe";

		private static bool? _isInstalled;

		private static string _eneginFileName;

		public object EnergyAnalyzerIDispatch => _energyAnalyzerPointer;

		public string AppName => _appName;

		public string LastError => _lastError;

		public bool IsInitialized => _baseApp != null;

		public bool Visible
		{
			get
			{
				return _baseApp.Visible;
			}
			set
			{
				_baseApp.Visible = value;
			}
		}

		public static bool IsInstalled
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_isInstalled.HasValue)
				{
					_isInstalled = false;
					RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("Software\\AspenTech\\Aspen Energy Analyzer\\41.0");
					if (registryKey != null)
					{
						string text = (registryKey.GetValue("HXNettop") ?? string.Empty).ToString();
						_isInstalled = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) && File.Exists(text.TrimEnd('\\') + "\\aeainterface.dll");
					}
				}
				return _isInstalled.Value;
			}
		}

		public static string ExecutableFile
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsInstalled)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_eneginFileName))
				{
					RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("Software\\AspenTech\\Aspen Energy Analyzer\\41.0") ?? Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("Software\\AspenTech\\Aspen Energy Analyzer\\40.0");
					if (registryKey != null)
					{
						string text = (registryKey.GetValue("HXNettop") ?? string.Empty).ToString();
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							_eneginFileName = Path.Combine(text, "concepts.exe");
						}
					}
				}
				return _eneginFileName;
			}
		}

		public EnergyAnalyzerApplication(string simName)
		{
			try
			{
				_baseApp = (IsInstalled ? ((HXNetSingleUse)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("DABE4207-B95D-48D6-BEC3-5B3F67CFC6F2")))) : null);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				_ = ex.Message;
				_baseApp = null;
			}
			_appName = simName;
		}

		public bool ConceptualDesignCaseStudy_Open(string fileName)
		{
			try
			{
				_command = _baseApp.CommandProtectionFor(_appName, Unused: true);
				_energyAnalyzerPointer = _command.ConceptualDesignCaseStudy_Open(fileName);
				return true;
			}
			catch (COMException ex)
			{
				_lastError = ex.Message;
				return false;
			}
			catch (UnauthorizedAccessException ex2)
			{
				_lastError = ex2.Message;
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool ConceptualDesignCaseStudy_Add(string caseName, string type)
		{
			try
			{
				_command = _baseApp.CommandProtectionFor(_appName, Unused: true);
				_energyAnalyzerPointer = _command.ConceptualDesignCaseStudy_Add(caseName, type) as _ConceptualDesignCaseStudy;
			}
			catch (COMException ex)
			{
				_lastError = ex.Message;
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private CommandProtectionOverride CommandProtectionFor(string appName, bool isUsed)
		{
			return _baseApp.CommandProtectionFor(appName, isUsed);
		}

		public void Quit()
		{
			if (_baseApp != null)
			{
				try
				{
					_baseApp.Quit();
				}
				catch (COMException)
				{
				}
				catch (InvalidCastException)
				{
				}
				if (_command != null)
				{
					Marshal.FinalReleaseComObject(_command);
					_command = null;
				}
				if (_baseApp != null)
				{
					Marshal.FinalReleaseComObject(_baseApp);
					_baseApp = null;
				}
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(isDisposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		private void Dispose(bool isDisposing)
		{
			if (!_isDisposed)
			{
				_isDisposed = true;
			}
		}

		~EnergyAnalyzerApplication()
		{
			Dispose(isDisposing: false);
		}
	}

			_scenarioName = scenarioName;
			_firstPartOfMoniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.0({1})/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{2}:", commandPrefix, hipProjectName, scenarioName);
		}

		public bool GetNumOfShiftedTempIntervals(out int numShiftTIs)

			return GetNumberOfTempIntervals(out numShiftTIs, 661);

		public bool SetGraphCoordinates(double xValue, double yValue)
		{
			if (_backDoor == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			string message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.0({1})/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{2}:ExtraData.555", _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName);
			string message2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.0({1})/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{2}:ExtraData.556", _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName);
			RealVariableMessage realVariableMessage = new RealVariableMessage(_backDoor);
			if (realVariableMessage == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			xValue = double.Parse(realVariableMessage.GetData(message).ToString(), CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
			yValue = double.Parse(realVariableMessage.GetData(message2).ToString());
			return true;
		}

		public bool GetLstOfIntervalStreams(int graphType, out string lstIntervalStrms)
		{
			lstIntervalStrms = string.Empty;
			if (_backDoor == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			string message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.0({1})/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{2}:HeatFlow.ExtraData.{3}", _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName, (graphType == 2) ? 661 : 662);
			TextFlexVariableMessage textFlexVariableMessage = new TextFlexVariableMessage(_backDoor);
			if (textFlexVariableMessage == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			object data = textFlexVariableMessage.GetData(message);
			lstIntervalStrms = ((data == null) ? string.Empty : data.ToString());
			return true;
		}

		private bool GetHeatFlowsInformation(out IList<double> result, int commandType)
		{
			result = null;
			if (_backDoor == null)

				return false;
			}
			RealFlexVariableMessage realFlexVariableMessage = new RealFlexVariableMessage(_backDoor);
			if (realFlexVariableMessage == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			result = realFlexVariableMessage.GetData(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.0({1})/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{2}:HeatFlow.{3}.[]", _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName, commandType)) as IList<double>;
			return true;
		}

			if (realFlexVariableMessage == null)
