			return true;
		}

		private bool GetNumberOfTempIntervals(out int numShiftTIs, int commandType)
		{
			numShiftTIs = -1;
			if (_backDoor == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			RealVariableMessage realVariableMessage = new RealVariableMessage(_backDoor);
			if (realVariableMessage == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return int.TryParse(realVariableMessage.GetData(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.0({1})/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{2}:Index.{3}", _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName, commandType)).ToString(), out numShiftTIs);
		}

		public static EnergyAnalyzerBackDoorDXWrapper CreateInstance(BackDoor backDoor, string commandPrefix, string hipProjectName, string scenarioName)
		{
			return new EnergyAnalyzerBackDoorDXWrapper(backDoor, commandPrefix, hipProjectName, scenarioName);
		}

		public void SendCommand(BackDoorMessage message)
		{
			if (message != null)
			{
				string text = string.Empty;
				IBackDoorMessage backDoorMessage = null;
				switch (message.MessageType)
				{
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_PRBPIgnoreOrTreat:
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_IgnoreSteamSubCooling:
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_IgnoreSteamSuper:
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_ExtractLiveSteam:
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_ReactionHeat:
					text = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Boolean.{0}", (int)message.MessageType);
					backDoorMessage = new RealVariableMessage(_backDoor);
					break;
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_BoilerFeedWaterTemperature:
					text = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "Temperature.{0}", (int)message.MessageType);
					backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, _backDoor);
					break;
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_SegTolerances:
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.HeatExchangerCost_ReturnRate:
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.HeatExchangerCost_OperationLifeOfPlant:
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.HeatExchangerCost_OperationHoursPerYear:
					text = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "ExtraData.{0}", (int)message.MessageType);
					backDoorMessage = new RealVariableMessage(_backDoor);
					break;
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.HeatExchangerCost_Models:
					ProceedHeatExchangerCostModel(message.State as IEnumerable<IList<string>>);
					return;
				case BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Utility_DeltaTemperatures:
					text = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "DeltaTemp.{0}.[]", 4421.ToString().Substring(0, 3));
					backDoorMessage = new RealFlexVariableMessage(_backDoor);
					break;
				}
				if (backDoorMessage != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					string message2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.0({1})/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{2}:{3}", _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName, text);
					backDoorMessage.Send(message2, message.State);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ProceedHeatExchangerCostModel(IEnumerable<IList<string>> modelEnumerable)
		{
			if (modelEnumerable == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (IList<string> item in modelEnumerable)
			{
				IBackDoorMessage backDoorMessage = null;
				string text = null;
				object value = null;
				if (item.Count != 6 || !int.TryParse(item[5], out var result))
				{
					result = -1;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < item.Count; i++)
				{
					double result2;
					switch (i)
					{
					case 0:
						backDoorMessage = new TextVariableMessage(_backDoor);
						text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Name.{0}.{1}", 500, (result == -1) ? num : result);
						value = item[i];
						break;
					case 1:
						backDoorMessage = new RealVariableMessage(_backDoor);
						text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Cost.{0}.{1}", 501, (result == -1) ? num : result);
						if (double.TryParse(item[i], out result2))
						{
							value = double.Parse(item[i]);
						}
						if (item[i] == string.Empty)
						{
							value = 10000.0;
						}
						break;
					case 3:
						backDoorMessage = new RealVariableMessage(_backDoor);
						text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "ExtraData.{0}.{1}", 502, (result == -1) ? num : result);
						if (double.TryParse(item[i], out result2))
						{
							value = double.Parse(item[i]);
						}
						if (item[i] == string.Empty)
						{
							value = 800;
						}
						break;
					case 4:
						backDoorMessage = new RealVariableMessage(_backDoor);
						text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Enum.{0}.{1}", 101, (result == -1) ? num : result);
						value = double.Parse(item[i]);
						break;
					case 2:
					{
						backDoorMessage = new RealVariableMessage(_backDoor);
						string text2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.0({1})/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{2}:", _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName);
						text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "ExtraData.{0}.{1}", 503, (result == -1) ? num : result);
						if (double.TryParse(item[i], out result2))
						{
							value = double.Parse(item[i]);
						}
						if (item[i] == string.Empty)
						{
							value = 0.8;
						}
						backDoorMessage.Send(text2 + text, value);
						_backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage(text2 + "ApplyNewBCValues");
						backDoorMessage = null;
						break;
					}
					}
					if (backDoorMessage != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						backDoorMessage.Send(string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.0({1})/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{2}:{3}", _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName, text), value);
					}
				}
				num++;
			}
		}
	}
	internal enum BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum
	{
		Unknown,
		Real,
		Text,
		RealFlex,
		TextFlex
	}
	internal class BackDoorVariableMessageFactory
	{
		private static object _sysLock = new object();

		private static BackDoorVariableMessageFactory _instance;

		public static BackDoorVariableMessageFactory Instance
		{
			get
			{
				lock (_sysLock)
				{
					if (_instance == null)
					{
						_instance = new BackDoorVariableMessageFactory();
					}
					return _instance;
				}
			}
		}

		private BackDoorVariableMessageFactory()
		{
		}

		public IBackDoorMessage CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum type, BackDoor backDoor)
		{
			return type switch
			{
				BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real => new RealVariableMessage(backDoor), 
				BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.RealFlex => new RealFlexVariableMessage(backDoor), 
				BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Text => new TextVariableMessage(backDoor), 
				BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.TextFlex => new TextFlexVariableMessage(backDoor), 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}
	}
	internal interface IBackDoorMessage
	{
		void Send(string message, object value);

		object GetData(string message);
	}
	internal class RealVariableMessage : IBackDoorMessage
	{
		private BackDoor _backDoor;

		public RealVariableMessage(BackDoor backDoor)
		{
			_backDoor = backDoor;
		}

		public void Send(string message, object value)
		{
			if (_backDoor == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(message) || value == null || !double.TryParse(value.ToString(), out var result))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)message);
				if (internalVariableWrapper != null && internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable)
				{
					internalRealVariable.Value = result;
				}
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
			}
			catch (UnauthorizedAccessException)
			{
			}
		}

		public object GetData(string message)
		{
			if (_backDoor == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(message))
			{
				return "-1";
			}
			try
			{
				InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)message);
				if (internalVariableWrapper == null)
				{
					return "-1";
				}
				if (!(internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable))
				{
					return "-1";
				}
				return internalRealVariable.Value;
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
				return "-1";
			}
		}
	}
	internal class TextVariableMessage : IBackDoorMessage
	{
		private BackDoor _backDoor;

		public TextVariableMessage(BackDoor backDoor)
		{
			_backDoor = backDoor;
		}

		public void Send(string message, object value)
		{
			if (_backDoor == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(message) || value == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)message);
				if (internalVariableWrapper == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = internalVariableWrapper.Variable as InternalTextVariable;
				if (internalTextVariable == null)
				{
					internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)message);
					if (internalVariableWrapper == null)
					{
						return;
					}
					internalTextVariable = internalVariableWrapper.Variable as InternalTextVariable;
					if (internalTextVariable == null)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				internalTextVariable.Value = value.ToString();
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
			}
		}

		public object GetData(string message)
		{
			if (_backDoor == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(message))
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			try
			{
				InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)message);
				if (internalVariableWrapper == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = internalVariableWrapper.Variable as InternalTextVariable;
				if (internalTextVariable == null)
				{
					internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)message);
					if (internalVariableWrapper == null)
					{
						return string.Empty;
					}
					internalTextVariable = internalVariableWrapper.Variable as InternalTextVariable;
					if (internalTextVariable == null)
					{
						return string.Empty;
					}
				}
				return internalTextVariable.Value;
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class RealFlexVariableMessage : IBackDoorMessage
	{
		private BackDoor _backDoor;

		public RealFlexVariableMessage(BackDoor backDoor)
		{
			_backDoor = backDoor;
		}

		public void Send(string message, object value)
		{
			if (_backDoor == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(message) || value == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)message);
				if (internalVariableWrapper != null && internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalRealFlexVariable internalRealFlexVariable)
				{
					internalRealFlexVariable.Values = value;
				}
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
			}
		}

		public object GetData(string message)
		{
			if (_backDoor == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(message))
			{
				return null;
			}
			try
			{
				InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)message);
				if (internalVariableWrapper == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (!(internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalRealFlexVariable { Values: var values }))
				{
					return null;
				}
				return values;
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class TextFlexVariableMessage : IBackDoorMessage
	{
		private BackDoor _backDoor;

		public TextFlexVariableMessage(BackDoor backDoor)
		{
			_backDoor = backDoor;
		}

		public void Send(string message, object value)
		{
			if (_backDoor == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(message) || value == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)message);
				if (internalVariableWrapper != null && internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalTextFlexVariable internalTextFlexVariable)
				{
					internalTextFlexVariable.Values = value;
				}
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
			}
		}

		public object GetData(string message)
		{
			if (_backDoor == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(message))
			{
				return null;
			}
			try
			{
				InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)message);
				if (internalVariableWrapper == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (!(internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalTextFlexVariable { Values: var values }))
				{
					return null;
				}
				return values;
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}
	}
	internal abstract class EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor
	{
		private static Regex _scenarioNumberRegx = new Regex("\\d+", RegexOptions.Compiled);

		private static Regex _projectNumberRegx = new Regex("((\\d+)\\s*)$", RegexOptions.Compiled);

		private string _scenarioIndexer;

		private BackDoor _backDoor;

		private string _monikerPrefix;

		public string ScenarioIndexer => _scenarioIndexer;

		protected BackDoor BackDoor => _backDoor;

		protected string MonikerPrefix => _monikerPrefix;

		public EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor(BackDoor backDoor, string commandPrefix, string hipProjectName, string scenarioName)
		{
			_backDoor = backDoor;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hipProjectName) && _projectNumberRegx.IsMatch(hipProjectName))
			{
				int.Parse(_projectNumberRegx.Match(hipProjectName).Groups[2].Value);
			}
			_monikerPrefix = BuildPrefixMoniker(commandPrefix, hipProjectName, scenarioName);
			MatchCollection matchCollection = _scenarioNumberRegx.Matches(scenarioName);
			_scenarioIndexer = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(matchCollection[matchCollection.Count - 1].Value) ? "0" : matchCollection[matchCollection.Count - 1].Value);
		}

		public static string BuildPrefixMoniker(string commandPrefix, string hipProjectName, string scenarioName)
		{
			int num = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hipProjectName) ? 1 : ((!_projectNumberRegx.IsMatch(hipProjectName)) ? 1 : int.Parse(_projectNumberRegx.Match(hipProjectName).Groups[2].Value)));
			string text = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.{1}({2})", commandPrefix, (num > 0) ? (num - 1) : 0, hipProjectName);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(scenarioName))
			{
				return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerScenario.200.{1}", text, scenarioName);
			}
			return text;
		}

		public void SendBackDoorMessage(string message, object value = null, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum valueType = BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Unknown)
		{
			try
			{
				if (value == null || valueType == BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Unknown)
				{
					_backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage((message.IndexOf('/') > 0 && message.Substring(0, message.IndexOf('/')).Equals(_monikerPrefix.Substring(0, _monikerPrefix.IndexOf('/')))) ? message : (_monikerPrefix + message));
				}
				else
				{
					BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(valueType, _backDoor).Send((message.IndexOf('/') > 0 && message.Substring(0, message.IndexOf('/')).Equals(_monikerPrefix.Substring(0, _monikerPrefix.IndexOf('/')))) ? message : (_monikerPrefix + message), value);
				}
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
			}
		}

		public void SendAttachObject(string moniker, string objectMoniker)
		{
			try
			{
				_backDoor.SendAttachObject2(moniker, objectMoniker);
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
			}
		}

		public abstract object ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum messageType);
	}

		[ComVisible(true)]
		public void LaunchEnergyAnalyzer(string SimName)
		{
			if (_baseApp != null)
			{
				DestroyStaticObjects();
			}
			_baseApp = new EnergyAnalyzerApplication(SimName);
			if (_baseApp != null && _baseApp.IsInitialized)
			{
				_baseApp.Visible = false;
			}
			if (_baseApp != null && !_baseApp.IsInitialized)
			{
				CloseProgressBar();
			}
		}

		private void CloseProgressBar()
		{
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<WorkflowControlStateEvent>().Publish(new WorkflowControlStateArgs
			{
				State = "close",
				ControlType = WorkflowControl.ModifyHeatExchanger_ProcessBar
			});
		}

		private void ResetSteps()
		{
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisAgent>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>())?.ResetGenerateStep();
		}

		private void SetAnalysisInput(string SimName, object FSNames, object UtNames, object UtilDTmins)
		{
			if (string.Compare(SimName, "aspenplus") == 0)
			{
				_valueUnit = ValueUnitEnum.Simulator;
			}
			else
			{
				_valueUnit = ValueUnitEnum.HYSYSSimulator;
			}
			_namesOfUtility = UtNames as IList<string>;
			_deltaTemperatures = UtilDTmins as IList<double>;
			_subflowSheets = FSNames as IList<string>;
		}

		private void FinishTheAnalysis(bool Okay, object UtNames, object UtilFactors, object UtilDTmins, object paras)
		{
			if (Okay)
			{
				ProceedWithStream(OperationEnum.Add, paras);
			}
			extractSavingInformation(UtilFactors, UtilDTmins, UtNames, Okay, paras);
			extractCurvesInformation(UtilFactors, UtilDTmins, UtNames, Okay, paras);
			UnityContainerExtensions.RegisterInstance<Action<IList<BackDoorMessage>>>(base.Container, "SendingVariableAction", (Action<IList<BackDoorMessage>>)SendCommand);
			_outSideCommand.Clear();
		}

		private void AddEconomicMessages(object projectkey, Collection<BackDoorMessage> messages)
		{
			HYSYSParameters hYSYSParameters = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			string rateOfReturn = GetRateOfReturn(projectkey as string);
			if (hYSYSParameters != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(rateOfReturn))
			{
				AddEconomicModelMessages(projectkey as string, messages);
				messages.Add(new BackDoorMessage
				{
					MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.HeatExchangerCost_OperationHoursPerYear,
					State = hYSYSParameters.OperationHoursPerYear
				});
				messages.Add(new BackDoorMessage
				{
					MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.HeatExchangerCost_OperationLifeOfPlant,
					State = hYSYSParameters.OperatingLifeOfPlant
				});
				messages.Add(new BackDoorMessage
				{
					MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.HeatExchangerCost_ReturnRate,
					State = rateOfReturn
				});
			}
		}

		private void AddEconomicModelMessages(string projectkey, Collection<BackDoorMessage> messages)
		{
			string path = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Constraint_HeatExchangerModel, projectkey);
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>())?.GetValue(path, isResultXmlSection: true);
			List<HeatExchangerCostView> list = new List<HeatExchangerCostView>();
			if (dictionary == null || dictionary.Count == 0)
			{
				list.Add(new HeatExchangerCostView
				{
					Name = UIResources.HeaterExchangerCost_CostRule_DefaultRuleName,
					ValueUnitConverter = UnitValueFactory.Create(_valueUnit),
					FormulaType = HeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger,
					VariableA = Convert.ToDouble("1.000e+04", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture).ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
					VariableB = 800.ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
					VariableC = 0.8.ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
					Index = 0
				});
				list.Add(new HeatExchangerCostView
				{
					Name = UIResources.HeaterExchangerCost_CostRule_FireHeaterRuleName,
					ValueUnitConverter = UnitValueFactory.Create(_valueUnit),
					FormulaType = HeatExchangerType.FireHeater,
					VariableA = Convert.ToDouble("1.000e+05", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture).ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
					VariableB = Convert.ToDouble("1000", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture).ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
					VariableC = 0.8.ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture),
					Index = 1
				});
			}
			else
			{
				int num = 0;
				for (int i = 0; i < dictionary.Count; i++)
				{
					XElement xElement = XElement.Parse(dictionary.ElementAt(i).Value);
					if (xElement == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					IEnumerable<XElement> enumerable = xElement.Descendants("Name");
					IEnumerable<XElement> enumerable2 = xElement.Descendants("VariableA");
					IEnumerable<XElement> enumerable3 = xElement.Descendants("VariableB");
					IEnumerable<XElement> enumerable4 = xElement.Descendants("VariableC");
					IEnumerable<XElement> enumerable5 = xElement.Descendants("HTConfiguration");
					if (enumerable2 != null && enumerable2.Count() != 0 && enumerable != null && enumerable.Count() != 0 && enumerable3 != null && enumerable3.Count() != 0 && enumerable4 != null && enumerable4.Count() != 0 && enumerable5 != null && enumerable5.Count() != 0)
					{
						try
						{
							list.Add(new HeatExchangerCostView
							{
								Name = enumerable.ElementAt(0).Value,
								ValueUnitConverter = UnitValueFactory.Create(_valueUnit),
								FormulaType = ((string.Compare(enumerable5.ElementAt(0).Value, "0") != 0) ? HeatExchangerType.FireHeater : HeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger),
								VariableA = enumerable2.ElementAt(0).Value,
								VariableB = enumerable3.ElementAt(0).Value,
								VariableC = enumerable4.ElementAt(0).Value,
								Index = num++
							});
						}
						catch (Exception ex)
						{
							_ = ex.Message;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if (list == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Collection<IList<string>> collection = new Collection<IList<string>>();
			foreach (HeatExchangerCostView item in list)
			{
				if (string.Compare(item.Name, UIResources.HeaterExchangerCost_CostRule_NewRuleName, ignoreCase: true) != 0)
				{
					collection.Add(new string[5]
					{
						item.Name,
						item.VariableA,
						item.VariableC,
						item.VariableB,
						((int)item.FormulaType).ToString()
					});
				}
			}
			messages.Add(new BackDoorMessage
			{
				MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.HeatExchangerCost_Models,
				State = collection
			});
		}

		private string GetRateOfReturn(string projectKey)
		{
			double num = 10.0;
			string path = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Constraint_RateOfReturn, projectKey);
			Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument caseDocument = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = caseDocument?.GetValue(path, isResultXmlSection: true);
			if (dictionary != null && dictionary.Count > 0 && double.TryParse(dictionary.First().Value, out var result))
			{
				num = result;
			}
			if (dictionary == null)
			{
				caseDocument.Write(path, 10.0);
			}
			return num.ToString("0.00");
		}

		public void RunAnalysis(string SimName, object SimCase, string FileName, bool hchExists, object FSNames, object UtNames, object UtilFactors, object UtilDTmins, object paras)
		{
			IEnergyAnalysisAgent agent = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisAgent>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			if (agent != null)
			{
				agent.StartExtractingDashboardResults();
			}
			Dispatcher.CurrentDispatcher.BeginInvoke(DispatcherPriority.Input, (Action)delegate
			{
				ResetSteps();
				if (_baseApp != null && _baseApp.IsInitialized)
				{
					SetAnalysisInput(SimName, FSNames, UtNames, UtilDTmins);
					string text = paras as string;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						EACFormFactory.AddEACProjectTreeNode(text);
					}
					bool okay = ProceedHIProjectWithTransfer(_backDoor, SimName, FileName, hchExists, SimCase, paras);
					FinishTheAnalysis(okay, UtNames, UtilFactors, UtilDTmins, paras);
					if (agent != null)
					{
						agent.ShowDashboardResults();
					}
				}
			}).Wait();
		}

		public void StartAnalysis(string SimName, object SimCase, string FileName, object FSNames, object UtNames, object UtilFactors, object UtilDTmins, object paras)
		{
			ResetSteps();
			if (_baseApp != null && _baseApp.IsInitialized)
			{
				SetAnalysisInput(SimName, FSNames, UtNames, UtilDTmins);
				bool okay = ProceedWithTransfer(FileName, SimName, SimCase, paras);
				FinishTheAnalysis(okay, UtNames, UtilFactors, UtilDTmins, paras);
			}
		}

		public void extractSavingInformation(object UtilFactors, object UtilDTmins, object UtNames, bool transferIsGood, object paras)
		{
			bool isRefresh = _projectNameMapping.ContainsKey(paras as string);
			if (transferIsGood)
			{
				SubscribeEvent();
			}
			DataExtractorForSavingPotentials dataExtractorForSavingPotentials = new DataExtractorForSavingPotentials(_backDoor, _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName);
			dataExtractorForSavingPotentials.isRefresh = isRefresh;
			IList<string> list = _namesOfUtilityFromEnergyAnalyzer;
			if (list == null)
			{
				Array utilityNames = null;
				GetUtilityNames(out utilityNames);
				list = ((utilityNames != null) ? (utilityNames as IList<string>) : null);
			}
			new DataProcessForSavingPotentials(base.Container, dataExtractorForSavingPotentials).WorkflowProcess(new object[7] { UtilFactors, UtilDTmins, UtNames, list, transferIsGood, _valueUnit, paras });
		}

		public void extractCurvesInformation(object UtilFactors, object UtilDTmins, object UtNames, bool transferIsGood, object paras)
		{
			bool isRefresh = _projectNameMapping.ContainsKey(paras as string);
			if (transferIsGood)
			{
				SubscribeEvent();
			}
			DataExtractorForCurves dataExtractorForCurves = new DataExtractorForCurves(_backDoor, _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName);
			dataExtractorForCurves.isRefresh = isRefresh;
			IList<string> list = _namesOfUtilityFromEnergyAnalyzer;
			if (list == null)
			{
				Array utilityNames = null;
				GetUtilityNames(out utilityNames);
				list = ((utilityNames != null) ? (utilityNames as IList<string>) : null);
			}
			new DataProcessForCurves(base.Container, dataExtractorForCurves).WorkflowProcess(new object[7] { UtilFactors, UtilDTmins, UtNames, list, transferIsGood, _valueUnit, paras });
		}

		public bool ActivateEACase(string fileName, out bool hchExists)
		{
			if (string.Compare(fileName, _lastOpenedCaseFile, ignoreCase: true) != 0)
			{
				if (!OpenCaseFile(fileName, out hchExists))
				{
					return false;
				}
				_lastOpenedCaseFile = fileName;
			}
			else
			{
				hchExists = IsHchFileReady(GetWorkingFile(fileName));
				_backDoor = _simCase as BackDoor;
				if (_backDoor == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool ProceedWithTransfer(string fileName, string simName, object simulatorDispather, object projectKey)
		{
			ActivateEACase(fileName, out var hchExists);
			return ProceedHIProjectWithTransfer(_backDoor, simName, fileName, hchExists, simulatorDispather, projectKey);
		}

		private bool IsHysysBk(string casefile)
		{
			string extension = Path.GetExtension(casefile);
			if (extension.StartsWith(".bk"))
			{
				return extension != ".bkp";
			}
			return false;
		}

		private string GetHchExtension(string casefile)
		{
			if (IsHysysBk(casefile))
			{
				return Path.GetExtension(casefile).Replace(".bk", ".hc").Substring(1);
			}
			return "hch";
		}

		private string GetXmlExtension(string casefile)
		{
			if (IsHysysBk(casefile))
			{
				return Path.GetExtension(casefile).Replace(".bk", ".xm").Substring(1);
			}
			return "xml";
		}

		public string GetWorkingFile(string fileName)
		{
			string text = null;
			if (_workingFiles.ContainsKey(fileName))
			{
				text = _workingFiles[fileName];
			}
			else
			{
				string text2 = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), Guid.NewGuid().ToString("B"));
				while (Directory.Exists(text2))
				{
					text2 = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), Guid.NewGuid().ToString("B"));
				}
				string text3 = fileName;
				if (string.Compare(Path.GetExtension(fileName), ".apwz", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					IHostApplicationInfoMessenger hostApplicationInfoMessenger = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IHostApplicationInfoMessenger>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
					if (hostApplicationInfoMessenger != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hostApplicationInfoMessenger.CompoundDocRootFile))
					{
						text3 = hostApplicationInfoMessenger.CompoundDocRootFile;
					}
				}
				Directory.CreateDirectory(text2);
				text3 = Path.Combine(Directory.GetCurrentDirectory(), text3.Substring(0, (text3.LastIndexOf('.') > 0) ? text3.LastIndexOf('.') : text3.Length) + "." + GetHchExtension(fileName));
				text = Path.Combine(text2, Path.GetFileName(text3));
				if (File.Exists(text3))
				{
					File.Copy(text3, text, overwrite: true);
					FileInfo fileInfo = new FileInfo(text);
					try
					{
						fileInfo.IsReadOnly = false;
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				string text4 = Path.ChangeExtension(text3, GetXmlExtension(fileName));
				if (File.Exists(text4))
				{
					string text5 = Path.ChangeExtension(text, GetXmlExtension(fileName));
					string destFileName = Path.ChangeExtension(text, "oxml");
					File.Copy(text4, text5, overwrite: true);
					FileInfo fileInfo2 = new FileInfo(text5);
					try
					{
						fileInfo2.IsReadOnly = false;
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
					try
					{
						File.Copy(text5, destFileName, overwrite: true);
					}
					catch
					{
					}
				}
				_workingFiles.Add(fileName, text);
			}
			return text;
		}

		public string GetWorkingFileIfExists(string casename)
		{
			if (_workingFiles.ContainsKey(casename))
			{
				return GetWorkingFile(casename);
			}
			return casename;
		}

		private bool OpenCaseFile(string fileName, out bool isHchFileExists)
		{
			string workingFile = GetWorkingFile(fileName);
			isHchFileExists = IsHchFileReady(workingFile);
			if (_simCase != null)
			{
				_simCase.Close(false, _lastOpenedCaseFile);
				_projectNameMapping.Clear();
			}
			if (!(isHchFileExists ? _baseApp.ConceptualDesignCaseStudy_Open(workingFile) : _baseApp.ConceptualDesignCaseStudy_Add(workingFile, string.Empty)))
			{
				if (_baseApp != null)
				{
					_baseApp.Visible = true;
				}
				_baseApp = null;
				return false;
			}
			object energyAnalyzerIDispatch = _baseApp.EnergyAnalyzerIDispatch;
			if (energyAnalyzerIDispatch == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			_simCase = energyAnalyzerIDispatch as _ConceptualDesignCaseStudy;
			if (_simCase == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			_backDoor = _simCase as BackDoor;
			if (_backDoor == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			ActivateEnergyAnalyzer(visible: true);
			return true;
		}

		private bool IsHchFileReady(string hchFile)
		{
			if (File.Exists(hchFile))
			{
				FileInfo fileInfo = new FileInfo(hchFile);
				if (fileInfo.IsReadOnly)
				{
					System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(fileInfo.Name + " is read only", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK);
					return false;
				}
				return !fileInfo.IsReadOnly;
			}
			return false;
		}

					_tmpStart = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}/Viewable.500.{1}", "Viewable.500.(FeatureManager)", num);
				}
			}
		}

		private string CreateProject(int projectIndex, string projectName, BackDoor backDoor)
		{
			string arg = "HIP1";
			int result;
			if (projectIndex == -1)
			{
				int num = 0;
				do
				{
					num++;
					arg = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "HIP{0}", num);
					projectIndex = (int)(dynamic)_simCase.HIProjectName_Exists(arg);
				}
				while (projectIndex != -1);
				arg = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "HIP{0}", num);
				projectIndex = (int)(dynamic)_simCase.NumberOfProjects_Get();
			}
			else if (int.TryParse(new Regex("((\\d+)\\s*)$").Match(projectName).Groups[1].Value, out result))
			{
				projectIndex = (((int)(dynamic)_simCase.NumberOfProjects_Get() >= result) ? ((int)(dynamic)_simCase.NumberOfProjects_Get()) : (result - 1));
			}
			string message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}:CreateFeature HI Project", "Viewable.500.(FeatureManager)");
			backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage(message);
			message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/Viewable.500.{1}", "Viewable.500.(FeatureManager)", projectIndex);
			string moniker = message + ":Viewable.299";
			string objectMoniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.{2}({1})", message, arg, projectIndex);
			backDoor.SendAttachObject2(moniker, objectMoniker);
			string moniker2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}/Viewable.299({1}):ExtraData.210.1", message, arg);
			InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)moniker2);
			if ((internalVariableWrapper != null && (dynamic)internalVariableWrapper.Variable != null) && internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable)
			{
				internalTextVariable.Value = projectName;
			}
			return message;
		}

		private static string GenerateHIProjectName(string simName, string projectKey)
		{
			Regex regex = new Regex("((\\d+)\\s*)$");
			int num = ((string.IsNullOrEmpty(projectKey) || !regex.IsMatch(projectKey)) ? 1 : ((!regex.IsMatch(projectKey)) ? 1 : int.Parse(regex.Match(projectKey).Groups[2].Value)));
			if (string.Compare(simName, "hysys", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				if (num != 1)
				{
					return string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0} {1}", "HYSYS_Import", regex.Match(projectKey).Groups[2].Value);
				}
				return "HYSYS_Import";
			}
			if (string.Compare(simName, "aspenplus", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) == 0)
			{
				if (num != 1)
				{
					return string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0} {1}", "APLUS_Import", regex.Match(projectKey).Groups[2].Value);
				}
				return "APLUS_Import";
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void RemoveCurrentProject(object projectKey)
		{
			try
			{
				if (RemoveProjectFromHXNet(projectKey) == 1 && _projectNameMapping.ContainsKey((string)projectKey))
				{
					_projectNameMapping.Remove((string)projectKey);
					string key = string.Format("{0}{1}", (string)projectKey, "IsFirstScenarioCreated");
					if (_projectNameMapping.ContainsKey(key))
					{
						_projectNameMapping.Remove(key);
					}
				}
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).RemoveCachedValues((string)projectKey);
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IDataResolver>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).ClearProjectKeys((string)projectKey);
			}
			catch (SystemException ex)
			{
				_ = ex.Message;
			}
		}

		public void RemoveClosedFile(string filepath)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(filepath))
			{
				return;
			}
			Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument caseDocument = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			if (caseDocument != null)
			{
				IDataResolver dataResolver = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IDataResolver>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
				if (dataResolver != null)
				{
					string filePath = Path.ChangeExtension(filepath, GetXmlExtension(filepath));
					caseDocument.RemoveClosedFile(filePath);
					dataResolver.RemoveClosedFile(filePath);
				}
			}
		}

		private int RemoveProjectFromHXNet(object projectKey)
		{
			int result = 0;
			string text = "";
			if (_projectNameMapping.ContainsKey((string)projectKey))
			{
				text = _projectNameMapping[(string)projectKey];
				try
				{
					string arg = "Viewable.500.(FeatureManager)/Viewable.600.(Heat Integration Manager)";
					string message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}:ExtraData.401", arg);
					IBackDoorMessage backDoorMessage = BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Text, _backDoor);
					backDoorMessage.Send(message, "HI Project");
					string message2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}:ExtraData.501", arg);
					backDoorMessage.Send(message2, text);
					string message3 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}:DeleteFeature", arg);
					_backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage(message3);
					result = 1;
				}
				catch (COMException)
				{
				}
				return result;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void SetEMATCorrectly(object projectKey, bool isReloading)
		{
			if (_backDoor == null || _simCase == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			BackDoor backDoor = _backDoor;
			int num = 0;
			string text = string.Empty;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			empty = ((_valueUnit == ValueUnitEnum.HYSYSSimulator) ? "hysys" : "aspenplus");

			{
				return;
			}

			{
				_hysysHIProjectName = GenerateHIProjectName(empty, projectKey as string);
				num = (int)(dynamic)_simCase.HIProjectName_Exists(_hysysHIProjectName);

				{
					return;
				}
				text = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}/Viewable.500.{1}", "Viewable.500.(FeatureManager)", num);
			}
			double result = _globalDeltaMinTemperature;
			Dictionary<string, string> value = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetValue(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Constraint_ExchangerMinApproachTemperature, projectKey.ToString()));
			if (value != null && value.Count() > 0)
			{
				double.TryParse(value.First().Value, out result);
			}
			double num2 = 2.0;
			string moniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DesignViewerTemplate + ":ExtraData.596", text, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName, num);
			InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker);
			if ((internalVariableWrapper != null && (dynamic)internalVariableWrapper.Variable != null) && internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalRealVariable { Value: >0.0 } internalRealVariable)
			{

			}
			string moniker2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DesignViewerTemplate + "/HIDesignViewerDesign.200.1:DeltaTemp.608", text, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName, num);
			if (_projectNameMapping.ContainsKey(projectKey.ToString()) && num2 == 1.0)
			{

			if ((internalVariableWrapper2 != null && (dynamic)internalVariableWrapper2.Variable != null) && internalVariableWrapper2.Variable is InternalRealVariable { Value: >0.0 } internalRealVariable2)
			{
