			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Constraint_ExchangerMinApproachTemperature, projectKey.ToString()), result);
		}

		private IList<double> FillUtilityNamesAndReorderDTMinValues(IList<double> deltaTValuesFromHysys)
		{
			if (deltaTValuesFromHysys == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			double[] array = new double[deltaTValuesFromHysys.Count];
			Array utilityNames = null;
			if (GetUtilityNames(out utilityNames) && utilityNames != null)
			{
				_namesOfUtilityFromEnergyAnalyzer = (IList<string>)utilityNames;
				if (deltaTValuesFromHysys.Count == 1 && deltaTValuesFromHysys[0] <= 0.0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				Func<string, double> func = delegate(string utilityName)
				{
					double result = -1.0;
					for (int i = 0; i < _namesOfUtility.Count; i++)
					{
						if (string.Compare(utilityName, _namesOfUtility[i]) == 0)
						{
							if (i >= deltaTValuesFromHysys.Count)
							{
								return -1.0;
							}
							return deltaTValuesFromHysys.ElementAt(i);
						}
					}
					return result;
				};
				for (int num = 0; num < deltaTValuesFromHysys.Count && num < _namesOfUtilityFromEnergyAnalyzer.Count; num++)
				{
					array[num] = func(_namesOfUtilityFromEnergyAnalyzer[num]);
				}
				return array;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void TransferDataOfSubflowSheets(BackDoor backDoor, string simName, string tmp1)
		{
			if (_subflowSheets == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string moniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}:ExtraData.570", EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor.BuildPrefixMoniker(tmp1, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName));
			InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)moniker);
			if (!((internalVariableWrapper != null && (dynamic)internalVariableWrapper.Variable != null) ? true : false) || !(internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable))
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string subflowSheet in _subflowSheets)
			{
				internalTextVariable.Value = subflowSheet;
			}
		}

		private bool GetUtilityNames(out Array utilityNames)
		{
			utilityNames = null;
			if (_backDoor == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			string moniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}:Name.660.[]", EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor.BuildPrefixMoniker(_tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName));
			try
			{
				InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker);
				if (internalVariableWrapper == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!(internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalTextFlexVariable internalTextFlexVariable))
				{
					return false;
				}
				utilityNames = internalTextFlexVariable.Values as Array;
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private int GetCurrentScenarioIndex(string currentName)
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (_backDoor == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			try
			{
				lock (locker)
				{
					string message = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}:ExtraData.258", EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor.BuildPrefixMoniker(_tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, null), BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Summary_ScenarioName);
					BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Text, _backDoor).Send(message, currentName);
					string message2 = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}:ExtraData.255", EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor.BuildPrefixMoniker(_tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, null), BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Summary_ScenarioIndex);
					return (int)(double)BackDoorVariableMessageFactory.Instance.CreateBackDoorMessage(BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real, _backDoor).GetData(message2);
				}
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
				return 0;
			}
		}

		private int GetBaseScenarioIndex()
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (_backDoor == null)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			string moniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}:ExtraData.205", EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor.BuildPrefixMoniker(_tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, null));
			try
			{
				InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker);
				if (internalVariableWrapper == null)
				{
					return 0;
				}
				if (!(internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable))
				{
					return 0;
				}
				return (int)internalRealVariable.Value;
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
				return 0;
			}
		}

		public bool SetMinimumDeltaT(double differMinTemperature)
		{
			try
			{
				new DataExtractorForSummary(_backDoor, _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName).SendBackDoorMessage(":DeltaTemp.1", differMinTemperature, BackDoorVariableMessageTypeEnum.Real);
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public bool SetUtilMinDeltaTs(IList<double> utilityMinTempratureDiffer)
		{
			if (utilityMinTempratureDiffer == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			EnergyAnalyzerBackDoorDXWrapper energyAnalyzerBackDoorDXWrapper = EnergyAnalyzerBackDoorDXWrapper.CreateInstance(_backDoor, _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName);
			if (energyAnalyzerBackDoorDXWrapper == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			energyAnalyzerBackDoorDXWrapper.SendCommand(new BackDoorMessage
			{
				MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Utility_DeltaTemperatures,
				State = utilityMinTempratureDiffer
			});
			return true;
		}

		public bool AmIRetrofitReady()
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			if (_backDoor == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			DataExtractorForSummary dataExtractorForSummary = new DataExtractorForSummary(_backDoor, _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName);
			if (dataExtractorForSummary == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (_simCase == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			try
			{
				if ((int)(dynamic)_simCase.HIProjectName_Exists(_hysysHIProjectName) < 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (HasUtilExchWarnings(GetCurProject()))
				{
					return false;
				}
				num = (double)dataExtractorForSummary.ExtractData(BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.CheckRetrofitReady);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (num != 1.0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public bool HasUtilExchWarnings(string projKey)
		{
			bool result = false;
			UtilityView utilityView = null;
			IEnumerable<UtilityInformationView> enumerable = null;
			UtilityInformationView utilityInformationView = null;
			UtilityInformationView utilityInformationView2 = null;
			IDataResolver dataResolver = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IDataResolver>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			if (dataResolver != null)
			{
				utilityView = dataResolver.ResolveData<UtilityView>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_UtilityView, projKey));
			}
			if (utilityView != null && utilityView.UtilityItems != null && utilityView.UtilityItems.Count > 0)
			{
				enumerable = utilityView.UtilityItems.Except(new UtilityInformationView[2] { utilityInformationView2, utilityInformationView });
				utilityInformationView = utilityView.UtilityItems.FirstOrDefault((UtilityInformationView item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
				utilityInformationView2 = utilityView.UtilityItems.LastOrDefault((UtilityInformationView item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
				IEnumerable<SurfaceAreanBaseView> enumerable2 = utilityView.UtilityItems.SelectMany((UtilityInformationView item) => item.HeatExchangers);
				if (enumerable2 != null && enumerable != null)
				{
					bool num = enumerable2.Any((SurfaceAreanBaseView item) => item.HXStatus != "1");
					bool flag = enumerable.Any((UtilityInformationView item) => item.UtilityStatus == "Insufficient") || enumerable.Any((UtilityInformationView item) => item.UtilityStatus == "Cross pinch");
					if (num || flag)
					{
						result = true;
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private bool ActivateEnergyAnalyzer(bool visible)
		{
			try
			{
				if (visible && _simCase != null)
				{
					_simCase.Activate();
					_baseApp.Visible = false;
				}
			}
			catch (COMException)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void SaveHch()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastOpenedCaseFile))
			{
				if (_simCase != null)
				{
					_simCase.Save();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				SaveAsHch(GetWorkingFile(_lastOpenedCaseFile));
			}
		}

		public void SaveAsHch(string hchPath)
		{
			try
			{
				if (_simCase != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(hchPath))
				{
					_simCase.SaveAs(hchPath);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		public void AfterDocumentSave()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastOpenedCaseFile) || !_workingFiles.ContainsKey(_lastOpenedCaseFile))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = Path.ChangeExtension(_lastOpenedCaseFile, "xml");
			string destFileName = Path.ChangeExtension(_workingFiles[_lastOpenedCaseFile], "xml");
			string text2 = Path.ChangeExtension(_workingFiles[_lastOpenedCaseFile], "oxml");
			try
			{
				File.Copy(text, destFileName, overwrite: true);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			if (File.Exists(text2))
			{
				try
				{
					File.Copy(text2, text, overwrite: true);
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			try
			{
				File.Delete(text);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		public string CurrentWorkingFolder(string casename)
		{
			if (_workingFiles.ContainsKey(casename))
			{
				return Path.GetDirectoryName(_workingFiles[casename]);
			}
			return Path.GetDirectoryName(casename);
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(isDisposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		private void Dispose(bool isDisposing)
		{
			if (isDisposing)
			{
				DestroyStaticObjects();
			}
		}

		public void DestroyStaticObjects()
		{
			if (_baseApp != null)
			{
				_baseApp.Quit();
				_baseApp = null;
			}
			if (_simCase != null)
			{
				Marshal.FinalReleaseComObject(_simCase);
				Marshal.FinalReleaseComObject(_backDoor);
				_simCase = null;
				_backDoor = null;
			}
			_projectNameMapping.Clear();
			EACFormFactory.RemoveAllProjectsOnClosing();
			_lastOpenedCaseFile = string.Empty;
			_currentCaseRestoreFile = string.Empty;
		}

		private void ProceedWithStream(OperationEnum operation = OperationEnum.UnKnow, object nodeKey = null)
		{
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = (from tmpItem in _outSideCommand
				where tmpItem.MessageType == BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.HeatExchangerCost_Models
				select from item in (Collection<IList<string>>)tmpItem.State
					select item[0]).SelectMany((IEnumerable<string> item) => item);
			EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor extrator = new DataExtractorForProjectSetup(_backDoor, _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName);
			new DataProcessForProjectSetup(base.Container, extrator).WorkflowProcess(new object[3] { operation, enumerable, nodeKey });
			EnergyAnalyzerDataExtractor extrator2 = new DataExtractorForCurves(_backDoor, _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName);
			new DataProcessForCurves(base.Container, extrator2).WorkflowProcess(new object[3] { operation, enumerable, nodeKey });
		}

		private void PassSimulNameToHXNet(string simulName, string projName, int projNo)
		{
			if (_backDoor != null)
			{
				string arg = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}/Viewable.500.{1}", "Viewable.500.(FeatureManager)", projNo);
				string moniker = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}/HIDesignViewerProject.200.{2}({1}):ExtraData.308", arg, projName, projNo);
				InternalVariableWrapper internalVariableWrapper = _backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)moniker);
				if ((internalVariableWrapper != null && (dynamic)internalVariableWrapper.Variable != null) && internalVariableWrapper.Variable is InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(simulName))
				{
					internalTextVariable.Value = simulName;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool FirstTimeEACaseRestore(string filePathFromSimulation)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if ((!IsEnginAlive && string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentCaseRestoreFile)) || string.Compare(_currentCaseRestoreFile, filePathFromSimulation, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) != 0)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void SubscribeEvent(IUnityContainer container = null)
		{
			if (base.Container == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_subscribeToken != null)
			{
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeSelectionEvent>().Unsubscribe(_subscribeToken);
			}
			_subscribeToken = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeSelectionEvent>().Subscribe(delegate(UITreeNodeSelectionArgs e)
			{
				string retrofitIndex = "-1";
				object[] array = e.State as object[];
				int retType = 0;
				int maxDes = 5;
				OperationEnum result;
				if (array.Length >= 2)
				{
					if (!Enum.TryParse<OperationEnum>(array[1].ToString(), out result))
					{
						result = OperationEnum.UnKnow;
					}
					if (array.Length > 2)
					{
						retrofitIndex = array[2].ToString();
					}
				}
				else
				{
					result = OperationEnum.UnKnow;
				}
				if (e.NodeKey.IndexOf("Modify Heat Exchangers") > 0 || e.NodeKey.IndexOf("Add New") > 0 || e.NodeKey.IndexOf("Relocate") > 0)
				{
					bool ignoreDeleteInHXNet = array.Length >= 4 && array[3] is bool && (bool)array[3];
					EventWaitHandle waitHandle = ((array.Length >= 5) ? (array[4] as EventWaitHandle) : null);
					ModifyXchyangerDataExtractionProcess(e.NodeKey, result, retrofitIndex, ignoreDeleteInHXNet, waitHandle);
				}
				else if (new Regex("Scenario\\(\\d+\\)$").IsMatch(e.NodeKey))
				{
					if (array.Length >= 4)
					{
						retType = Convert.ToInt32(array[2]);
						maxDes = Convert.ToInt32(array[3]);
					}
					SummaryDataExtractionProcess(e.NodeKey, result, retType, maxDes);
				}
				else if (e.NodeKey.IndexOf("Saving Potentials") >= 0)
				{
					SavingPotentailsDataExtractionProcess(result, e.NodeKey);
				}
				else if (e.NodeKey.Split('.').Length == 1)
				{
					if (result == OperationEnum.Delete)
					{
						RemoveCurrentProject(e.NodeKey);
					}
				}
				else if (e.NodeKey.IndexOf("Setup") >= 0)
				{
					ProceedWithStream(result, e.NodeKey);
				}
			}, ThreadOption.BackgroundThread, keepSubscriberReferenceAlive: true, delegate(UITreeNodeSelectionArgs arg)
			{
				if (arg.State == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				return arg.State is object[] array && (bool)array[0];
			});
		}

		private void SubscriptCaseRestoreEvent(IUnityContainer container)
		{
			if (base.Container == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			WorkflowControlStateEvent workflowControlStateEvent = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<WorkflowControlStateEvent>();
			if (_caseRestoreToken != null)
			{
				workflowControlStateEvent.Unsubscribe(_caseRestoreToken);
			}
			_caseRestoreToken = workflowControlStateEvent.Subscribe(delegate(WorkflowControlStateArgs arg)
			{
				if (arg.State is object[] array && array.Length >= 3 && array[0] != null && array[1] != null && array[2] != null)
				{
					SubscribeEvent(base.Container);
					bool flag = true;
					if (FirstTimeEACaseRestore(array[0].ToString()))
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					bool flag2 = array.Length >= 5 && array[4] is bool && (bool)array[4];
					if ((string.IsNullOrEmpty(_currentCaseRestoreFile) || !flag) && !flag2)
					{
						_currentCaseRestoreFile = array[0].ToString();
					}
					if (EnergyAnalyzerApplication.IsInstalled && !flag2)
					{
						string workingFile = GetWorkingFile(array[0].ToString());
						if (!File.Exists(Path.ChangeExtension(workingFile, "xml")) && !IsEnginAlive)
						{
							IDataResolver dataResolver = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IDataResolver>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
							if (dataResolver != null && !flag)
							{
								dataResolver.Load(null);
							}
							UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateTreeEvent>().Publish(new UpdateTreeEventArgs
							{
								Action = OperationEnum.CreateDefault,
								State = ThreadOption.BackgroundThread
							});
							return;
						}
						if (File.Exists(workingFile) && !new FileInfo(workingFile).IsReadOnly && string.Compare(array[0].ToString(), _lastOpenedCaseFile, ignoreCase: true) != 0)
						{
							LaunchEnergyAnalyzer(array[1].ToString());
							if (!OpenCaseFile(array[0].ToString(), out var _))
							{
								return;
							}
							_valueUnit = ((string.Compare(array[1].ToString(), "hysys") == 0) ? ValueUnitEnum.HYSYSSimulator : ValueUnitEnum.Simulator);
							string simulName = array[1].ToString();
							_hysysHIProjectName = GenerateHIProjectName(array[1].ToString(), ParseProjectKeyFromFile(array[0].ToString()));
							int num = (int)(dynamic)_simCase.HIProjectName_Exists(_hysysHIProjectName);
							_tmpStart = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}/Viewable.500.{1}", "Viewable.500.(FeatureManager)", num);
							if (num >= 0 && _backDoor != null)
							{
								PassSimulNameToHXNet(simulName, _hysysHIProjectName, num);
							}
							_lastOpenedCaseFile = array[0].ToString();
							UnityContainerExtensions.RegisterInstance<Action<IList<BackDoorMessage>>>(base.Container, "SendingVariableAction", (Action<IList<BackDoorMessage>>)SendCommand);
						}
					}
					bool flag3 = array.Length > 3 && array[3] is bool && (bool)array[3];
					if (_workingFiles.ContainsKey(array[0].ToString()))
					{
						string text = Path.ChangeExtension(_workingFiles[array[0].ToString()], "xml");
						string text2 = Path.ChangeExtension(array[0].ToString(), "xml");
						string text3 = Path.ChangeExtension(text, "oxml");
						string path = Path.ChangeExtension(array[0].ToString(), "hch");
						if (!(flag3 && File.Exists(path) && File.Exists(text2) && flag))
						{
							bool flag4 = false;
							if (string.Compare(Path.GetExtension(array[0].ToString()), ".apwz", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) == 0)
							{
								flag4 = true;
							}
							try
							{
								if (!flag4)
								{
									File.Copy(text, text2, overwrite: true);
								}
							}
							catch (Exception)
							{
							}
							if (!flag4)
							{
								UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).Restore(base.Container, text2, flag3, flag);
							}
							else
							{
								UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).Restore(base.Container, Path.ChangeExtension(GetWorkingFile(array[0].ToString()), "xml"), flag3, flag);
							}
							if (File.Exists(text3))
							{
								try
								{
									if (!flag4)
									{
										File.Copy(text3, text2, overwrite: true);
									}
									return;
								}
								catch (Exception)
								{
									return;
								}
							}
							try
							{
								File.Delete(text2);
							}
							catch (Exception)
							{
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).Restore(base.Container, Path.ChangeExtension(GetWorkingFile(array[0].ToString()), "xml"), flag3, flag);
					}
				}
			}, ThreadOption.PublisherThread, keepSubscriberReferenceAlive: true, (WorkflowControlStateArgs filter) => filter.ControlType == WorkflowControl.SimulatorCase);
		}

		private string ParseProjectKeyFromFile(string filePath)
		{
			string result = string.Empty;
			filePath = Path.ChangeExtension(GetWorkingFile(filePath), "xml");
			try
			{
				XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
				xmlDocument.Load(filePath);
				XmlNode xmlNode = xmlDocument.DocumentElement.SelectSingleNode(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.NavigationTree).Replace('.', '/'));
				if (xmlNode != null)
				{
					string xml = Encoding.UTF8.GetString(Convert.FromBase64String(xmlNode.FirstChild.Value));
					xmlDocument.LoadXml(xml);
					XmlNode xmlNode2 = xmlDocument.DocumentElement.SelectSingleNode("EACTreeNode/EACTreeNode[@IsSelected='True']/@Key") ?? xmlDocument.DocumentElement.SelectSingleNode("EACTreeNode/EACTreeNode/EACTreeNode[@IsSelected='True']/@Key");
					if (xmlNode2 != null)
					{
						result = xmlNode2.Value.Split('.')[0];
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void ModifyXchyangerDataExtractionProcess(string heatChangerNodeKey, OperationEnum operation = OperationEnum.UnKnow, string retrofitIndex = "-1", bool ignoreDeleteInHXNet = false, EventWaitHandle waitHandle = null)
		{
			string text = $"Scenario 1 {ParseScenarioIndexer(heatChangerNodeKey)}";
			string scenarioName = GetCurrentScenarioIndex(text) + "(" + text + ")";
			string hipProjectName = GenerateHIProjectName((_valueUnit == ValueUnitEnum.HYSYSSimulator) ? "hysys" : "aspenplus", heatChangerNodeKey.Split('.')[0]);
			WorkflowDataProcess workflowDataProcess = null;
			workflowDataProcess = ((heatChangerNodeKey.IndexOf("Modify Heat Exchangers") > 0) ? new DataProcessForModifyHeatExchanger(base.Container, new DataExtractorForModifyHeatExchanger(_backDoor, _tmpStart, hipProjectName, scenarioName)) : ((heatChangerNodeKey.IndexOf("Relocate") <= 0) ? ((DataProcessForHeatExchangerBaseOperation)new DataProcessForAddHeatExchanger(base.Container, new DataExtractorFoAddHeatExchanger(_backDoor, _tmpStart, hipProjectName, scenarioName))) : ((DataProcessForHeatExchangerBaseOperation)new DataProcessForRelocateHeatExchanger(base.Container, new DataExtractorFoRelocateHeatExchanger(_backDoor, _tmpStart, hipProjectName, scenarioName)))));
			workflowDataProcess.ValueUnit = _valueUnit;
			workflowDataProcess.WorkflowProcess(new object[6]
			{
				UnitValueFactory.Create(_valueUnit),
				operation,
				retrofitIndex,
				ignoreDeleteInHXNet,
				waitHandle,
				heatChangerNodeKey.Split('.')[0]
			});
			if (operation == OperationEnum.Add || operation == OperationEnum.Delete || operation == OperationEnum.CreateDefault || operation == OperationEnum.UnKnow)
			{
				SetDocumentDirty();
			}
		}

		public void SetDocumentDirty()
		{
			try
			{
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IApplicationWrapper>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>())?.SetSimulatorCaseDirty();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		private static string ParseScenarioIndexer(string nodeKey)
		{
			Regex regex = new Regex("(Scenario(\\(\\d+\\)))");
			if (!regex.IsMatch(nodeKey))
			{
				return "1";
			}
			return new Regex("(\\d+)").Match(regex.Match(nodeKey).Groups[1].Value).Groups[1].Value;
		}

		private void SummaryDataExtractionProcess(string scenarioNodeKey, OperationEnum operation = OperationEnum.UnKnow, int retType = 0, int maxDes = 5)
		{
			string projectKey = scenarioNodeKey.Split('.')[0];
			string hipProjectName = GenerateHIProjectName((_valueUnit == ValueUnitEnum.HYSYSSimulator) ? "hysys" : "aspenplus", projectKey);
			Dictionary<string, string> value = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetValue(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Constraint_ExchangerMinApproachTemperature, projectKey));
			if (value != null && value.Count > 1)
			{
				SetEMATCorrectly(projectKey, isReloading: true);
			}
			string text = ParseScenarioIndexer(scenarioNodeKey);
			DataExtractorForSummary dataExtractor = new DataExtractorForSummary(_backDoor, _tmpStart, hipProjectName, _scenarioName);
			WorkflowDataProcess workflowDataProcess = new DataProcessForSummary(base.Container, dataExtractor)
			{
				ValueUnit = _valueUnit
			};
			string key = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.CachedData_CreatingFirstScenario, scenarioNodeKey.Split('.')[0]);
			bool flag = _projectNameMapping.ContainsKey(key);
			workflowDataProcess.WorkflowProcess(new object[7] { scenarioNodeKey, _tmpStart, flag, text, operation, retType, maxDes });
			if (flag)
			{
				_projectNameMapping.Remove(key);
			}
		}

		private void SavingPotentailsDataExtractionProcess(OperationEnum operation = OperationEnum.UnKnow, string nodeKey = null)
		{
			if (operation == OperationEnum.Restore && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(nodeKey))
			{
				_projectNameMapping[nodeKey.Split('.')[0]] = _hysysHIProjectName;
			}
			string text = GenerateHIProjectName((_valueUnit == ValueUnitEnum.HYSYSSimulator) ? "hysys" : "aspenplus", string.IsNullOrEmpty(nodeKey) ? "Project 1" : nodeKey.Split('.')[0]);
			string commandPrefix = string.Empty;
			if (_simCase != null)
			{
				int num = (int)(dynamic)_simCase.HIProjectName_Exists(text);
				commandPrefix = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "{0}/Viewable.500.{1}", "Viewable.500.(FeatureManager)", num);
			}
			new DataProcessForSavingPotentials(base.Container, new DataExtractorForSavingPotentials(_backDoor, commandPrefix, text, _scenarioName)).WorkflowProcess(new object[2] { operation, nodeKey });
		}

		public void SetBaseScenarioName()
		{
			string text = GetBaseScenarioIndex().ToString();
			_scenarioName = text + "(Scenario 1)";
		}

		private void SendCommand(IList<BackDoorMessage> messages)
		{
			if (messages == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			EnergyAnalyzerBackDoorDXWrapper backDoorWrapper = EnergyAnalyzerBackDoorDXWrapper.CreateInstance(_backDoor, _tmpStart, _hysysHIProjectName, _scenarioName);
			Parallel.ForEach(messages, delegate(BackDoorMessage message)
			{
				if (message.MessageType != BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.Unknow)
				{
					backDoorWrapper.SendCommand(message);
				}
			});
		}

		public void Snapshoot()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastOpenedCaseFile))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				string text = null;
				string path = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}\\{1}{2}1.{3}", Path.GetDirectoryName(_lastOpenedCaseFile), Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(_lastOpenedCaseFile), "_Copy", "hch");
				if (IsEnginAlive)
				{
					text = Path.Combine(CheckWriteAccess(Path.GetDirectoryName(_lastOpenedCaseFile)) ? Path.GetDirectoryName(_lastOpenedCaseFile) : Path.GetTempPath(), string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}_{1}.{2}", Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(_lastOpenedCaseFile), DateTime.Now.ToString().GetHashCode(), "hch"));
					SaveAsHch(text);
				}
				else
				{
					string workingFile = GetWorkingFile(_lastOpenedCaseFile);
					if (File.Exists(workingFile))
					{
						text = workingFile;
					}
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					return;
				}
				bool num = File.Exists(path);
				bool flag = true;
				if (num)
				{
					try
					{
						File.Delete(path);
					}
					catch
					{
						flag = false;
					}
				}
				string text2 = CreateCopy(text);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					OpenFileIndependently(text2);
				}
				if (File.Exists(text) && flag)
				{
					File.Delete(text);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		private string CreateCopy(string sourceHchFile)
		{
			string fileNameWithoutExtension = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(_lastOpenedCaseFile);
			string directoryName = Path.GetDirectoryName(sourceHchFile);
			string[] files = Directory.GetFiles(directoryName, string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}{1}*.{2}", fileNameWithoutExtension, "_Copy", "hch"));
			string text = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0}\\{1}{2}{3}.{4}", directoryName, fileNameWithoutExtension, "_Copy", files.Length + 1, "hch");
			try
			{
				File.Copy(sourceHchFile, text);
			}
			catch
			{
				text = string.Empty;
			}
			return text;
		}

		private void OpenFileIndependently(string fileName)
		{
			ShellExecute(IntPtr.Zero, "open", EnergyAnalyzerApplication.ExecutableFile, string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "-hxnet \"{0}\"", fileName), null, 1);
		}

		private bool CheckWriteAccess(string directory)
		{
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				string path = Path.Combine(directory, DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfffffff") + ".txt");
				File.Create(path).Dispose();
				result = true;
				File.Delete(path);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool IsActivated()
		{
			return _simCase != null;
		}

		[DllImport("shell32.dll")]
		public static extern int ShellExecute(IntPtr hwnd, string op, string shell, string paras, string path, int cmd);

		public override void Initialize()
		{
			//IL_0007: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0011: Expected O, but got Unknown
			UnityContainerExtensions.RegisterInstance<IEnergyAnalysis>(base.Container, (IEnergyAnalysis)this, (LifetimeManager)new ContainerControlledLifetimeManager());
			base.Initialize();
		}

		public string[] CreateRecoveryFiles(string casepath, string recoverycasepath)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			if (_workingFiles.ContainsKey(casepath))
			{
				try
				{
					if (File.Exists(_workingFiles[casepath]))
					{
						File.Copy(_workingFiles[casepath], Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "hc$"), overwrite: true);
						list.Add(Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "hc$"));
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				try
				{
					string text = Path.ChangeExtension(_workingFiles[casepath], "xml");
					if (File.Exists(text))
					{
						File.Copy(text, Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "xm$"), overwrite: true);
						list.Add(Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "xm$"));
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (File.Exists(Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hch")))
				{
					try
					{
						File.Copy(Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hch"), Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "hc$"), overwrite: true);
						list.Add(Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "hc$"));
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				if (File.Exists(Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "xml")))
				{
					try
					{
						File.Copy(Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "xml"), Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "xm$"), overwrite: true);
						list.Add(Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "xm$"));
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			return Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}

		public void DeleteRecoveryFiles(string casepath, string recoverycasepath)
		{
			try
			{
				if (File.Exists(Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "hc$")))
				{
					File.Delete(Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "hc$"));
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			try
			{
				if (File.Exists(Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "xm$")))
				{
					File.Delete(Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "xm$"));
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		public string[] RestoreRecoveryFiles(string casepath, string recoverycasepath)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string text = Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "hc$");
			string text2 = Path.ChangeExtension(recoverycasepath, "xm$");
			if (File.Exists(text))
			{
				try
				{
					File.Copy(text, Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hch"), overwrite: true);
					list.Add(Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hch"));
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				try
				{
					File.Delete(text);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			if (File.Exists(text2))
			{
				try
				{
					File.Copy(text2, Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "xml"), overwrite: true);
					list.Add(Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "xml"));
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				try
				{
					File.Delete(text2);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			return Enumerable.ToArray(list);
		}

		public void CacheTemporary()
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastOpenedCaseFile) && IsEnginAlive)
			{
				string workingFile = GetWorkingFile(_lastOpenedCaseFile);
				SaveHch();
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).Save(base.Container, Path.ChangeExtension(workingFile, GetXmlExtension(_lastOpenedCaseFile)));
			}
		}

		public void OnSave(string casepath)
		{
			if (Path.GetExtension(casepath) == ".tmp")
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastOpenedCaseFile) && IsEnginAlive)
				{
					SaveAsHch(Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hch"));
					UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).Save(base.Container, Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "xml"));
				}
				else
				{
					if (!_workingFiles.ContainsKey(casepath))
					{
						return;
					}
					string text = _workingFiles[casepath];
					string text2 = Path.ChangeExtension(text, GetXmlExtension(casepath));
					string destFileName = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hch");
					string destFileName2 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "xml");
					if (File.Exists(text))
					{
						try
						{
							File.Copy(text, destFileName, overwrite: true);
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
						}
					}
					if (File.Exists(text2))
					{
						try
						{
							File.Copy(text2, destFileName2, overwrite: true);
							return;
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							return;
						}
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).HYSYSFile = casepath;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastOpenedCaseFile) && _lastOpenedCaseFile == casepath && IsEnginAlive)
			{
				SaveHch();
				string saveToPath = (_workingFiles.ContainsKey(casepath) ? Path.ChangeExtension(_workingFiles[casepath], GetXmlExtension(casepath)) : Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, GetXmlExtension(casepath)));
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).Save(base.Container, saveToPath);
			}
			if (!_workingFiles.ContainsKey(casepath))
			{
				return;
			}
			string text3 = _workingFiles[casepath];
			string text4 = Path.ChangeExtension(text3, GetXmlExtension(casepath));
			string destFileName3 = Path.ChangeExtension(text3, "oxml");
			string destFileName4 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, GetHchExtension(casepath));
			string destFileName5 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, GetXmlExtension(casepath));
			if (File.Exists(text3))
			{
				try
				{
					File.Copy(text3, destFileName4, overwrite: true);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			if (!File.Exists(text4))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				File.Copy(text4, destFileName5, overwrite: true);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			try
			{
				File.Copy(text4, destFileName3, overwrite: true);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		public void OnSaveAs(string casepath, string newcasepath)
		{
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).HYSYSFile = newcasepath;
			IHostApplicationInfoMessenger hostApplicationInfoMessenger = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IHostApplicationInfoMessenger>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			if (hostApplicationInfoMessenger != null && hostApplicationInfoMessenger.HostApplicationType == ApplicationType.AspenHYSYS && string.Compare(Path.GetExtension(newcasepath), ".hscz", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) != 0 && string.Compare(Path.GetExtension(newcasepath), ".hsc", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) != 0 && string.Compare(Path.GetExtension(newcasepath), ".tmp", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) != 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (Path.GetExtension(newcasepath) == ".tmp")
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastOpenedCaseFile) && IsEnginAlive)
				{
					SaveAsHch(Path.ChangeExtension(newcasepath, "hch"));
					UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(base.Container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).Save(base.Container, Path.ChangeExtension(newcasepath, "xml"));
					return;
				}
				if (_workingFiles.ContainsKey(casepath))
				{
					string text = _workingFiles[casepath];
					string text2 = Path.ChangeExtension(text, GetXmlExtension(casepath));
					string destFileName = Path.ChangeExtension(newcasepath, "hch");
					string destFileName2 = Path.ChangeExtension(newcasepath, "xml");
					if (File.Exists(text))
					{
						try
						{
							File.Copy(text, destFileName, overwrite: true);
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
						}
					}
					if (File.Exists(text2))
					{
						try
						{
							File.Copy(text2, destFileName2, overwrite: true);
							return;
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							return;
						}
					}
					return;
				}
				string text3 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hch");
				string text4 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, GetXmlExtension(casepath));
				string destFileName3 = Path.ChangeExtension(newcasepath, "hch");
				string destFileName4 = Path.ChangeExtension(newcasepath, "xml");
				if (File.Exists(text3))
				{
					try
					{
						File.Copy(text3, destFileName3, overwrite: true);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				if (File.Exists(text4))
				{
					try
					{
						File.Copy(text4, destFileName4, overwrite: true);
						return;
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			if (hostApplicationInfoMessenger != null)
			{
				string currentDocumentPath = hostApplicationInfoMessenger.CurrentDocumentPath;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(currentDocumentPath) && string.Compare(Path.GetExtension(currentDocumentPath), ".apwz", StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase) == 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastOpenedCaseFile) && _lastOpenedCaseFile == currentDocumentPath && IsEnginAlive)
				{
					GetWorkingFile(newcasepath);
					_lastOpenedCaseFile = newcasepath;
					OnSave(newcasepath);
					_lastOpenedCaseFile = currentDocumentPath;
					return;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_lastOpenedCaseFile) && _lastOpenedCaseFile == casepath)
			{
				GetWorkingFile(newcasepath);
				_lastOpenedCaseFile = newcasepath;
				OnSave(newcasepath);
			}
			else if (_workingFiles.ContainsKey(casepath))
			{
				string text5 = _workingFiles[casepath];
				string text6 = Path.ChangeExtension(text5, GetXmlExtension(casepath));
				string destFileName5 = Path.ChangeExtension(newcasepath, "hch");
				string destFileName6 = Path.ChangeExtension(newcasepath, "xml");
				if (File.Exists(text5))
				{
					try
					{
						File.Copy(text5, destFileName5, overwrite: true);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				if (File.Exists(text6))
				{
					try
					{
						File.Copy(text6, destFileName6, overwrite: true);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				GetWorkingFile(newcasepath);
			}
			else
			{
				string text7 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hch");
				string text8 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, GetXmlExtension(casepath));
				string destFileName7 = Path.ChangeExtension(newcasepath, "hch");
				string destFileName8 = Path.ChangeExtension(newcasepath, "xml");
				if (File.Exists(text7))
				{
					try
					{
						File.Copy(text7, destFileName7, overwrite: true);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				if (File.Exists(text8) && checkXmlEnergyAnalyzerFile(text8))
				{
					try
					{
						File.Copy(text8, destFileName8, overwrite: true);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			OnQuit(casepath);
		}

		private bool checkXmlEnergyAnalyzerFile(string nameXmlFile)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			xmlDocument.Load(nameXmlFile);
			XmlNode xmlNode = xmlDocument.SelectSingleNode("ProjectSetup");
			if (xmlNode == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (xmlNode.SelectSingleNode("Navigation") == null || xmlNode.SelectSingleNode("EnginData") == null || xmlNode.SelectSingleNode("DataExtractionRules") == null || xmlNode.SelectSingleNode("Flowsheet") == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void OnQuit(string casepath)
		{
			if (casepath != null)
			{
				if (_workingFiles.ContainsKey(casepath))
				{
					string path = _workingFiles[casepath];
					string path2 = Path.ChangeExtension(path, GetXmlExtension(casepath));
					string path3 = Path.ChangeExtension(path, "oxml");
					try
					{
						File.Delete(path);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
					try
					{
						File.Delete(path2);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
					try
					{
						File.Delete(path3);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
					try
					{
						Directory.Delete(Path.GetDirectoryName(path));
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
					_workingFiles.Remove(casepath);
				}
				return;
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (string key in _workingFiles.Keys)
			{
				list.Add(key);
			}
			foreach (string item in list)
			{
				OnQuit(item);
			}
		}

		public void DeleteRecoveredFiles(string casepath)
		{
			try
			{
				if (File.Exists(Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hch")))
				{
					File.Delete(Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hch"));
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			try
			{
				if (File.Exists(Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "xml")))
				{
					File.Delete(Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "xml"));
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		public void CascadeBackups(string casepath, int number_to_save)
		{
			for (int num = number_to_save; num > 1; num--)
			{
				string text = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, $"hc{num - 1}");
				string text2 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, $"hc{num - 2}");
				string text3 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, $"xm{num - 1}");
				string text4 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, $"xm{num - 2}");
				if (File.Exists(text2))
				{
					try
					{
						if (File.Exists(text))
						{
							File.Delete(text);
						}
						File.Move(text2, text);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				if (File.Exists(text4))
				{
					try
					{
						if (File.Exists(text3))
						{
							File.Delete(text3);
						}
						File.Move(text4, text3);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			if (number_to_save <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			string destFileName = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hc0");
			string text5 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "hch");
			string destFileName2 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "xm0");
			string text6 = Path.ChangeExtension(casepath, "xml");
			if (File.Exists(text5))
			{
				try
				{
					File.Copy(text5, destFileName, overwrite: true);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			if (File.Exists(text6))
			{
				try
				{
					File.Copy(text6, destFileName2, overwrite: true);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
namespace Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.Integration.Dashboard
{
	public class GenerateHelper
	{
		private const string SCENARIO = "Scenario";

		internal const string RETROFITINDEX = "RetrofitIndex";

		private readonly IEnergyAnalysisAgent _agent;

		private readonly StartEnergyAnalysis _eac;

		private readonly IUnityContainer _container;

		private readonly IHostApplicationInfoMessenger _appInfo;

		private readonly DataSourceChangedEvent _dataSourceChangeEvent;

		private string _nodeKey;

		private string _addAreaNodeKey;

		private string _addHXNodeKey;

		private string _relocateHXNodeKey;

		private SubscriptionToken _updateRelocateHXNodeKeyToken;

		private SubscriptionToken _updateAddHXNodeKeyToken;

		private string[] _createdScenarioKeys = new string[3];

		private SubscriptionToken _addAreaSubscriptToken;

		private SubscriptionToken _addHXSubscriptToken;

		private SubscriptionToken _relocateHXSubscriptToken;

		public static bool bCanceled;

		public static int nGenerateStep;

		private SubscriptionToken _addAreaProcessSubscriptionToken;

		private SubscriptionToken _addHeatExchangerProcessSubscriptionToken;

		private SubscriptionToken _relocateHeatExchangerProcessSubscriptionToken;

		private int myNumberAddHXDesigns = 5;

		private int myNumberRelocateHXDesigns = 5;

		public int NumberAddHXDesigns
		{
			get
			{
				return myNumberAddHXDesigns;
			}
			set
			{
				myNumberAddHXDesigns = value;
			}
		}

		public int NumberRelocateHXDesigns
		{
			get
			{
				return myNumberRelocateHXDesigns;
			}
			set
			{
				myNumberRelocateHXDesigns = value;
			}
		}

		public GenerateHelper(IEnergyAnalysisAgent agent, StartEnergyAnalysis eac, IUnityContainer container, IHostApplicationInfoMessenger appInfo)
		{
			_agent = agent;
			_eac = eac;
			_container = container;
			_appInfo = appInfo;
			_dataSourceChangeEvent = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>();
		}

		private void ReportStateChanged(EAGenerateOperation operation, EAGenerateState state)
		{
			EAGenerateResult eAGenerateResult = new EAGenerateResult();
			if (state == EAGenerateState.Succeeded)
			{
				switch (operation)
				{
				case EAGenerateOperation.Modify:
					if (_addAreaNodeKey != null && _nodeKey != null)
					{
						eAGenerateResult.DesignChange = _addAreaNodeKey;
						eAGenerateResult.NavigationPath = _addAreaNodeKey;
					}
					break;
				case EAGenerateOperation.Add:
					if (_addHXNodeKey != null && _nodeKey != null)
					{
						eAGenerateResult.DesignChange = _addHXNodeKey.Substring(_nodeKey.Length + 1);
						eAGenerateResult.NavigationPath = _addHXNodeKey;
					}
					break;
				case EAGenerateOperation.Relocate:
					if (_relocateHXNodeKey != null && _nodeKey != null)
					{
						eAGenerateResult.DesignChange = _relocateHXNodeKey.Substring(_nodeKey.Length + 1);
						eAGenerateResult.NavigationPath = _relocateHXNodeKey;
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			ReportStateChanged(operation, state, string.IsNullOrEmpty(eAGenerateResult.DesignChange) ? null : eAGenerateResult);
		}

		private void ReportStateChanged(EAGenerateOperation operation, EAGenerateState state, EAGenerateResult result)
		{
			EACFormFactory.EACTreeNodeRibbonInfo eACRibbonInfo = EACFormFactory.GetEACRibbonInfo("Project 1");
			if (eACRibbonInfo != null && (state == EAGenerateState.Failed || state == EAGenerateState.Succeeded))
			{
				int scenarioCount = eACRibbonInfo.ScenarioCount + 1;
				eACRibbonInfo.ScenarioCount = scenarioCount;
			}
			_agent.OnEAGenerateStateReported(new EAGenerateStateEventArgs(operation, state, result));
		}

		public void DoGenerate(int nAddHX, int nRelocateHX)
		{
			try
			{
				myNumberAddHXDesigns = nAddHX;
				myNumberRelocateHXDesigns = nRelocateHX;
				DeleteCreatedScenarios();
				ReportStateChanged(EAGenerateOperation.Modify, EAGenerateState.Running);
				_addAreaSubscriptToken = _dataSourceChangeEvent.Subscribe(OnModifyCallback, ThreadOption.UIThread, keepSubscriberReferenceAlive: true, (DataSourceChangedEventArgs filter) => filter.SourceType == DataSourceTypeEnum.UpdateModifyHeatExchanger);
				StartAddAreaProcess();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
			IEnergyAnalysisMessenger energyAnalysisMessenger = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisMessenger>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			if (energyAnalysisMessenger != null)
			{
				energyAnalysisMessenger.IsGenerationDone = true;
			}
		}

		private static string ParseScenarioKey(string nodeKey)
		{
			string text = "1";
			Regex regex = new Regex("(Scenario(\\(\\d+\\)))");
			if (regex.IsMatch(nodeKey))
			{
				text = new Regex("(\\d+)").Match(regex.Match(nodeKey).Groups[1].Value).Groups[1].Value;
			}
			return text + " Scenario";
		}

		private static string ParseProjectKey(string nodeKey)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(nodeKey))
			{
				return nodeKey.Split('.')[0];
			}
			return "Project 1";
		}

		private Dictionary<string, object> GetResolvedData(string moniker)
		{
			IDataResolver dataResolver = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IDataResolver>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			object obj = null;
			if (dataResolver != null)
			{
				obj = dataResolver.ResolveData(moniker);
			}
			if (obj != null && obj is Dictionary<string, object>)
			{
				return obj as Dictionary<string, object>;
			}
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (obj != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add(moniker, obj);
			}
			return dictionary;
		}

		private void CollectNewAreaData(string nodeKey, bool canUpdate)
		{
			EAGenerateState state = (canUpdate ? EAGenerateState.Succeeded : EAGenerateState.Clear);
			EAGenerateResult eAGenerateResult = new EAGenerateResult
			{
				DesignChange = "NULL"
			};
			string text = ParseScenarioKey(nodeKey);
			string projectKey = ParseProjectKey(nodeKey);
			string text2 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Scenario, projectKey, text);
			Dictionary<string, object> resolvedData = GetResolvedData(text2);
			SummaryUtilities summaryUtilities = (resolvedData.ContainsKey(text2) ? (resolvedData[text2] as SummaryUtilities) : null) ?? new SummaryUtilities();
			if (canUpdate && summaryUtilities != null && summaryUtilities.Count >= 3)
			{
				SummaryUtilityInformationView summaryUtilityInformationView = summaryUtilities.ElementAt(1);
				SummaryUtilityInformationView summaryUtilityInformationView2 = summaryUtilities.ElementAt(0);
				string[] array = summaryUtilityInformationView.Name.Split('–');
				string energyCost = ((Convert.ToDouble(summaryUtilityInformationView2.Cost, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) - Convert.ToDouble(summaryUtilityInformationView.Cost, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture)) / 1000000.0).ToString("N03");
				string moniker = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_ModifyHeatExchanger, projectKey, text, "1");
				SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection source = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IDataResolver>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).ResolveData<SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection>(moniker);
				string moniker2 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Payback_ModifyHeatExchanger, projectKey, text.ToString(), "1");
				double num = Math.Round(UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IDataResolver>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).ResolveData<double>(moniker2), 4);
				eAGenerateResult.DesignChange = ((array.Length < 2) ? string.Empty : array[1]);
				eAGenerateResult.EnergySaving = summaryUtilityInformationView.Reduction;
				eAGenerateResult.EnergyCost = energyCost;
				eAGenerateResult.CapitalCost = source.Sum((SurfaceAreanEntityView item) => item.ExtraCapitalCost / 1000000.0).ToString("N03");
				eAGenerateResult.NewArea = source.Sum((SurfaceAreanEntityView item) => item.NewArea).ToString();
				eAGenerateResult.Payback = num.ToString();
				eAGenerateResult.NavigationPath = _addAreaNodeKey;
			}
			ReportStateChanged(EAGenerateOperation.Modify, state, eAGenerateResult);
		}

		private void CollectNewHeatExchangerData(string nodeKey, bool canUpdate)
		{
			EAGenerateState state = (canUpdate ? EAGenerateState.Succeeded : EAGenerateState.Clear);
			EAGenerateResult eAGenerateResult = new EAGenerateResult
			{
				DesignChange = "NULL"
			};
			string scenarioKey = ParseScenarioKey(nodeKey);
			string projectKey = ParseProjectKey(nodeKey);
			string text = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_AddHeatExchanger, projectKey, scenarioKey, "1");
			Dictionary<string, object> resolvedData = GetResolvedData(text);
			if (canUpdate && resolvedData != null && resolvedData.Count > 0)
			{
				IOrderedEnumerable<NewHeatExchangerView> source = (((resolvedData.ContainsKey(text) ? resolvedData[text] : null) as NewHeatExchangerViewCollection) ?? new NewHeatExchangerViewCollection()).OrderBy((NewHeatExchangerView item) => double.Parse(item.Payback));
				NewHeatExchangerView newHX = source.ElementAt(0);
				string text2 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Scenario, projectKey, scenarioKey);
				Dictionary<string, object> resolvedData2 = GetResolvedData(text2);
				SummaryUtilityInformationView summaryUtilityInformationView = (((resolvedData2.ContainsKey(text2) ? resolvedData2[text2] : null) as SummaryUtilities) ?? new SummaryUtilities()).FirstOrDefault((SummaryUtilityInformationView item) => item.DesignIndex == newHX.DesignIndex);
				string key = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_AddHeatExchanger_Item, projectKey, scenarioKey, null, newHX.DesignIndex.ToString());
				SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection source2 = ((resolvedData.ContainsKey(key) ? resolvedData[key] : null) as SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection) ?? new SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection();
				string value = new Regex("–\\s*(\\w+)\\b").Match(summaryUtilityInformationView.Name ?? string.Empty).Groups[1].Value;
				string name = source2.FirstOrDefault((SurfaceAreanEntityView item) => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.HeatExchangerType)).Name;
				eAGenerateResult.DesignChange = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0} {1}", value, name);
				eAGenerateResult.EnergySaving = summaryUtilityInformationView.Reduction;
				eAGenerateResult.EnergyCost = (Convert.ToDouble(newHX.EnergySavingPerTime, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) / 1000000.0).ToString("N03");
				eAGenerateResult.CapitalCost = (Convert.ToDouble(newHX.TotalCapitalCost, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) / 1000000.0).ToString("N03");
				eAGenerateResult.NewArea = newHX.Area;
				eAGenerateResult.Payback = newHX.Payback;
				eAGenerateResult.NavigationPath = _addHXNodeKey;
			}
			ReportStateChanged(EAGenerateOperation.Add, state, eAGenerateResult);
		}

		private void CollectRelocatedHeatExchangerData(string nodeKey, bool canUpdate)
		{
			EAGenerateState state = (canUpdate ? EAGenerateState.Succeeded : EAGenerateState.Clear);
			EAGenerateResult eAGenerateResult = new EAGenerateResult
			{
				DesignChange = "NULL"
			};
			string text = ParseScenarioKey(nodeKey);
			string projectKey = ParseProjectKey(nodeKey);
			string text2 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_RelocateHeatExchanger, projectKey, text, "1");
			Dictionary<string, object> resolvedData = GetResolvedData(text2);
			if (canUpdate && resolvedData != null && resolvedData.Count > 0)
			{
				IOrderedEnumerable<RelocateHeatExchangerView> source = (((resolvedData.ContainsKey(text2) ? resolvedData[text2] : null) as RelocateHeatExchangerViewCollection) ?? new RelocateHeatExchangerViewCollection()).OrderBy((RelocateHeatExchangerView item) => double.Parse(item.Payback));
				RelocateHeatExchangerView relocateHX = source.ElementAt(0);
				string scenarioKey = text;
				string text3 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_Scenario, projectKey, scenarioKey);
				Dictionary<string, object> resolvedData2 = GetResolvedData(text3);
				SummaryUtilityInformationView summaryUtilityInformationView = (((resolvedData2.ContainsKey(text3) ? resolvedData2[text3] : null) as SummaryUtilities) ?? new SummaryUtilities()).FirstOrDefault((SummaryUtilityInformationView item) => item.DesignIndex == relocateHX.DesignIndex);
				string key = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_RelocateHeatExchanger_Item, projectKey, text, null, relocateHX.DesignIndex.ToString());
				SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection source2 = ((resolvedData.ContainsKey(key) ? resolvedData[key] : null) as SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection) ?? new SurfaceAreanEntityViewCollection();
				string value = new Regex("–\\s*(\\w+)\\b").Match(summaryUtilityInformationView.Name ?? string.Empty).Groups[1].Value;
				string name = source2.FirstOrDefault((SurfaceAreanEntityView item) => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.HeatExchangerType)).Name;
				eAGenerateResult.DesignChange = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0} {1}", value, name);
				eAGenerateResult.EnergySaving = summaryUtilityInformationView.Reduction;
				eAGenerateResult.EnergyCost = (Convert.ToDouble(relocateHX.EnergySavingPerTime, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) / 1000000.0).ToString("N03");
				eAGenerateResult.CapitalCost = (Convert.ToDouble(relocateHX.TotalCapitalCost, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) / 1000000.0).ToString("N03");
				eAGenerateResult.NewArea = relocateHX.Area;
				eAGenerateResult.Payback = relocateHX.Payback;
				eAGenerateResult.NavigationPath = _relocateHXNodeKey;
			}
			ReportStateChanged(EAGenerateOperation.Relocate, state, eAGenerateResult);
		}

		private void DeleteCreatedScenarios()
		{
			if (_createdScenarioKeys == null || (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_createdScenarioKeys[0]) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(_createdScenarioKeys[1]) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(_createdScenarioKeys[2])))
			{
				NavigatorTreeNodeCollection navigatorTreeNodeCollection = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<NavigatorTreeNodeCollection>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
				if (navigatorTreeNodeCollection == null || navigatorTreeNodeCollection.Count == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				int num = 0;
				foreach (NavigatorTreeNode child in navigatorTreeNodeCollection[0].Children)
				{
					IEnergyAnalysisMessenger energyAnalysisMessenger = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisMessenger>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
					if (energyAnalysisMessenger != null && !energyAnalysisMessenger.IsGenerationDone && string.Compare(((EACTreeNode)child).SpecialFlag, "dashboard", ignoreCase: true) != 0 && new Regex("Scenario\\(\\d+\\)$").IsMatch(child.KeyReadOnly))
					{
						StartEnergyAnalysis.DBGenerateFirst = false;
					}
					if (string.Compare(((EACTreeNode)child).SpecialFlag, "dashboard", ignoreCase: true) == 0 && new Regex("Scenario\\(\\d+\\)$").IsMatch(child.KeyReadOnly))
					{
						_createdScenarioKeys[num++] = child.KeyReadOnly;
						if (num == 3)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			IList<string> list = new List<string>();
			for (int num2 = _createdScenarioKeys.Length - 1; num2 >= 0; num2--)
			{
				list.Add(_createdScenarioKeys[num2]);
			}
			StartEnergyAnalysis eac = _eac;
			eac.DashboardGenerate = true;
			foreach (string item in list)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item))
				{
					UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateTreeEvent>().Publish(new UpdateTreeEventArgs
					{
						Action = OperationEnum.Delete,
						State = new object[2] { "background", item }
					});
					string text = null;
					string value = new Regex("\\s*\\((\\d+)\\)").Match(item).Groups[1].Value;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
					{
						text = value + " Scenario";
						UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<WorkflowControlStateEvent>().Publish(new WorkflowControlStateArgs
						{
							ControlType = WorkflowControl.ClearCachedData,
							State = new object[3]
							{
								text,
								FormCategory.Scenario,
								-1
							}
						});
						UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeSelectionEvent>().Publish(new UITreeNodeSelectionArgs
						{
							NodeKey = item,
							State = new object[3]
							{
								true,
								OperationEnum.Delete,
								-1
							}
						});
						SystemUnit_Label.ScenarioDeleteEventWaitHandle.WaitOne();
						SystemUnit_Label.ScenarioDeleteEventWaitHandle.Reset();
					}
				}
			}
			eac?.SetBaseScenarioName();
			_createdScenarioKeys = new string[3];
		}

		private void OnModifyCallback(DataSourceChangedEventArgs arg)
		{
			if (UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisAgent>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) != null)
			{
				nGenerateStep++;
				if (_addAreaSubscriptToken != null)
				{
					_dataSourceChangeEvent.Unsubscribe(_addAreaSubscriptToken);
				}
				ReportStateChanged(EAGenerateOperation.Modify, arg.CanUpdate ? EAGenerateState.Succeeded : EAGenerateState.Failed);
				CollectNewAreaData(_nodeKey, arg.CanUpdate);
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<WorkflowControlStateEvent>().Publish(new WorkflowControlStateArgs
				{
					ControlType = WorkflowControl.NavigationTree,
					State = new object[2] { _nodeKey, "ScenarioNumber:1" }
				});
				ReportStateChanged(EAGenerateOperation.Add, EAGenerateState.Running);
				_addHXSubscriptToken = _dataSourceChangeEvent.Subscribe(OnAddCallback, ThreadOption.UIThread, keepSubscriberReferenceAlive: true, (DataSourceChangedEventArgs filter1) => filter1.SourceType == DataSourceTypeEnum.UpdateAddHeatExchanger);
				StartAddHeatExchangerProcess();
			}
		}

		private void OnAddCallback(DataSourceChangedEventArgs arg)
		{
			if (UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisAgent>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) != null)
			{
				nGenerateStep++;
				if (_addHXSubscriptToken != null)
				{
					_dataSourceChangeEvent.Unsubscribe(_addHXSubscriptToken);
				}
				ReportStateChanged(EAGenerateOperation.Add, arg.CanUpdate ? EAGenerateState.Succeeded : EAGenerateState.Failed);
				CollectNewHeatExchangerData(_nodeKey, arg.CanUpdate);
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<WorkflowControlStateEvent>().Publish(new WorkflowControlStateArgs
				{
					ControlType = WorkflowControl.NavigationTree,
					State = new object[2] { _nodeKey, "ScenarioNumber:1" }
				});
				ReportStateChanged(EAGenerateOperation.Relocate, EAGenerateState.Running);
				_relocateHXSubscriptToken = _dataSourceChangeEvent.Subscribe(OnRelocateCallback, ThreadOption.UIThread, keepSubscriberReferenceAlive: true, (DataSourceChangedEventArgs filter) => filter.SourceType == DataSourceTypeEnum.UpdateRelocateHeatExchanger);
				StartRelocateHeatExchangerProcess();
				if (_updateAddHXNodeKeyToken != null)
				{
					UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeEvent>().Unsubscribe(_updateAddHXNodeKeyToken);
					_updateAddHXNodeKeyToken = null;
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnRelocateCallback(DataSourceChangedEventArgs arg)
		{
			IEnergyAnalysisAgent energyAnalysisAgent = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisAgent>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			if (energyAnalysisAgent != null)
			{
				nGenerateStep++;
				if (_relocateHXSubscriptToken != null)
				{
					_dataSourceChangeEvent.Unsubscribe(_relocateHXSubscriptToken);
				}
				ReportStateChanged(EAGenerateOperation.Relocate, arg.CanUpdate ? EAGenerateState.Succeeded : EAGenerateState.Failed);
				CollectRelocatedHeatExchangerData(_nodeKey, arg.CanUpdate);
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<WorkflowControlStateEvent>().Publish(new WorkflowControlStateArgs
				{
					ControlType = WorkflowControl.NavigationTree,
					State = new object[2] { _nodeKey, "ScenarioNumber:1" }
				});
				if (_updateRelocateHXNodeKeyToken != null)
				{
					UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeEvent>().Unsubscribe(_updateRelocateHXNodeKeyToken);
					_updateRelocateHXNodeKeyToken = null;
				}
				MMViewModelContainer.bIsBackground = false;
				energyAnalysisAgent?.CancelGenerate();
			}
		}

		private void AddScenario(int retrofitType, int maxDesigns)
		{
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateTreeEvent>().Publish(new UpdateTreeEventArgs
			{
				Action = OperationEnum.Add,
				Category = FormCategory.Scenario,
				State = new object[2]
				{
					ThreadOption.BackgroundThread,
					"dashboard"
				}
			});
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeSelectionEvent>().Publish(new UITreeNodeSelectionArgs
			{
				NodeKey = _nodeKey,
				State = new object[4]
				{
					true,
					OperationEnum.Add,
					retrofitType,
					maxDesigns
				}
			});
		}

		private void NodeKeyWatcher(string waitFlag)
		{
			ManualResetEvent wait = new ManualResetEvent(initialState: false);
			try
			{
				System.Threading.Timer timer = new System.Threading.Timer(delegate
				{
					if (_nodeKey != null && _nodeKey.IndexOf(waitFlag) > 0)
					{
						wait.Set();
					}
				}, null, 0, 500);
				wait.WaitOne();
				timer.Dispose();
			}
			finally
			{
				if (wait != null)
				{
					((IDisposable)wait).Dispose();
				}
			}
		}

		private void StartAddAreaProcess()
		{
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisAgent>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).ResetGenerateStep();
			nGenerateStep = 0;
			bCanceled = false;
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateTreeEvent>().Publish(new UpdateTreeEventArgs
			{
				Action = OperationEnum.Add,
				Category = FormCategory.SavingPotentials,
				State = ThreadOption.BackgroundThread
			});
			_nodeKey = string.Format("{0}.{1}", "Project 1", StartEnergyAnalysis.GetDefaultNewScenarioName(0));
			AddScenario(0, 1);
			_nodeKey += ".Modify Heat Exchangers";
			_createdScenarioKeys[0] = _nodeKey;
			_addAreaProcessSubscriptionToken = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Subscribe(delegate
			{
				if (_addAreaProcessSubscriptionToken != null)
				{
					UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Unsubscribe(_addAreaProcessSubscriptionToken);
				}
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateTreeEvent>().Publish(new UpdateTreeEventArgs
				{
					Action = OperationEnum.Add,
					Category = FormCategory.ModifyHeatExchanger,
					State = new object[2]
					{
						ThreadOption.BackgroundThread,
						"dashboard"
					}
				});
				NodeKeyWatcher("Modify Heat Exchangers");
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeSelectionEvent>().Publish(new UITreeNodeSelectionArgs
				{
					NodeKey = _nodeKey,
					State = new object[1] { true }
				});
				_addAreaNodeKey = _nodeKey;
			}, ThreadOption.UIThread, keepSubscriberReferenceAlive: true, (DataSourceChangedEventArgs filter) => filter.SourceType == DataSourceTypeEnum.Summary);
		}

		private void StartAddHeatExchangerProcess()
		{
			if (bCanceled)
			{
				if (_addAreaSubscriptToken != null)
				{
					_dataSourceChangeEvent.Unsubscribe(_addAreaSubscriptToken);
				}
				if (_addHXSubscriptToken != null)
				{
					_dataSourceChangeEvent.Unsubscribe(_addHXSubscriptToken);
				}
				if (_relocateHXSubscriptToken != null)
				{
					_dataSourceChangeEvent.Unsubscribe(_relocateHXSubscriptToken);
				}
				return;
			}
			_nodeKey = string.Format("{0}.{1}", "Project 1", StartEnergyAnalysis.GetDefaultNewScenarioName(1));
			AddScenario(1, myNumberAddHXDesigns);
			_nodeKey += ".Add New";
			_createdScenarioKeys[1] = _nodeKey;
			_addHeatExchangerProcessSubscriptionToken = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Subscribe(delegate
			{
				if (_addHeatExchangerProcessSubscriptionToken != null)
				{
					UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Unsubscribe(_addHeatExchangerProcessSubscriptionToken);
				}
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateTreeEvent>().Publish(new UpdateTreeEventArgs
				{
					Action = OperationEnum.Add,
					Category = FormCategory.AddNewE400,
					State = new object[2]
					{
						ThreadOption.BackgroundThread,
						"dashboard"
					}
				});
				NodeKeyWatcher("Add New");
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeSelectionEvent>().Publish(new UITreeNodeSelectionArgs
				{
					NodeKey = _nodeKey,
					State = new object[1] { true }
				});
				_addHXNodeKey = _nodeKey;
				_updateAddHXNodeKeyToken = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeEvent>().Subscribe(delegate(UITreeNodeArgs treeNodeName)
				{
					_addHXNodeKey = _addHXNodeKey.TrimEnd() + " " + treeNodeName.NodeText;
					if (_updateAddHXNodeKeyToken != null)
					{
						UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeEvent>().Unsubscribe(_updateAddHXNodeKeyToken);
						_updateAddHXNodeKeyToken = null;
					}
				}, ThreadOption.PublisherThread, keepSubscriberReferenceAlive: false, (UITreeNodeArgs filter) => filter.State is object[] array && array.Length > 1 && array[0] is FormCategory && (FormCategory)array[0] == FormCategory.AddNewE400);
			}, ThreadOption.UIThread, keepSubscriberReferenceAlive: true, (DataSourceChangedEventArgs filter) => filter.SourceType == DataSourceTypeEnum.Summary);
		}

		private void StartRelocateHeatExchangerProcess()
		{
			if (bCanceled)
			{
				if (_addAreaSubscriptToken != null)
				{
					_dataSourceChangeEvent.Unsubscribe(_addAreaSubscriptToken);
				}
				if (_addHXSubscriptToken != null)
				{
					_dataSourceChangeEvent.Unsubscribe(_addHXSubscriptToken);
				}
				if (_relocateHXSubscriptToken != null)
				{
					_dataSourceChangeEvent.Unsubscribe(_relocateHXSubscriptToken);
				}
				return;
			}
			_nodeKey = string.Format("{0}.{1}", "Project 1", StartEnergyAnalysis.GetDefaultNewScenarioName(2));
			AddScenario(2, myNumberRelocateHXDesigns);
			_nodeKey += ".Relocate";
			_createdScenarioKeys[2] = _nodeKey;
			_relocateHeatExchangerProcessSubscriptionToken = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Subscribe(delegate
			{
				if (_relocateHeatExchangerProcessSubscriptionToken != null)
				{
					UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DataSourceChangedEvent>().Unsubscribe(_relocateHeatExchangerProcessSubscriptionToken);
				}
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateTreeEvent>().Publish(new UpdateTreeEventArgs
				{
					Action = OperationEnum.Add,
					Category = FormCategory.RelocateE108,
					State = new object[2]
					{
						ThreadOption.BackgroundThread,
						"dashboard"
					}
				});
				NodeKeyWatcher("Relocate");
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeSelectionEvent>().Publish(new UITreeNodeSelectionArgs
				{
					NodeKey = _nodeKey,
					State = new object[1] { true }
				});
				_relocateHXNodeKey = _nodeKey;
				_updateRelocateHXNodeKeyToken = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeEvent>().Subscribe(delegate(UITreeNodeArgs treeNodeName)
				{
					_relocateHXNodeKey = _relocateHXNodeKey.TrimEnd() + " " + treeNodeName.NodeText;
					if (_updateRelocateHXNodeKeyToken != null)
					{
						UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UITreeNodeEvent>().Unsubscribe(_updateRelocateHXNodeKeyToken);
						_updateRelocateHXNodeKeyToken = null;
					}
				}, ThreadOption.PublisherThread, keepSubscriberReferenceAlive: false, (UITreeNodeArgs filter) => filter.State is object[] array && array.Length > 1 && array[0] is FormCategory && (FormCategory)array[0] == FormCategory.RelocateE108);
			}, ThreadOption.UIThread, keepSubscriberReferenceAlive: true, (DataSourceChangedEventArgs filter) => filter.SourceType == DataSourceTypeEnum.Summary);
		}
	}
	public static class UtilitiesExtensions
	{
		public static double ToDouble(this string valueText, double defaultValue = 0.0, double defaultPropertyFactor = 1.0)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(valueText))
			{
				double result = 0.0;
				if (double.TryParse(valueText, out result))
				{
					return result * defaultPropertyFactor;
				}
			}
			return defaultValue;
		}

		public static double EASum(this IEnumerable<UtilityInformationView> uivs, Func<UtilityInformationView, string> valueProperty, double defaultPropertyValue = 0.0, double defaultPropertyFactor = 1.0)
		{
			if (valueProperty == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("valueProperty");
			}
			return uivs?.Sum((UtilityInformationView x) => valueProperty(x).ToDouble(defaultPropertyValue, defaultPropertyFactor)) ?? 0.0;
		}

		public static double EAToDouble(this UtilityInformationView uiv, Func<UtilityInformationView, string> valueProperty, double defaultValue = 0.0)
		{
			if (valueProperty == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("valueProperty");
			}
			if (uiv == null)
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			return valueProperty(uiv).ToDouble(defaultValue);
		}
	}
}
namespace Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.Integration.ActivationDashboard
{
	public class EnergyAnalysisAgentHelper
	{
		public const double MILLION_VALUE = 1000000.0;

		private readonly IUnityContainer _container;

		private readonly StartEnergyAnalysis _eac;

		private readonly IHostApplicationInfoMessenger _appInfo;

		private string simulatorName
		{
			get
			{
				string empty = string.Empty;
				if (_appInfo != null)
				{
					if (_appInfo.HostApplicationType != ApplicationType.AspenHYSYS)
					{
						return "aspenplus";
					}
					return "hysys";
				}
				return empty;
			}
			set
			{
			}
		}

		private bool isActivated
		{
			get
			{
				bool flag = StartEnergyAnalysis.IsActivated();
				if (!flag && _eac != null)
				{
					flag = _eac.IsEnginAlive;
				}
				return flag;
			}
			set
			{
			}
		}

		public EnergyAnalysisAgentHelper(IUnityContainer container)
		{
			if (container == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("container");
			}
			_container = container;
			_eac = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysis>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) as StartEnergyAnalysis;
			_appInfo = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IHostApplicationInfoMessenger>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
		}

		public bool TrySaveEnergyAnalyzerResult()
		{
			try
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_eac.CaseFilePath))
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (!CheckPermission(_eac.CaseFilePath))
				{
					string text = _eac.CaseFilePath.Substring(_eac.CaseFilePath.LastIndexOf('\\') + 1);
					string hostProductName = _appInfo.HostProductName;
					if (System.Windows.MessageBox.Show($"You are not allowed to save Aspen Energy Analyzer file {text} in {_eac.CaseFilePath.Substring(0, _eac.CaseFilePath.LastIndexOf('\\') + 1)}. If you choose Yes, the file will not be saved and data will be lost. Save the {hostProductName} case file in different folder.{Environment.NewLine}Do you want to continue?", hostProductName, MessageBoxButton.YesNo, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation) != MessageBoxResult.Yes)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				_eac.SaveHch();
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).Save(_container);
				_eac.AfterDocumentSave();
				CopyIfCompFile();
				return true;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		public void InitializeEASettings(bool launchEA = true)
		{
			string currentDocumentPath = _appInfo.CurrentDocumentPath;
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DocumentOpenedEvent>().Publish(new DocumentOpenedArgs
			{
				OpenedDocumentPath = currentDocumentPath,
				UpdateTree = false,
				State = _container,
				SimulatorName = simulatorName,
				DoNotLaunchEA = !launchEA
			});
		}

		public ActivationResult RetrieveActivationResult()
		{
			try

				IEnumerable<UtilityInformationView> enumerable = null;

				UtilityInformationView utilityInformationView2 = null;
				IEnumerable<UtilityInformationView> enumerable3 = null;
				double num = -1.0;
				string projectKeyFromNodeKey = GetProjectKeyFromNodeKey(null);
				string moniker = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_UtilityView, projectKeyFromNodeKey);
				UtilityView utilityView = null;

				}
				if (UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()) == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				if (utilityView == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				ActivationResult activationResult = new ActivationResult();
				utilityInformationView = utilityView.UtilityItems.FirstOrDefault((UtilityInformationView item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
				utilityInformationView2 = utilityView.UtilityItems.LastOrDefault((UtilityInformationView item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.NONE);
				enumerable3 = utilityView.UtilityItems.Except(new UtilityInformationView[2] { utilityInformationView2, utilityInformationView });
				num = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).CarbonFee;
				enumerable = utilityView.UtilityItems.Where((UtilityInformationView item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.HEATING);
				enumerable2 = utilityView.UtilityItems.Where((UtilityInformationView item) => item.UtilityType == UtilityType.COOLING);
				int num2 = enumerable.Count();
				int num3 = enumerable2.Count();
				double operationHoursPerYear = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).OperationHoursPerYear;
				activationResult.ShowWarning = false;
				IEnumerable<SurfaceAreanBaseView> enumerable4 = utilityView.UtilityItems.SelectMany((UtilityInformationView item) => item.HeatExchangers);
				if (enumerable4 != null && enumerable3 != null)

						energyAnalysisAgent.IsUtilitiesWarning = flag2;

					activationResult.ShowWarning = flag || flag2;

				double num4 = 3600.0 * operationHoursPerYear / 1000000.0;
				double num5 = ((num3 > 0) ? enumerable2.EASum((UtilityInformationView x) => x.ActualCost, -1.0) : 0.0);
				double num6 = ((num3 > 0) ? enumerable2.EASum((UtilityInformationView x) => x.TargetCost, -1.0) : 0.0);
				double num7 = num5 * num4;
				double num8 = num6 * num4;
				double num9 = ((num2 > 0) ? enumerable.EASum((UtilityInformationView x) => x.ActualCost, -1.0) : 0.0);
				double num10 = ((num2 > 0) ? enumerable.EASum((UtilityInformationView x) => x.TargetCost, -1.0) : 0.0);
				double num11 = num9 * num4;
				double num12 = num10 * num4;
				double num13 = (utilityInformationView2?.EAToDouble((UtilityInformationView x) => x.GHGCurrent) ?? 0.0) + (utilityInformationView?.EAToDouble((UtilityInformationView x) => x.GHGCurrent) ?? 0.0);
				double num14 = (utilityInformationView2?.EAToDouble((UtilityInformationView x) => x.GHGTarget) ?? 0.0) + (utilityInformationView?.EAToDouble((UtilityInformationView x) => x.GHGTarget) ?? 0.0);
				double num15 = ((num2 > 0) ? enumerable.EASum((UtilityInformationView x) => x.GHGCurrentDB, -1.0, num4 * num) : 0.0) + ((num3 > 0) ? enumerable2.EASum((UtilityInformationView x) => x.GHGCurrentDB, -1.0, num4 * num) : 0.0);
				double num16 = ((num2 > 0) ? enumerable.EASum((UtilityInformationView x) => x.GHGTargetDB, -1.0, num4 * num) : 0.0) + ((num3 > 0) ? enumerable2.EASum((UtilityInformationView x) => x.GHGTargetDB, -1.0, num4 * num) : 0.0);
				activationResult.CoolingUnits = SystemUnit_Label.Instance.EnthalpyUserUnit;

				activationResult.CoolingTooltipUnits = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0} Millions /Yr", SystemUnit_Label.Instance.FiscalUserUnit);
				activationResult.HeatingTooltipUnits = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0} Millions /Yr", SystemUnit_Label.Instance.FiscalUserUnit);
				activationResult.GHGUnits = SystemUnit_Label.Instance.MassflowUserUnit;
