				activationResult.UtilityCostUnits = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0} Millions /Yr", SystemUnit_Label.Instance.FiscalUserUnit);
				activationResult.CapitalCostUnit = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0} Millions", SystemUnit_Label.Instance.FiscalUserUnit);
				activationResult.AreaUnit = SystemUnit_Label.Instance.AreaUserUnit;
				activationResult.GHGCostUnits = string.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "{0} Millions /Yr", SystemUnit_Label.Instance.FiscalUserUnit);
				activationResult.CoolingCurrentValue = ((utilityInformationView2 != null && num3 > 0) ? utilityInformationView2.Current : "0.0");
				activationResult.CoolingTotalValue = ((utilityInformationView2 != null && num3 > 0) ? utilityInformationView2.Target : "0.0");
				activationResult.CoolingTotalCost = num8.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.CoolingCurrentCost = num7.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.CoolingSavingPotential = (num7 - num8).ToString("G4");
				activationResult.HeatingCurrentValue = ((utilityInformationView != null && num2 > 0) ? utilityInformationView.Current : "0.0");
				activationResult.HeatingTotalValue = ((utilityInformationView != null && num2 > 0) ? utilityInformationView.Target : "0.0");
				activationResult.HeatingTotalCost = num12.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.HeatingCurrentCost = num11.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.HeatingSavingPotential = (num11 - num12).ToString("G4");
				activationResult.GHGCurrentValue = num13.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.GHGTotalValue = num14.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.GHGTotalCost = num16.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.GHGCurrentCost = num15.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.GHGSavingPotential = (num15 - num16).ToString("G4");
				double num17 = num4 * (num9 + num5);
				double num18 = num4 * (num10 + num6);
				activationResult.UtilityCostCurrentValue = num17.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.UtilityCostTotalValue = num18.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.UtilityPercent = ((num17 - num18) / Math.Abs((num17 != 0.0) ? num17 : 1.0)).ToString("P2");
				activationResult.GHGCostCurrentValue = num15.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.GHGCostTotalValue = num16.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.GHGPercent = ((num15 - num16) / Math.Abs((num15 != 0.0) ? num15 : 1.0)).ToString("P2");
				double num19 = ((utilityInformationView2 != null && num3 > 0) ? utilityInformationView2.dblCurrent : 0.0) + ((utilityInformationView != null && num2 > 0) ? utilityInformationView.dblCurrent : 0.0);
				double num20 = ((utilityInformationView2 != null && num3 > 0) ? utilityInformationView2.dblTarget : 0.0) + ((utilityInformationView != null && num2 > 0) ? utilityInformationView.dblTarget : 0.0);
				activationResult.TotalUnits = SystemUnit_Label.Instance.EnthalpyUserUnit;
				double num21 = Convert.ToDouble(activationResult.CoolingCurrentValue, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + Convert.ToDouble(activationResult.HeatingCurrentValue, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
				double num22 = Convert.ToDouble(activationResult.HeatingTotalValue, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) + Convert.ToDouble(activationResult.CoolingTotalValue, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
				activationResult.TotalCurrentValue = num21.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.TotalTargetValue = num22.ToString("G4");
				activationResult.TotalFlowSavingPercent = ((num19 - num20) / ((num19 != 0.0) ? num19 : 1.0)).ToString("P2");
				activationResult.TotalFlowSavingPercentDB = ((num19 - num20) / ((num19 != 0.0) ? num19 : 1.0) * 100.0).ToString("N2");
				double num23 = Convert.ToDouble(activationResult.HeatingCurrentValue, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
				double num24 = Convert.ToDouble(activationResult.HeatingTotalValue, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
				activationResult.HeatingFlowSavingPercent = ((utilityInformationView != null) ? ((utilityInformationView.dblCurrent - utilityInformationView.dblTarget) / ((utilityInformationView.dblCurrent != 0.0) ? utilityInformationView.dblCurrent : 1.0)).ToString("P2") : "0.0");
				double num25 = Convert.ToDouble(activationResult.CoolingCurrentValue, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
				double num26 = Convert.ToDouble(activationResult.CoolingTotalValue, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
				activationResult.CoolingFlowSavingPercent = ((utilityInformationView2 != null) ? ((utilityInformationView2.dblCurrent - utilityInformationView2.dblTarget) / ((utilityInformationView2.dblCurrent != 0.0) ? utilityInformationView2.dblCurrent : 1.0)).ToString("P2") : "0.0");
				double num27 = num13;
				double num28 = num14;
				activationResult.GHGFlowSavingPercent = ((num27 - num28) / ((num27 != 0.0) ? num27 : 1.0)).ToString("P2");
				double num29 = Convert.ToDouble(activationResult.HeatingCurrentCost, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
				double num30 = Convert.ToDouble(activationResult.HeatingTotalCost, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);

				double num32 = Convert.ToDouble(activationResult.CoolingTotalCost, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture);
				activationResult.CoolingCostSavingPercent = ((num31 - num32) / ((num31 != 0.0) ? Math.Abs(num31) : 1.0)).ToString("P2");
				activationResult.TotalFlowDifference = (num21 - num22).ToString("G4");
				activationResult.HeatingFlowDifference = (num23 - num24).ToString("G4");
				activationResult.CoolingFlowDifference = (num25 - num26).ToString("G4");
				activationResult.GHGFlowDifference = (num27 - num28).ToString("G4");

		public void NavigateToViewUtilities()
		{
			SwitchToEnergyAnalysisEnvironment();
			if (EACFormFactory.ProjectCount == 0)
			{
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateTreeEvent>().Publish(new UpdateTreeEventArgs
				{
					Action = OperationEnum.Add,
					Category = FormCategory.SavingPotentials,
					State = null
				});
			}
			SynchronizationContext.Current.Post(delegate
			{
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateTreeEvent>().Publish(new UpdateTreeEventArgs
				{
					Action = OperationEnum.Location,
					State = "Project 1.Saving Potentials"
				});
			}, null);
		}

		public IEditableObject GenerateEditOptionsContent()
		{
			return new Project_Setup_SubflowsheetSelection(_container);
		}

		public bool CanDoGeneration()
		{
			EnsureDocumentOpenedExtecuted();
			return _eac.AmIRetrofitReady();
		}

		public void EnsureDocumentOpenedExtecuted()
		{
			IEnergyAnalysisAgent energyAnalysisAgent = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisAgent>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			if (!_eac.IsEnginAlive)
			{
				bool flag = energyAnalysisAgent.IsAutoRun();
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<DocumentOpenedEvent>().Publish(new DocumentOpenedArgs
				{
					IsRestoringLayout = false,
					OpenedDocumentPath = _appInfo.CurrentDocumentPath,
					SimulatorName = simulatorName,
					State = _container,
					UpdateTree = false,
					DoNotLaunchEA = !flag
				});
			}
		}

		public void NavigateToGenerateResult(string path)
		{
			SwitchToEnergyAnalysisEnvironment();
			System.Windows.Application.Current.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateTreeEvent>().Publish(new UpdateTreeEventArgs
				{
					Action = OperationEnum.Location,
					State = path
				});
			});
		}

		private string GetProjectKeyFromNodeKey(string treeNode)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(treeNode))
			{
				return treeNode.Split('.')[0];
			}
			return "Project 1";
		}

		public void Activate(bool analyze = true)
		{
			IEnergyAnalysisAgent energyAnalysisAgent = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEnergyAnalysisAgent>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			try
			{
				bool flag = isActivated;
				InitializeEASettings();
				string projectKeyFromNodeKey = GetProjectKeyFromNodeKey(null);
				PrepareEnergyAnalysisConstraints(projectKeyFromNodeKey, flag, out var deltTemp, out var selectedFlowsheets, out var msgCollection);
				StartEnergyAnalysis eac = _eac;
				eac.BackDoorMessage.Clear();
				eac.GlobalDeltaMinTemperature = deltTemp;
				foreach (BackDoorMessage item in msgCollection)
				{
					eac.BackDoorMessage.Add(item);
				}
				HYSYSParameters hYSYSParameters = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
				if (hYSYSParameters == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				bool isEnginAlive = eac.IsEnginAlive;
				if (!isEnginAlive)
				{
					eac.LaunchEnergyAnalyzer(hYSYSParameters.SimulatorName);
					eac.HasTopologyChanged = true;
				}
				else
				{
					eac.HasTopologyChanged = hYSYSParameters.topologyChanged;
				}
				bool flag2 = false;
				if (eac.IsEnginAlive)
				{
					flag2 = energyAnalysisAgent.IsExchangerWarning || energyAnalysisAgent.IsUtilitiesWarning;
				}
				if (!eac.ActivateEACase(hYSYSParameters.HYSYSFile, out var hchExists))
				{
					energyAnalysisAgent.NeedSaveClose = false;
					energyAnalysisAgent.EndLoadingAnalysis();
					energyAnalysisAgent.ResetDashboard();
					return;
				}
				if (hchExists || flag2)
				{
					eac.HasTopologyChanged = true;
				}
				energyAnalysisAgent?.EndLoadingAnalysis();
				UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>())?.Write(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Constraint_GlobalDTmin, (projectKeyFromNodeKey != null) ? projectKeyFromNodeKey : "Project 1"), deltTemp.ToString("G4"));
				if (analyze || !hchExists)
				{
					eac.RunAnalysis(hYSYSParameters.SimulatorName, hYSYSParameters.SimCase, hYSYSParameters.HYSYSFile, hchExists, (selectedFlowsheets != null && selectedFlowsheets.Count > 0) ? selectedFlowsheets : hYSYSParameters.FlowsheetsName, hYSYSParameters.UtilityNames, hYSYSParameters.UtilityFactors, hYSYSParameters.UtilityDeltaMinTemprature, projectKeyFromNodeKey);
					SetDocumentDirty();
				}
				string currentDocumentPath = _appInfo.CurrentDocumentPath;
				ResetTopologyFlagInSimWrapper(currentDocumentPath);
				UtilityView utilityView = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IDataResolver>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).ResolveData<UtilityView>(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.EnginData_UtilityView, projectKeyFromNodeKey));
				if (utilityView != null)
				{
					utilityView.GlobalMinDeltaTemperature = deltTemp;
				}
				if (!isEnginAlive)
				{
					UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IEventAggregator>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).GetEvent<UpdateTreeEvent>().Publish(new UpdateTreeEventArgs
					{
						Action = OperationEnum.Add,
						Category = FormCategory.SavingPotentials,
						State = ThreadOption.BackgroundThread
					});
				}
				EACFormFactory.EACTreeNodeRibbonInfo eACRibbonInfo = EACFormFactory.GetEACRibbonInfo(projectKeyFromNodeKey);
				if (eACRibbonInfo != null)
				{
					eACRibbonInfo.Status = EACFormFactory.EACTreeNodeRibbonStatus.Analyzed;
				}
			}
			catch (ResolutionFailedException)
			{
				energyAnalysisAgent?.EndLoadingAnalysis();
			}
			catch (Exception ex2)
			{
				energyAnalysisAgent?.EndLoadingAnalysis();
				_ = ex2.Message;
			}
		}

		private void PrepareEnergyAnalysisConstraints(string projectKey, bool isActivated, out double deltTemp, out IList<string> selectedFlowsheets, out Collection<BackDoorMessage> msgCollection)
		{
			msgCollection = new Collection<BackDoorMessage>();
			Func<string, bool, object> func = delegate(string path, bool isResultXmlSection)
			{
				try
				{
					Dictionary<string, string> dictionary2 = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<Aspentech.EnergyAnalysis.UserInterface.CaseDocument>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>())?.GetValue(path, isResultXmlSection);
					if (isResultXmlSection)
					{
						return (dynamic)(dictionary2 ?? new Dictionary<string, string>());
					}
					return (dynamic)((dictionary2 == null || dictionary2.Count == 0) ? null : dictionary2.First().Value);
				}
				catch (ResolutionFailedException)
				{
					return (dynamic)null;
				}
			};
			msgCollection.Add(new BackDoorMessage
			{
				MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_PRBPIgnoreOrTreat,
				State = ((string.Compare((dynamic)func(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.DataExtraction_ExtractRule_PRBPIgnore, projectKey), arg2: false), "0") == 0) ? "0" : "1")
			});
			double num = 0.9;
			if (((!double.TryParse((dynamic)func(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.DataExtraction_ExtractRule_SegTolerances, projectKey), arg2: false), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, out num))) && ((!double.TryParse((dynamic)func(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.DataExtraction_ExtractRule_SegTolerances, projectKey), arg2: false), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out num)) ? true : false))
			{
				num = 0.9;
			}
			if (num <= 0.0 || num >= 1.0)
			{
				num = 0.9;
			}
			msgCollection.Add(new BackDoorMessage
			{
				MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_SegTolerances,
				State = num
			});
			double num2 = 0.0;
			if (((!double.TryParse((dynamic)func(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.DataExtraction_ExtractRule_ReactionHeat, projectKey), arg2: false), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, out num2))) && ((!double.TryParse((dynamic)func(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.DataExtraction_ExtractRule_ReactionHeat, projectKey), arg2: false), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out num2)) ? true : false))
			{
				num2 = 0.0;
			}
			if (num2 != 0.0 && num2 != 1.0)
			{
				num2 = 0.0;
			}
			msgCollection.Add(new BackDoorMessage
			{
				MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_ReactionHeat,
				State = num2
			});
			bool flag = string.Compare((dynamic)func(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.DataExtraction_ExtractRule_ExtractLive, projectKey), arg2: false), "1") == 0;
			msgCollection.Add(new BackDoorMessage
			{
				MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_ExtractLiveSteam,
				State = (flag ? "1" : "0")
			});
			if (flag)
			{
				if ((bool)(string.Compare((dynamic)func(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.DataExtraction_ExtractRule_IgnoreStreamSuper, projectKey), arg2: false), "1") == 0))
				{
					msgCollection.Add(new BackDoorMessage
					{
						MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_IgnoreSteamSuper,
						State = "1"
					});
				}
				if ((bool)(string.Compare((dynamic)func(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.DataExtraction_ExtractRule_IgnoreStreamSub, projectKey), arg2: false), "1") == 0))
				{
					msgCollection.Add(new BackDoorMessage
					{
						MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_IgnoreSteamSubCooling,
						State = "1"
					});
				}
				msgCollection.Add(new BackDoorMessage
				{
					MessageType = BackDoorMessageTypeEnum.DataExtractionRules_BoilerFeedWaterTemperature,
					State = double.Parse((dynamic)(func(DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.DataExtraction_ExtractRule_BoilerWater, projectKey), arg2: false) ?? "20"))
				});
			}
			string value = GlobalProjectDeltaMinTemperature.GetValue((projectKey != null) ? projectKey : "Project 1");
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				if (!double.TryParse(value, out deltTemp))
				{
					deltTemp = 10.0;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				string arg = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Constraint_GlobalDTmin, projectKey);
				deltTemp = double.Parse((dynamic)(func(arg, arg2: false) ?? "10"));
			}
			string arg2 = DataMoniker.BuildMoniker(DataMonikerType.Constraint_Flowsheets, projectKey);
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = (dynamic)func(arg2, arg2: true);
			_ = _eac;
			selectedFlowsheets = FlowsheetDataSource.SelectedItems;
			if (isActivated)
			{
				selectedFlowsheets = FlowsheetDataSource.SelectedItems;
				return;
			}
			if (FlowsheetDataSource.SelectedItems != null && FlowsheetDataSource.SelectedItems.Count > 0)
			{
				bool flag2 = true;
				IList<string> list = new List<string>();
				if (!(UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>()).FlowsheetsName is IList<string> list2))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (FlowsheetDataSource.AllFSItems.Count == list2.Count)
				{
					foreach (string item in list2)
					{
						if (!FlowsheetDataSource.AllFSItems.Contains(item))
						{
							flag2 = false;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					selectedFlowsheets = null;
				}
				if (!flag2)
				{
					selectedFlowsheets = null;
				}
			}
			if ((selectedFlowsheets == null || selectedFlowsheets.Count == 0) && dictionary != null && dictionary.Count > 0)
			{
				selectedFlowsheets = ParseFlowSheetsFromXML(dictionary);
			}
		}

		private IList<string> ParseFlowSheetsFromXML(Dictionary<string, string> tmpList)
		{
			IList<string> list = new List<string>();
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";
			using (Dictionary<string, string>.Enumerator enumerator = tmpList.GetEnumerator())
			{
				if (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					text = enumerator.Current.Value;
					text2 = text;
					text3 = text;
					string text4 = "<Flowsheet>";
					string text5 = "</Flowsheet>";
					if (text.Contains(text4))
					{
						text2 = text.Remove(0, text4.Length);
					}
					if (text2.Contains(text5))
					{
						text3 = text2.Remove(text2.Length - text5.Length, text5.Length);
					}
				}
			}
			string[] array = text3.Split('|');
			foreach (string text6 in array)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6))
				{
					string[] array2 = text6.Split(',');
					if (array2.Count() > 1 && !dictionary.ContainsKey(array2[0]))
					{
						dictionary.Add(array2[0], array2[1]);
					}
				}
			}
			HYSYSParameters hYSYSParameters = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<HYSYSParameters>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			IList<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			if (!(hYSYSParameters.FlowsheetsName is IList<string> list3))
			{
				return list;
			}
			foreach (string item in list3)
			{
				if (dictionary.ContainsKey(item) && dictionary[item] == "1")
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		private void ResetTopologyFlagInSimWrapper(string filePath)
		{
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IApplicationWrapper>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>())?.ResetTopologyFlaginSimulator(filePath, topologyFlag: false);
		}

		private void SetDocumentDirty()
		{
			UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IApplicationWrapper>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>())?.SetSimulatorCaseDirty();
		}

		private void SwitchToEnergyAnalysisEnvironment()
		{
			IShellApplication shellApplication = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<IShellApplication>(_container, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			if (shellApplication != null)
			{
				IShellMainWindow activeWindow = shellApplication.ActiveWindow;
				NavigatorView navigatorView = ((CommonShellWindow)((activeWindow is CommonShellWindow) ? activeWindow : null)).ContentHost.NavigatorBar.NavigatorViews.FirstOrDefault((NavigatorView x) => x is UINavigtorView);
				if (navigatorView != null)
				{
					navigatorView.IsSelected = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void CopyIfCompFile()
		{
			string text = _eac.CurrentWorkingFolder(_eac.CaseFilePath);
			string directoryName = Path.GetDirectoryName(_eac.CaseFilePath);
			string text2 = directoryName.Substring(directoryName.LastIndexOf('\\') + 1);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && text2.Length == 8)
			{
				text2 = directoryName.Substring(0, directoryName.Length - 8);
				if (File.Exists(text2 + Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(_eac.CaseFilePath) + ".hscz"))
				{
					string text3 = text + "\\" + Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(_eac.CaseFilePath);
					File.Copy(text3 + ".xml", text2 + Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(_eac.CaseFilePath) + ".xml");
					File.Copy(text3 + ".hch", text2 + Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(_eac.CaseFilePath) + ".hch");
				}
			}
		}

		private bool CheckPermission(string filePath)
		{
			try
			{
				Directory.GetAccessControl(filePath.Substring(0, filePath.LastIndexOf('\\')));
				return true;
			}
			catch (UnauthorizedAccessException ex)
			{
				_ = ex.Message;
				return false;
			}
		}
	}
